  31: 
  32: bool UGBPixelVfxComponent::Play(FName ClipId, bool bRestart)
  33: {
  34: 	if (!bRestart && bPlaying && CurrentClipId == ClipId && CurrentClip.IsValid())
  35: 	{
  36: 		return true;
  37: 	}
  38: 	UGameInstance* GameInstance = nullptr;
  39: 	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
  40: 	{
  41: 		GameInstance = World->GetGameInstance();
  42: 	}
  43: 	UGBPixelVfxSubsystem* Subsystem = GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelVfxSubsystem>() : nullptr;
  44: 	if (!Subsystem)
  45: 	{
  46: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx Play '%s': no subsystem."), *ClipId.ToString());
  47: 		return false;
  48: 	}
  49: 	const FGBPixelVfxClip* Found = Subsystem->FindClip(ClipId);
  50: 	if (!Found)
  51: 	{
  52: 		return false;
  53: 	}
  54: 	UTexture2D* Atlas = Subsystem->GetAtlas(ClipId);
  55: 	if (!Atlas)
  56: 	{
  57: 		return false;
  58: 	}
  59: 	CurrentClip = MakeShared<FGBPixelVfxClip>(*Found);
  60: 	CurrentClipId = ClipId;
  61: 	if (!bLightCastsShadowsSet)
  62: 	{
  63: 		bLightCastsShadows = CurrentClip->Light.bCastShadows;
  64: 	}
  65: 	PlayTime = StartTimeOffset;
  66: 	CurrentFrame = -1;
  67: 	bPlaying = true;
  68: 	EnsureBuilt();
  69: 	UpdateFrame(true);
  70: 	return true;
  71: }
  72: 
  73: void UGBPixelVfxComponent::Stop()
  74: {
  75: 	bPlaying = false;
  76: 	if (ClipLight)
  77: 	{
  78: 		ClipLight->DestroyComponent();
  79: 		ClipLight = nullptr;
  80: 	}
  81: 	if (Quad)
  82: 	{
  83: 		Quad->DestroyComponent();
  84: 		Quad = nullptr;
  85: 	}
  86: 	QuadMaterial = nullptr;
  87: }
  88: 
  89: void UGBPixelVfxComponent::EnsureBuilt()
  90: {
  91: 	if (!CurrentClip.IsValid())
  92: 	{
  93: 		return;
  94: 	}
  95: 	if (!Quad)
  96: 	{
  97: 		UStaticMesh* PlaneMesh = Cast<UStaticMesh>(StaticLoadObject(UStaticMesh::StaticClass(), nullptr,
  98: 			GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::MeshPath));
  99: 		if (!PlaneMesh)
 100: 		{
 101: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx: cannot load the quad mesh %s."),
 102: 				GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::MeshPath);
 103: 			return;
 104: 		}
 105: 		Quad = NewObject<UStaticMeshComponent>(GetOwner() ? static_cast<UObject*>(GetOwner()) : static_cast<UObject*>(this), NAME_None, RF_Transient);
 106: 		Quad->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
 107: 		Quad->AttachToComponent(this, FAttachmentTransformRules::KeepRelativeTransform);
 108: 		Quad->SetStaticMesh(PlaneMesh);
 109: 		Quad->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
 110: 		Quad->SetCastShadow(false);
 111: 		Quad->SetVisibleInRayTracing(true);
 112: 		Quad->SetAffectDynamicIndirectLighting(true);
 113: 		Quad->bVisibleInReflectionCaptures = true;
 114: 		Quad->RegisterComponent();
 115: 
 116: 		const TCHAR* MaterialPath = CurrentClip->bLit
 117: 			? GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::LitMaterialPath
 118: 			: GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::SpriteMaterialPath;
 119: 		UMaterialInterface* Base = Cast<UMaterialInterface>(StaticLoadObject(UMaterialInterface::StaticClass(),
 120: 			nullptr, MaterialPath));
 121: 		if (!Base && !bQuadMissingMaterialWarned)
 122: 		{
 123: 			bQuadMissingMaterialWarned = true;
 124: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx: material %s is missing, falling back to %s."),
 125: 				MaterialPath, GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::FallbackMaterialPath);
 126: 			Base = Cast<UMaterialInterface>(StaticLoadObject(UMaterialInterface::StaticClass(),
 127: 				nullptr, GBPixelVfxComponentLocal::FallbackMaterialPath));
 128: 		}
 129: 		QuadMaterial = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(Base, this);
 130: 		if (QuadMaterial)
 131: 		{
 132: 			UGBPixelVfxSubsystem* Subsystem = nullptr;
 133: 			if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
 134: 			{
 135: 				if (UGameInstance* GameInstance = World->GetGameInstance())
 136: 				{
 137: 					Subsystem = GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelVfxSubsystem>();
 138: 				}
 139: 			}
 140: 			UTexture2D* Atlas = Subsystem ? Subsystem->GetAtlas(CurrentClipId) : nullptr;
 141: 			if (Atlas)
 142: 			{
 143: 				QuadMaterial->SetTextureParameterValue(TEXT("Atlas"), Atlas);
 144: 			}
 145: 			QuadMaterial->SetVectorParameterValue(TEXT("Tint"), FLinearColor::White);
 146: 			QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("Glow"), CurrentClip->Glow);
 147: 		}
 148: 		if (QuadMaterial)
 149: 		{
 150: 			Quad->SetMaterial(0, QuadMaterial);
 151: 		}
 152: 	}
 153: 	RebuildLight();
 154: }
 155: 
 156: void UGBPixelVfxComponent::RebuildLight()
 157: {
 158: 	const bool bWantsLight = CurrentClip.IsValid() && CurrentClip->Light.bEnabled && bEnableLight;
 159: 	if (!bWantsLight)
 160: 	{
 161: 		if (ClipLight)
 162: 		{
 163: 			ClipLight->DestroyComponent();
 164: 			ClipLight = nullptr;
 165: 		}
 166: 		return;
 167: 	}
 168: 	if (!ClipLight)
 169: 	{
 170: 		ClipLight = NewObject<UPointLightComponent>(GetOwner() ? static_cast<UObject*>(GetOwner()) : static_cast<UObject*>(this), NAME_None, RF_Transient);
 171: 		ClipLight->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
 172: 		ClipLight->SetIntensityUnits(ELightUnits::Candelas);
 173: 		ClipLight->AttachToComponent(this, FAttachmentTransformRules::KeepRelativeTransform);
 174: 		ClipLight->RegisterComponent();
 175: 	}
 176: 	ClipLight->SetLightColor(CurrentClip->Light.Color);
 177: 	ClipLight->SetAttenuationRadius(CurrentClip->Light.RadiusCm * FMath::Max(0.01f, SizeScale));
 178: 	ClipLight->SetCastShadows(bLightCastsShadows);
 179: }
 180: 
 181: void UGBPixelVfxComponent::UpdateFrame(bool bForce)
 182: {
 183: 	if (!bPlaying || !CurrentClip.IsValid())
 184: 	{
 185: 		return;
 186: 	}
 187: 	bool bOutFinished = false;
 188: 	const int32 Frame = FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(*CurrentClip, PlayTime, bOutFinished);
 189: 	if (bOutFinished)
 190: 	{
 191: 		Stop();
 192: 		if (bAutoDestroyOwnerWhenFinished && GetOwner())
 193: 		{
 194: 			GetOwner()->Destroy();
 195: 		}
 196: 		return;
 197: 	}
 198: 	if (Frame == CurrentFrame && !bForce)
 199: 	{
 200: 		return;
 201: 	}
 202: 	CurrentFrame = Frame;
 203: 	const FVector4f Rect = FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameRect(*CurrentClip, Frame);
 204: 	if (QuadMaterial)
 205: 	{
 206: 		// The frame rect rides as four scalars (see make_pixelvfx_material.py): masks on a
 207: 		// uniform that feeds the texture UVs fail to compile, so there is no vector swizzle.
 208: 		// The Plane mesh is assumed to run U along +X and V along +Y with V = 0 at the bottom;
 209: 		// UE samples (0, 0) at the image top-left, so V is flipped here.
 210: 		QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameU"), Rect.X);
 211: 		QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameV"), Rect.Y + Rect.W);
 212: 		QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameW"), Rect.Z);
 213: 		QuadMaterial->SetScalarParameterValue(TEXT("FrameH"), -Rect.W);
 214: 	}
 215: 	if (ClipLight && CurrentClip->Light.bEnabled)
 216: 	{
 217: 		float TrackValue = 1.f;
 218: 		if (CurrentClip->Light.TrackMode.Equals(TEXT("Constant"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)
 219: 			|| CurrentClip->Light.Track.Num() != CurrentClip->FrameCount)
 220: 		{
 221: 			TrackValue = 1.f;
 222: 		}
 223: 		else if (CurrentClip->Light.Track.IsValidIndex(Frame))
 224: 		{
 225: 			TrackValue = CurrentClip->Light.Track[Frame];
 226: 		}
 227: 		ClipLight->SetIntensity(CurrentClip->Light.Intensity * TrackValue * LightIntensityScale);
 228: 	}
 229: }
 230: 
 231: FVector UGBPixelVfxComponent::PivotWorldOffset() const
 232: {
 233: 	// Quad-local position of the pivot point; the quad is shifted so it lands on the component.
 234: 	if (!CurrentClip.IsValid())
 235: 	{
 236: 		return FVector::ZeroVector;
 237: 	}
 238: 	const float W = CurrentClip->WorldWidthCm * SizeScale;
 239: 	const float H = CurrentClip->WorldHeightCm * SizeScale;
 240: 	return FVector((CurrentClip->PivotU - 0.5f) * W, (0.5f - CurrentClip->PivotV) * H, 0.f);
 241: }
 242: 
 243: void UGBPixelVfxComponent::UpdateQuadTransform()
 244: {
 245: 	if (!Quad || !CurrentClip.IsValid())
 246: 	{
 247: 		return;
 248: 	}
 249: 	const float W = CurrentClip->WorldWidthCm * SizeScale;
 250: 	const float H = CurrentClip->WorldHeightCm * SizeScale;
 251: 	Quad->SetRelativeScale3D(FVector(FMath::Max(0.01f, W / 100.f), FMath::Max(0.01f, H / 100.f), 1.f));
 252: 	Quad->SetRelativeLocation(-PivotWorldOffset());
 253: 
 254: 	EGBPixelVfxFacing Facing = CurrentClip->Facing;
 255: 	if (!FacingOverride.IsNone())
 256: 	{
 257: 		const FString Text = FacingOverride.ToString();
 258: 		if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Billboard"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Billboard;
 259: 		else if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Upright"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright;
 260: 		else if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Ground"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground;
 261: 		else if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Fixed"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed;
 262: 	}
 263: 
 264: 	if (Facing == EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed)
 265: 	{
 266: 		Quad->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator::ZeroRotator);
 267: 	}
 268: 	else if (Facing == EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground)
 269: 	{
 270: 		Quad->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator::ZeroRotator);
 271: 	}
 272: 	else
 273: 	{
 274: 		FVector CamPos = FVector::ZeroVector;
 275: 		FVector CamUp = FVector::UpVector;
 276: 		bool bHasCamera = false;
 277: 		if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
 278: 		{
 279: 			if (APlayerCameraManager* Manager = UGameplayStatics::GetPlayerCameraManager(World, 0))
 280: 			{
 281: 				CamPos = Manager->GetCameraLocation();
 282: 				CamUp = Manager->GetCameraRotation().RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
 283: 				bHasCamera = true;
 284: 			}
 285: 		}
 286: 		if (bHasCamera)
 287: 		{
 288: 			const FVector QuadPos = Quad->GetComponentLocation();
 289: 			FVector ToCam = CamPos - QuadPos;
 290: 			if (Facing == EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright)
 291: 			{
 292: 				ToCam.Z = 0.f;
 293: 				CamUp = FVector::UpVector;
 294: 			}
 295: 			if (ToCam.SizeSquared() > 1.f)
 296: 			{
 297: 				const FVector ZAxis = ToCam.GetSafeNormal();
 298: 				FVector XAxis = FVector::CrossProduct(CamUp, ZAxis);
 299: 				if (XAxis.SizeSquared() < 1.e-6f)
 300: 				{
 301: 					XAxis = FVector(1.f, 0.f, 0.f);
 302: 				}
 303: 				else
 304: 				{
 305: 					XAxis.Normalize();
 306: 				}
 307: 				const FVector YAxis = FVector::CrossProduct(ZAxis, XAxis);
 308: 				Quad->SetWorldRotation(FMatrix(XAxis, YAxis, ZAxis, FVector::ZeroVector).Rotator());
 309: 			}
 310: 		}
 311: 	}
 312: 
 313: 	if (ClipLight && CurrentClip.IsValid())
 314: 	{
 315: 		const float LW = CurrentClip->WorldWidthCm * SizeScale;
 316: 		const float LH = CurrentClip->WorldHeightCm * SizeScale;
 317: 		ClipLight->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(
 318: 			(CurrentClip->Light.AnchorU - CurrentClip->PivotU) * LW,
 319: 			(CurrentClip->PivotV - CurrentClip->Light.AnchorV) * LH,
 320: 			0.f));
 321: 	}
 322: }
 323: 
 324: void UGBPixelVfxComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType,
 325: 	FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
 326: {
 327: 	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);
 328: 	if (!bPlaying || !CurrentClip.IsValid())
 329: 	{
 330: 		return;
 331: 	}
 332: 	PlayTime += DeltaTime * PlayRate;
 333: 	UpdateFrame(false);
 334: 	UpdateQuadTransform();
 335: }
 336: 
 337: void UGBPixelVfxComponent::GetQuadWorldBounds(FVector& OutOrigin, FVector& OutExtent) const
 338: {
 339: 	OutOrigin = FVector::ZeroVector;
 340: 	OutExtent = FVector::ZeroVector;
 341: 	if (Quad && Quad->GetStaticMesh())
 342: 	{
 343: 		const FBoxSphereBounds MeshBounds = Quad->GetStaticMesh()->GetBounds();
 344: 		OutOrigin = Quad->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(MeshBounds.Origin);
 345: 		OutExtent = Quad->GetComponentTransform().TransformVector(MeshBounds.BoxExtent);
 346: 	}
 347: }
Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source/GBPixelVfx/Private/GBPixelVfxSubsystem.cpp
   1: // UGBPixelVfxSubsystem: clip JSON + PNG atlas loading with caching.
   2: #include "GBPixelVfxSubsystem.h"
   3: 
   4: #include "Engine/Texture2D.h"
   5: #include "HAL/FileManager.h"
   6: #include "ImageCore.h"
   7: #include "ImageUtils.h"
   8: #include "Misc/FileHelper.h"
   9: #include "Misc/Paths.h"
  10: #include "Modules/ModuleManager.h"
  11: 
  12: FString UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::VfxDataDir()
  13: {
  14: 	return FPaths::ProjectContentDir() / TEXT("Data/Vfx");
  15: }
  16: 
  17: bool UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::LoadClip(FName Id) const
  18: {
  19: 	if (Id.IsNone())
  20: 	{
  21: 		return false;
  22: 	}
  23: 	if (ClipCache.Contains(Id) || MissingIds.Contains(Id))
  24: 	{
  25: 		return ClipCache.Contains(Id);
  26: 	}
  27: 	const FString JsonPath = VfxDataDir() / (Id.ToString() + TEXT(".json"));
  28: 	FString JsonText;
  29: 	if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToString(JsonText, *JsonPath))
  30: 	{
  31: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx clip '%s' is missing (%s)."), *Id.ToString(), *JsonPath);
  32: 		MissingIds.Add(Id);
  33: 		return false;
  34: 	}
  35: 	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
  36: 	if (!Clip.LoadFromJsonString(JsonText))
  37: 	{
  38: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx clip '%s' is broken (%s)."), *Id.ToString(), *JsonPath);
  39: 		MissingIds.Add(Id);
  40: 		return false;
  41: 	}
  42: 	ClipCache.Add(Id, MoveTemp(Clip));
  43: 	return true;
  44: }
  45: 
  46: const FGBPixelVfxClip* UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::FindClip(FName Id) const
  47: {
  48: 	if (!LoadClip(Id))
  49: 	{
  50: 		return nullptr;
  51: 	}
  52: 	return ClipCache.Find(Id);
  53: }
  54: 
  55: UTexture2D* UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::GetAtlas(FName Id) const
  56: {
  57: 	const FGBPixelVfxClip* Clip = FindClip(Id);
  58: 	if (!Clip)
  59: 	{
  60: 		return nullptr;
  61: 	}
  62: 	if (TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>* Cached = AtlasCache.Find(Id))
  63: 	{
  64: 		if (*Cached)
  65: 		{
  66: 			return *Cached;
  67: 		}
  68: 	}
  69: 	const FString PngPath = VfxDataDir() / Clip->AtlasFile;
  70: 	TArray<uint8> PngBytes;
  71: 	if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToArray(PngBytes, *PngPath))
  72: 	{
  73: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx atlas for '%s' is missing (%s)."), *Id.ToString(), *PngPath);
  74: 		MissingIds.Add(Id);
  75: 		ClipCache.Remove(Id);
  76: 		return nullptr;
  77: 	}
  78: 	FImage Image;
  79: 	if (!FImageUtils::DecompressImage(PngBytes.GetData(), PngBytes.Num(), Image)
  80: 		|| Image.Format != ERawImageFormat::BGRA8)
  81: 	{
  82: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx atlas for '%s' could not be decoded."), *Id.ToString());
  83: 		return nullptr;
  84: 	}
  85: 	const int32 AtlasW = Image.SizeX;
  86: 	const int32 AtlasH = Image.SizeY;
  87: 	const int32 ExpectW = Clip->Columns * Clip->FrameWidth;
  88: 	const int32 ExpectH = Clip->Rows() * Clip->FrameHeight;
  89: 	if (AtlasW != ExpectW || AtlasH != ExpectH
  90: 		|| Image.RawData.Num() != static_cast<int64>(AtlasW) * AtlasH * 4)
  91: 	{
  92: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx atlas for '%s' is %dx%d but the clip needs %dx%d."),
  93: 			*Id.ToString(), AtlasW, AtlasH, ExpectW, ExpectH);
  94: 		return nullptr;
  95: 	}
  96: 	UTexture2D* Texture = UTexture2D::CreateTransient(AtlasW, AtlasH, PF_B8G8R8A8);
  97: 	if (!Texture || !Texture->GetPlatformData() || Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips.Num() == 0)
  98: 	{
  99: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx atlas for '%s': could not create a transient texture."), *Id.ToString());
 100: 		return nullptr;
 101: 	}
 102: 	Texture->Filter = TF_Nearest;
 103: 	Texture->SRGB = true;
 104: 	Texture->NeverStream = true;
 105: 	Texture->CompressionSettings = TC_EditorIcon;
 106: 	Texture->LODGroup = TEXTUREGROUP_Pixels2D;
 107: 	Texture->AddressX = TA_Clamp;
 108: 	Texture->AddressY = TA_Clamp;
 109: #if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
 110: 	Texture->MipGenSettings = TMGS_NoMipmaps;
 111: #endif
 112: 	// FColor is B, G, R, A in memory: exactly BGRA8; rows go top to bottom.
 113: 	FTexture2DMipMap& Mip = Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips[0];
 114: 	void* Data = Mip.BulkData.Lock(LOCK_READ_WRITE);
 115: 	FMemory::Memcpy(Data, Image.RawData.GetData(), Image.RawData.Num());
 116: 	Mip.BulkData.Unlock();
 117: 	Texture->UpdateResource();
 118: 	AtlasCache.Add(Id, Texture);
 119: 	return Texture;
 120: }
 121: 
 122: TArray<FName> UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::ListClipIds() const
 123: {
 124: 	TArray<FName> Ids;
 125: 	TArray<FString> Files;
 126: 	IFileManager::Get().FindFiles(Files, *(VfxDataDir() / TEXT("*.json")), true, false);
 127: 	for (const FString& File : Files)
 128: 	{
 129: 		Ids.Add(FName(*FPaths::GetBaseFilename(File)));
 130: 	}
 131: 	Ids.Sort([](const FName& A, const FName& B) { return A.LexicalLess(B); });
 132: 	return Ids;
 133: }
 134: 
 135: void UGBPixelVfxSubsystem::ClearCache()
 136: {
 137: 	AtlasCache.Reset();
 138: 	ClipCache.Reset();
 139: 	MissingIds.Reset();
 140: }
Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source/GBPixelVfx/Private/GBPixelVfxActor.cpp
   1: // AGBPixelVfxActor: root component wrapper plus the static spawn helper.
   2: #include "GBPixelVfxActor.h"
   3: 
   4: #include "GBPixelVfxComponent.h"
   5: #include "GBPixelVfxSubsystem.h"
   6: 
   7: AGBPixelVfxActor::AGBPixelVfxActor()
   8: {
   9: 	PixelVfx = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBPixelVfxComponent>(TEXT("PixelVfx"));
  10: 	SetRootComponent(PixelVfx);
  11: }
  12: 
  13: AGBPixelVfxActor* AGBPixelVfxActor::SpawnPixelVfx(UObject* WorldContext, FName ClipId,
  14: 	const FTransform& Transform, float SizeScale)
  15: {
  16: 	UWorld* World = WorldContext ? WorldContext->GetWorld() : nullptr;
  17: 	if (!World)
  18: 	{
  19: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PixelVfx spawn '%s': no world."), *ClipId.ToString());
  20: 		return nullptr;
  21: 	}
  22: 	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
  23: 	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
  24: 	AGBPixelVfxActor* Actor = World->SpawnActor<AGBPixelVfxActor>(AGBPixelVfxActor::StaticClass(), Transform, Params);
  25: 	if (!Actor)
  26: 	{
  27: 		return nullptr;
  28: 	}
  29: 	Actor->PixelVfx->SizeScale = SizeScale;
  30: 	const UGBPixelVfxSubsystem* Subsystem = World->GetGameInstance()
  31: 		? World->GetGameInstance()->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelVfxSubsystem>()
  32: 		: nullptr;
  33: 	const FGBPixelVfxClip* Clip = Subsystem ? Subsystem->FindClip(ClipId) : nullptr;
  34: 	if (Clip && !Clip->bLoop)
  35: 	{
  36: 		Actor->PixelVfx->bAutoDestroyOwnerWhenFinished = true;
  37: 	}
  38: 	if (!Actor->PixelVfx->Play(ClipId))
  39: 	{
  40: 		Actor->Destroy();
  41: 		return nullptr;
  42: 	}
  43: 	return Actor;
  44: }
Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source/GBPixelVfx/Private/GBPixelVfxModule.cpp
   1: // GBPixelVfx runtime module: log category plus the pvfx.Spawn console command (non-shipping).
   2: #include "GBPixelVfxLog.h"
   3: 
   4: #include "GBPixelVfxActor.h"
   5: #include "GBPixelVfxComponent.h"
   6: #include "HAL/IConsoleManager.h"
   7: #include "Kismet/GameplayStatics.h"
   8: #include "Modules/ModuleManager.h"
   9: 
  10: DEFINE_LOG_CATEGORY(LogGBPixelVfx);
  11: 
  12: class FGBPixelVfxModule : public FDefaultGameModuleImpl
  13: {
  14: };
  15: 
  16: IMPLEMENT_MODULE(FGBPixelVfxModule, GBPixelVfx);
  17: 
  18: #if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
  19: static void PixelVfxSpawnCommand(const TArray<FString>& Args, UWorld* World)
  20: {
  21: 	if (!World)
  22: 	{
  23: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("pvfx.Spawn: no world."));
  24: 		return;
  25: 	}
  26: 	if (Args.Num() < 1)
  27: 	{
  28: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("pvfx.Spawn usage: pvfx.Spawn <ClipId> [SizeScale]"));
  29: 		return;
  30: 	}
  31: 	const FName ClipId(*Args[0]);
  32: 	float SizeScale = 1.f;
  33: 	if (Args.Num() > 1)
  34: 	{
  35: 		SizeScale = FCString::Atof(*Args[1]);
  36: 	}
  37: 	APlayerCameraManager* Manager = UGameplayStatics::GetPlayerCameraManager(World, 0);
  38: 	if (!Manager)
  39: 	{
  40: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("pvfx.Spawn: no player camera."));
  41: 		return;
  42: 	}
  43: 	const FVector ViewPos = Manager->GetCameraLocation();
  44: 	const FVector ViewDir = Manager->GetCameraRotation().Vector();
  45: 	const FVector Point = ViewPos + ViewDir * 300.f;
  46: 	FVector SpawnAt = Point;
  47: 	FHitResult Hit;

   1: // Pure pixel-art conversion (namespace GBPixelVfxConvert): no UObject, no engine objects.
   2: // Operates on arrays of full resolution linear frames; every step is unit-testable.

   7: namespace GBPixelVfxConvert
   8: {
   9: // Bake settings that drive the conversion. Code defaults are conservative (cleanup passes off);
  10: // the bake jobs pick the real values.
  11: struct FSettings
  12: {
  13: 	int32 FrameWidth = 48;
  14: 	int32 FrameHeight = 64;
  15: 	int32 Fps = 12;
  16: 	bool bLoop = true;
  17: 	int32 LoopBlendFrames = 6;
  18: 	FString AlphaMode = TEXT("Max"); // Opacity, Luminance, Max
  19: 	float AlphaThreshold = 0.3f;
  20: 	FString ColorMode = TEXT("Split"); // Ramp, Split, Nearest
  21: 	float HotLuminance = 0.55f;
  22: 	float HotSaturation = 0.35f;
  23: 	// Palette ramps, dark to light, as sRGB bytes. Output colors are always one of these.
  24: 	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Ramps;
  25: 	float LevelsPercentile = 0.99f;
  26: 	float Gamma = 1.f;
  27: 	float Hysteresis = 0.03f;
  28: 	int32 MinIslandPixels = 0;
  29: 	bool bFillHoles = false;
  30: 	FString Outline = TEXT("None"); // None, Black, Darkest
  31: 	bool bLightEnabled = true;
  32: };
  33: 
  34: // One working frame: RGB is premultiplied by coverage against black (linear HDR),
  35: // Opacity is the coverage (0..1) before any threshold.
  36: struct FWorkFrame
  37: {
  38: 	int32 Width = 0;
  39: 	int32 Height = 0;
  40: 	TArray<FLinearColor> Color;
  41: 	TArray<float> Opacity;
  42: };
  43: 
  44: struct FMappedPixel
  45: {
  46: 	bool bSolid = false;
  47: 	uint8 Ramp = 0;
  48: 	uint8 Index = 0;
  49: 	float Value = 0.f; // levels value v (0..1)
  50: 	bool bHot = false;
  51: };
  52: 
  53: struct FFrameMap
  54: {
  55: 	int32 Width = 0;
  56: 	int32 Height = 0;
  57: 	TArray<FMappedPixel> Pixels;
  58: 
  59: 	int32 SolidCount() const
  60: 	{
  61: 		int32 Count = 0;
  62: 		for (const FMappedPixel& Pixel : Pixels)
  63: 		{
  64: 			if (Pixel.bSolid)
  65: 			{
  66: 				++Count;
  67: 			}
  68: 		}
  69: 		return Count;
  70: 	}
  71: };

Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source/GBPixelVfxBaker/Private/GBPixelVfxConvert.cpp
   1: // GBPixelVfxConvert: Niagara frame -> palette mapped pixel art. See the header for the step order.
   2: #include "GBPixelVfxConvert.h"
   3: 
   4: namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal
   5: {
   6: 	int32 HexDigit(TCHAR C)
   7: 	{
   8: 		if (C >= TEXT('0') && C <= TEXT('9')) return C - TEXT('0');
   9: 		if (C >= TEXT('a') && C <= TEXT('f')) return 10 + C - TEXT('a');
  10: 		if (C >= TEXT('A') && C <= TEXT('F')) return 10 + C - TEXT('A');
  11: 		return -1;
  12: 	}
  13: 
  14: 	float Saturate(float X) { return FMath::Clamp(X, 0.f, 1.f); }
  15: 
  16: 	bool IsSplitMode(const GBPixelVfxConvert::FSettings& Settings)
  17: 	{
  18: 		return Settings.ColorMode.Equals(TEXT("Split"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase) && Settings.Ramps.Num() >= 2;
  19: 	}
  20: 
  21: 	int32 RampIndexForValue(float V, int32 Count)
  22: 	{
  23: 		if (Count <= 0)
  24: 		{
  25: 			return 0;
  26: 		}
  27: 		return FMath::Clamp(FMath::FloorToInt(V * static_cast<float>(Count)), 0, Count - 1);
  28: 	}
  29: 
  30: 	// Nearest-rank percentile of a sorted ascending array. Falls back to 1 when empty.
  31: 	float PercentileOfSorted(const TArray<float>& Sorted, float P)
  32: 	{
  33: 		if (Sorted.Num() == 0)
  34: 		{
  35: 			return 1.f;
  36: 		}
  37: 		const int32 Rank = FMath::Clamp(FMath::CeilToInt(P * Sorted.Num()) - 1, 0, Sorted.Num() - 1);
  38: 		return Sorted[Rank];
  39: 	}
  40: 
  41: 	FLinearColor ToneMapForNearest(const FLinearColor& C, float Peak)
  42: 	{
  43: 		const float Inv = 1.f / FMath::Max(Peak, 1.e-4f);
  44: 		const FLinearColor S(C.R * Inv, C.G * Inv, C.B * Inv);
  45: 		return FLinearColor(S.R / (1.f + S.R), S.G / (1.f + S.G), S.B / (1.f + S.B));
  46: 	}
  47: 
  48: 	void NearestRampColor(const FLinearColor& C, const GBPixelVfxConvert::FSettings& Settings,
  49: 		float Peak, uint8& OutRamp, uint8& OutIndex)
  50: 	{
  51: 		OutRamp = 0;
  52: 		OutIndex = 0;
  53: 		if (Settings.Ramps.Num() == 0 || Settings.Ramps[0].Num() == 0)
  54: 		{
  55: 			return;
  56: 		}
  57: 		const FVector Target = GBPixelVfxConvert::LabFromLinear(ToneMapForNearest(C, Peak));
  58: 		float Best = FLT_MAX;
  59: 		for (int32 Ramp = 0; Ramp < Settings.Ramps.Num(); ++Ramp)
  60: 		{
  61: 			const TArray<FColor>& Colors = Settings.Ramps[Ramp];
  62: 			for (int32 i = 0; i < Colors.Num(); ++i)
  63: 			{
  64: 				const FVector Candidate = GBPixelVfxConvert::LabFromLinear(
  65: 					ToneMapForNearest(FLinearColor(Colors[i]), Peak));
  66: 				const float Dist = static_cast<float>(FVector::DistSquared(Target, Candidate));
  67: 				if (Dist < Best)
  68: 				{
  69: 					Best = Dist;
  70: 					OutRamp = static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Min(Ramp, 255));
  71: 					OutIndex = static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Min(i, 255));
  72: 				}
  73: 			}
  74: 		}
  75: 	}
  76: 
  77: 	bool Inside(int32 X, int32 Y, int32 W, int32 H)
  78: 	{
  79: 		return X >= 0 && Y >= 0 && X < W && Y < H;
  80: 	}
  81: }
  82: 
  83: namespace GBPixelVfxConvert
  84: {
  85: bool ParseHexColor(const FString& Hex, FColor& OutColor)
  86: {
  87: 	FString Text = Hex;
  88: 	Text.TrimStartAndEndInline();
  89: 	if (Text.StartsWith(TEXT("#")))
  90: 	{
  91: 		Text.RightChopInline(1);
  92: 	}
  93: 	if (Text.Len() != 6)
  94: 	{
  95: 		return false;
  96: 	}
  97: 	uint8 Bytes[4] = { 0, 0, 0, 255 };
  98: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
  99: 	{
 100: 		const int32 Hi = GBPixelVfxConvertLocal::HexDigit(Text[i * 2]);
 101: 		const int32 Lo = GBPixelVfxConvertLocal::HexDigit(Text[i * 2 + 1]);
 102: 		if (Hi < 0 || Lo < 0)
 103: 		{
 104: 			return false;
 105: 		}
 106: 		Bytes[i] = static_cast<uint8>(Hi * 16 + Lo);
 107: 	}
 108: 	OutColor = FColor(Bytes[0], Bytes[1], Bytes[2], Bytes[3]);
 109: 	return true;
 110: }
 111: 
 112: float PixelLuminance(const FLinearColor& Color)
 113: {
 114: 	return Color.R * 0.2126f + Color.G * 0.7152f + Color.B * 0.0722f;
 115: }
 116: 
 117: float PixelSaturation(const FLinearColor& Color)
 118: {
 119: 	const float MaxC = FMath::Max3(Color.R, Color.G, Color.B);
 120: 	if (MaxC <= 1.e-6f)
 121: 	{
 122: 		return 0.f;
 123: 	}
 124: 	const float MinC = FMath::Min3(Color.R, Color.G, Color.B);
 125: 	return (MaxC - MinC) / MaxC;
 126: }
 127: 
 128: FVector LabFromLinear(const FLinearColor& Color)
 129: {
 130: 	const float R = FMath::Max(0.f, Color.R);
 131: 	const float G = FMath::Max(0.f, Color.G);
 132: 	const float B = FMath::Max(0.f, Color.B);
 133: 	const float L = 0.4122214708f * R + 0.5363325363f * G + 0.0514459929f * B;
 134: 	const float M = 0.2119034982f * R + 0.6806995451f * G + 0.1073969566f * B;
 135: 	const float S = 0.0883024619f * R + 0.2817188376f * G + 0.6299787005f * B;
 136: 	const float Lc = FMath::Pow(FMath::Max(0.f, L), 1.f / 3.f);
 137: 	const float Mc = FMath::Pow(FMath::Max(0.f, M), 1.f / 3.f);
 138: 	const float Sc = FMath::Pow(FMath::Max(0.f, S), 1.f / 3.f);
 139: 	return FVector(
 140: 		0.2104542553 * Lc + 0.7936177850 * Mc - 0.0040720468 * Sc,
 141: 		1.9779984951 * Lc - 2.4285922050 * Mc + 0.4505937099 * Sc,
 142: 		0.0259040371 * Lc + 0.7827717662 * Mc - 0.8086757660 * Sc);
 143: }
 144: 
 145: bool IsHotPixel(const FLinearColor& Unpremultiplied, const FSettings& Settings)
 146: {
 147: 	return PixelLuminance(Unpremultiplied) >= Settings.HotLuminance
 148: 		|| PixelSaturation(Unpremultiplied) >= Settings.HotSaturation;
 149: }
 150: 
 151: void ApplyExposure(TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, float Exposure)
 152: {
 153: 	if (FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Exposure, 1.f))
 154: 	{
 155: 		return;
 156: 	}
 157: 	for (FWorkFrame& Frame : Frames)
 158: 	{
 159: 		for (FLinearColor& Pixel : Frame.Color)
 160: 		{
 161: 			Pixel.R *= Exposure;
 162: 			Pixel.G *= Exposure;
 163: 			Pixel.B *= Exposure;
 164: 		}
 165: 	}
 166: }
 167: 
 168: void ApplyLoopBlend(TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, int32 KeepCount, int32 BlendFrames, bool bLoop)
 169: {
 170: 	if (!bLoop || BlendFrames <= 0 || KeepCount <= 0 || Frames.Num() < KeepCount + BlendFrames)
 171: 	{
 172: 		while (Frames.Num() > KeepCount)
 173: 		{
 174: 			Frames.Pop();
 175: 		}
 176: 		return;
 177: 	}
 178: 	const int32 B = FMath::Min(BlendFrames, KeepCount);
 179: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < B; ++i)
 180: 	{
 181: 		FWorkFrame& Dst = Frames[i];
 182: 		const FWorkFrame& Extra = Frames[KeepCount + i];
 183: 		if (Dst.Color.Num() != Extra.Color.Num() || Dst.Opacity.Num() != Extra.Opacity.Num())
 184: 		{
 185: 			continue;
 186: 		}
 187: 		const float T = (static_cast<float>(i) + 0.5f) / static_cast<float>(B);
 188: 		for (int32 p = 0; p < Dst.Color.Num(); ++p)
 189: 		{
 190: 			Dst.Color[p] = Extra.Color[p] * (1.f - T) + Dst.Color[p] * T;
 191: 		}
 192: 		for (int32 p = 0; p < Dst.Opacity.Num(); ++p)
 193: 		{
 194: 			Dst.Opacity[p] = Extra.Opacity[p] * (1.f - T) + Dst.Opacity[p] * T;
 195: 		}
 196: 	}
 197: 	while (Frames.Num() > KeepCount)
 198: 	{
 199: 		Frames.Pop();
 200: 	}
 201: }
 202: 
 203: FWorkFrame DownsampleFrame(const FWorkFrame& In, int32 OutWidth, int32 OutHeight)
 204: {
 205: 	FWorkFrame Out;
 206: 	Out.Width = OutWidth;
 207: 	Out.Height = OutHeight;
 208: 	if (In.Width <= 0 || In.Height <= 0 || OutWidth <= 0 || OutHeight <= 0
 209: 		|| In.Color.Num() != In.Width * In.Height || In.Opacity.Num() != In.Width * In.Height)
 210: 	{
 211: 		return Out;
 212: 	}
 213: 	Out.Color.SetNumZeroed(OutWidth * OutHeight);
 214: 	Out.Opacity.SetNumZeroed(OutWidth * OutHeight);
 215: 	for (int32 y = 0; y < OutHeight; ++y)
 216: 	{
 217: 		const int32 Y0 = y * In.Height / OutHeight;
 218: 		const int32 Y1 = (y + 1) * In.Height / OutHeight;
 219: 		for (int32 x = 0; x < OutWidth; ++x)
 220: 		{
 221: 			const int32 X0 = x * In.Width / OutWidth;
 222: 			const int32 X1 = (x + 1) * In.Width / OutWidth;
 223: 			FLinearColor Sum(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.f);
 224: 			float OpSum = 0.f;
 225: 			int32 Count = 0;
 226: 			for (int32 sy = Y0; sy < Y1; ++sy)
 227: 			{
 228: 				for (int32 sx = X0; sx < X1; ++sx)
 229: 				{
 230: 					const int32 Src = sy * In.Width + sx;
 231: 					Sum += In.Color[Src];
 232: 					OpSum += In.Opacity[Src];
 233: 					++Count;
 234: 				}
 235: 			}
 236: 			const int32 Dst = y * OutWidth + x;
 237: 			if (Count > 0)
 238: 			{
 239: 				Out.Color[Dst] = Sum / static_cast<float>(Count);
 240: 				Out.Opacity[Dst] = OpSum / static_cast<float>(Count);
 241: 			}
 242: 		}
 243: 	}
 244: 	return Out;
 245: }
 246: 
 247: void ComputePeaks(const TArray<FWorkFrame>& Frames, const FSettings& Settings,
 248: 	float& OutPeak, float& OutHotPeak, float& OutColdPeak)
 249: {
 250: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
 251: 	TArray<float> All;
 252: 	TArray<float> Hot;
 253: 	TArray<float> Cold;
 254: 	const bool bSplit = IsSplitMode(Settings);
 255: 	for (const FWorkFrame& Frame : Frames)
 256: 	{
 257: 		const int32 Count = FMath::Min(Frame.Color.Num(), Frame.Opacity.Num());
 258: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < Count; ++i)
 259: 		{
 260: 			const float Op = Frame.Opacity[i];
 261: 			const FLinearColor& RGB = Frame.Color[i];
 262: 			const float Lum = PixelLuminance(RGB);
 263: 			float A = Op;
 264: 			if (Settings.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Luminance"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 265: 			{
 266: 				A = Saturate(Lum);
 267: 			}
 268: 			else if (Settings.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Max"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 269: 			{
 270: 				A = FMath::Max(Op, Saturate(Lum));
 271: 			}
 272: 			if (A < Settings.AlphaThreshold)
 273: 			{
 274: 				continue;
 275: 			}
 276: 			const FLinearColor C = RGB / FMath::Max(A, 1.e-4f);
 277: 			const float LumC = PixelLuminance(C);
 278: 			All.Add(LumC);
 279: 			if (bSplit)
 280: 			{
 281: 				if (IsHotPixel(C, Settings))
 282: 				{
 283: 					Hot.Add(LumC);
 284: 				}
 285: 				else
 286: 				{
 287: 					Cold.Add(LumC);
 288: 				}
 289: 			}
 290: 		}
 291: 	}
 292: 	All.Sort();
 293: 	Hot.Sort();
 294: 	Cold.Sort();
 295: 	OutPeak = PercentileOfSorted(All, Settings.LevelsPercentile);
 296: 	OutHotPeak = Hot.Num() > 0 ? PercentileOfSorted(Hot, Settings.LevelsPercentile) : OutPeak;
 297: 	OutColdPeak = Cold.Num() > 0 ? PercentileOfSorted(Cold, Settings.LevelsPercentile) : OutPeak;
 298: 	OutPeak = FMath::Max(OutPeak, 1.e-4f);
 299: 	OutHotPeak = FMath::Max(OutHotPeak, 1.e-4f);
 300: 	OutColdPeak = FMath::Max(OutColdPeak, 1.e-4f);
 301: }
 302: 
 303: FFrameMap MapFrame(const FWorkFrame& Frame, const FSettings& Settings,
 304: 	float Peak, float HotPeak, float ColdPeak)
 305: {
 306: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
 307: 	FFrameMap Map;
 308: 	Map.Width = Frame.Width;
 309: 	Map.Height = Frame.Height;
 310: 	const int32 Count = FMath::Min(Frame.Color.Num(), Frame.Opacity.Num());
 311: 	Map.Pixels.SetNum(Count);
 312: 	const bool bSplit = IsSplitMode(Settings);
 313: 	const bool bNearest = Settings.ColorMode.Equals(TEXT("Nearest"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
 314: 	const int32 Ramp0Count = Settings.Ramps.Num() > 0 ? Settings.Ramps[0].Num() : 0;
 315: 	const int32 Ramp1Count = Settings.Ramps.Num() > 1 ? Settings.Ramps[1].Num() : 0;
 316: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Count; ++i)
 317: 	{
 318: 		FMappedPixel& Dst = Map.Pixels[i];
 319: 		const float Op = Frame.Opacity[i];
 320: 		const FLinearColor& RGB = Frame.Color[i];
 321: 		const float Lum = PixelLuminance(RGB);
 322: 		float A = Op;
 323: 		if (Settings.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Luminance"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 324: 		{
 325: 			A = Saturate(Lum);
 326: 		}
 327: 		else if (Settings.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Max"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 328: 		{
 329: 			A = FMath::Max(Op, Saturate(Lum));
 330: 		}
 331: 		if (A < Settings.AlphaThreshold)
 332: 		{
 333: 			continue;
 334: 		}
 335: 		const FLinearColor C = RGB / FMath::Max(A, 1.e-4f);
 336: 		const float LumC = PixelLuminance(C);
 337: 		Dst.bSolid = true;
 338: 		if (bNearest)
 339: 		{
 340: 			Dst.Value = Saturate(FMath::Pow(FMath::Clamp(LumC / Peak, 0.f, 1.f), Settings.Gamma));
 341: 			Dst.bHot = true;
 342: 			NearestRampColor(C, Settings, Peak, Dst.Ramp, Dst.Index);
 343: 		}
 344: 		else if (bSplit)
 345: 		{
 346: 			if (IsHotPixel(C, Settings))
 347: 			{
 348: 				Dst.bHot = true;
 349: 				Dst.Value = Saturate(FMath::Pow(FMath::Clamp(LumC / HotPeak, 0.f, 1.f), Settings.Gamma));
 350: 				Dst.Ramp = 0;
 351: 				Dst.Index = static_cast<uint8>(RampIndexForValue(Dst.Value, Ramp0Count));
 352: 			}
 353: 			else
 354: 			{
 355: 				Dst.bHot = false;
 356: 				Dst.Value = Saturate(FMath::Pow(FMath::Clamp(LumC / ColdPeak, 0.f, 1.f), Settings.Gamma));
 357: 				Dst.Ramp = 1;
 358: 				Dst.Index = static_cast<uint8>(RampIndexForValue(Dst.Value, Ramp1Count));
 359: 			}
 360: 		}
 361: 		else
 362: 		{
 363: 			Dst.bHot = true;
 364: 			Dst.Value = Saturate(FMath::Pow(FMath::Clamp(LumC / Peak, 0.f, 1.f), Settings.Gamma));
 365: 			Dst.Ramp = 0;
 366: 			Dst.Index = static_cast<uint8>(RampIndexForValue(Dst.Value, Ramp0Count));
 367: 		}
 368: 	}
 369: 	return Map;
 370: }
 371: 
 372: void ApplyHysteresis(FFrameMap& Current, const FFrameMap& Prev, const FSettings& Settings)
 373: {
 374: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
 375: 	if (Current.Pixels.Num() != Prev.Pixels.Num() || Settings.Hysteresis <= 0.f)
 376: 	{
 377: 		return;
 378: 	}
 379: 	const int32 PrevSolid = Prev.SolidCount();
 380: 	if (PrevSolid > 0)
 381: 	{
 382: 		const int32 CurSolid = Current.SolidCount();
 383: 		// Fast changing frames (explosions): damping would smear real change, so skip it.
 384: 		if (FMath::Abs(static_cast<float>(CurSolid - PrevSolid)) / static_cast<float>(PrevSolid) > 0.3f)
 385: 		{
 386: 			return;
 387: 		}
 388: 	}
 389: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Current.Pixels.Num(); ++i)
 390: 	{
 391: 		FMappedPixel& Dst = Current.Pixels[i];
 392: 		const FMappedPixel& Src = Prev.Pixels[i];
 393: 		if (!Dst.bSolid || !Src.bSolid || Dst.Ramp != Src.Ramp)
 394: 		{
 395: 			continue;
 396: 		}
 397: 		if (Dst.Index == Src.Index)
 398: 		{
 399: 			continue;
 400: 		}
 401: 		int32 RampLen = 0;
 402: 		if (Src.Ramp < static_cast<uint8>(Settings.Ramps.Num()))
 403: 		{
 404: 			RampLen = Settings.Ramps[Src.Ramp].Num();
 405: 		}
 406: 		if (RampLen <= 0)
 407: 		{
 408: 			continue;
 409: 		}
 410: 		// The previous band widened by Hysteresis on both sides keeps the old index.
 411: 		const float BandLo = static_cast<float>(Src.Index) / static_cast<float>(RampLen) - Settings.Hysteresis;
 412: 		const float BandHi = (static_cast<float>(Src.Index) + 1.f) / static_cast<float>(RampLen) + Settings.Hysteresis;
 413: 		if (Dst.Value >= BandLo && Dst.Value <= BandHi)
 414: 		{
 415: 			Dst.Index = Src.Index;
 416: 		}
 417: 	}
 418: }
 419: 
 420: void RemoveSmallIslands(FFrameMap& Map, int32 MinIslandPixels)
 421: {
 422: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
 423: 	if (MinIslandPixels <= 1 || Map.Width <= 0 || Map.Height <= 0
 424: 		|| Map.Pixels.Num() != Map.Width * Map.Height)
 425: 	{
 426: 		return;
 427: 	}
 428: 	const int32 W = Map.Width;
 429: 	const int32 H = Map.Height;
 430: 	TArray<int32> Island;
 431: 	Island.Reserve(64);
 432: 	TArray<int32> Stack;
 433: 	Stack.Reserve(64);
 434: 	TArray<uint8> Seen;
 435: 	Seen.SetNumZeroed(W * H);
 436: 	const int32 DX[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
 437: 	const int32 DY[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
 438: 	for (int32 Start = 0; Start < W * H; ++Start)
 439: 	{
 440: 		if (!Map.Pixels[Start].bSolid || Seen[Start])
 441: 		{
 442: 			continue;
 443: 		}
 444: 		Island.Reset();
 445: 		Stack.Reset();
 446: 		Stack.Add(Start);
 447: 		Seen[Start] = 1;
 448: 		while (Stack.Num() > 0)
 449: 		{
 450: 			const int32 At = Stack.Pop();
 451: 			Island.Add(At);
 452: 			const int32 X = At % W;
 453: 			const int32 Y = At / W;
 454: 			for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
 455: 			{
 456: 				const int32 NX = X + DX[d];
 457: 				const int32 NY = Y + DY[d];
 458: 				if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H))
 459: 				{
 460: 					continue;
 461: 				}
 462: 				const int32 NAt = NY * W + NX;
 463: 				if (!Seen[NAt] && Map.Pixels[NAt].bSolid)
 464: 				{
 465: 					Seen[NAt] = 1;
 466: 					Stack.Add(NAt);
 467: 				}
 468: 			}
 469: 		}
 470: 		if (Island.Num() < MinIslandPixels)
 471: 		{
 472: 			for (int32 At : Island)
 473: 			{
 474: 				Map.Pixels[At].bSolid = false;
 475: 			}
 476: 		}
 477: 	}
 478: }
 479: 
 480: void FillOnePixelHoles(FFrameMap& Map)
 481: {
 482: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
 483: 	if (Map.Width <= 0 || Map.Height <= 0 || Map.Pixels.Num() != Map.Width * Map.Height)
 484: 	{
 485: 		return;
 486: 	}
 487: 	const int32 W = Map.Width;
 488: 	const int32 H = Map.Height;
 489: 	const int32 DX[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
 490: 	const int32 DY[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
 491: 	for (int32 y = 0; y < H; ++y)
 492: 	{
 493: 		for (int32 x = 0; x < W; ++x)
 494: 		{
 495: 			const int32 At = y * W + x;
 496: 			if (Map.Pixels[At].bSolid)
 497: 			{
 498: 				continue;
 499: 			}
 500: 			FMappedPixel Votes[4];
 501: 			int32 VoteCount = 0;
 502: 			bool bAllSolid = true;
 503: 			for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
 504: 			{
 505: 				const int32 NX = x + DX[d];
 506: 				const int32 NY = y + DY[d];
 507: 				if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H) || !Map.Pixels[NY * W + NX].bSolid)
 508: 				{
 509: 					bAllSolid = false;
 510: 					break;
 511: 				}
 512: 				Votes[VoteCount++] = Map.Pixels[NY * W + NX];
 513: 			}
 514: 			if (!bAllSolid)
 515: 			{
 516: 				continue;
 517: 			}
 518: 			// Most common neighbor color wins; ties go to the first neighbor.
 519: 			int32 Best = 0;
 520: 			int32 BestVotes = -1;
 521: 			for (int32 a = 0; a < VoteCount; ++a)
 522: 			{
 523: 				int32 VotesForA = 0;
 524: 				for (int32 b = 0; b < VoteCount; ++b)
 525: 				{
 526: 					if (Votes[b].Ramp == Votes[a].Ramp && Votes[b].Index == Votes[a].Index)
 527: 					{
 528: 						++VotesForA;
 529: 					}
 530: 				}
 531: 				if (VotesForA > BestVotes)
 532: 				{
 533: 					BestVotes = VotesForA;
 534: 					Best = a;
 535: 				}
 536: 			}
 537: 			Map.Pixels[At] = Votes[Best];
 538: 			Map.Pixels[At].bSolid = true;
 539: 		}
 540: 	}
 541: }
 542: 
 543: bool ResolveOutlineColor(const FSettings& Settings, FColor& OutColor)
 544: {
 545: 	if (Settings.Outline.Equals(TEXT("Black"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 546: 	{
 547: 		OutColor = FColor(0, 0, 0, 255);
 548: 		return true;
 549: 	}
 550: 	if (Settings.Outline.Equals(TEXT("Darkest"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)
 551: 		&& Settings.Ramps.Num() > 0 && Settings.Ramps[0].Num() > 0)
 552: 	{
 553: 		OutColor = Settings.Ramps[0][0];
 554: 		OutColor.A = 255;
 555: 		return true;
 556: 	}
 557: 	return false;
 558: }
 559: 
 560: void ResolveFrames(const TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings,
 561: 	TArray<TArray<FColor>>& OutPixels)
 562: {
 563: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
 564: 	OutPixels.Reset(Maps.Num());
 565: 	FColor OutlineColor(0, 0, 0, 255);
 566: 	const bool bOutline = ResolveOutlineColor(Settings, OutlineColor);
 567: 	for (const FFrameMap& Map : Maps)
 568: 	{
 569: 		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 570: 		Pixels.SetNum(Map.Pixels.Num());
 571: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < Map.Pixels.Num(); ++i)
 572: 		{
 573: 			const FMappedPixel& Pixel = Map.Pixels[i];
 574: 			if (Pixel.bSolid && Pixel.Ramp < Settings.Ramps.Num()
 575: 				&& Pixel.Index < Settings.Ramps[Pixel.Ramp].Num())
 576: 			{
 577: 				Pixels[i] = Settings.Ramps[Pixel.Ramp][Pixel.Index];
 578: 				Pixels[i].A = 255;
 579: 			}
 580: 			else
 581: 			{
 582: 				Pixels[i] = FColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
 583: 			}
 584: 		}
 585: 		if (bOutline && Map.Width > 0 && Map.Height > 0 && Map.Pixels.Num() == Map.Width * Map.Height)
 586: 		{
 587: 			const int32 W = Map.Width;
 588: 			const int32 H = Map.Height;
 589: 			const int32 DX[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
 590: 			const int32 DY[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
 591: 			for (int32 y = 0; y < H; ++y)
 592: 			{
 593: 				for (int32 x = 0; x < W; ++x)
 594: 				{
 595: 					const int32 At = y * W + x;
 596: 					if (Map.Pixels[At].bSolid)
 597: 					{
 598: 						continue;
 599: 					}
 600: 					bool bAdjacent = false;
 601: 					for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
 602: 					{
 603: 						const int32 NX = x + DX[d];
 604: 						const int32 NY = y + DY[d];
 605: 						if (Inside(NX, NY, W, H) && Map.Pixels[NY * W + NX].bSolid)
 606: 						{
 607: 							bAdjacent = true;
 608: 							break;
 609: 						}
 610: 					}
 611: 					if (bAdjacent)
 612: 					{
 613: 						Pixels[At] = OutlineColor;
 614: 					}
 615: 				}
 616: 			}
 617: 		}
 618: 		OutPixels.Add(MoveTemp(Pixels));
 619: 	}
 620: }
 621: 
 622: void ComputeLightTrack(const TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings,
 623: 	TArray<float>& OutTrack, float& OutAnchorU, float& OutAnchorV)
 624: {
 625: 	OutTrack.Reset();
 626: 	OutAnchorU = 0.5f;
 627: 	OutAnchorV = 0.5f;
 628: 	if (!Settings.bLightEnabled || Maps.Num() == 0)
 629: 	{
 630: 		return;
 631: 	}
 632: 	OutTrack.SetNum(Maps.Num());
 633: 	float MaxE = 0.f;
 634: 	double AnchorUSum = 0.0;
 635: 	double AnchorVSum = 0.0;
 636: 	double AnchorWeight = 0.0;
 637: 	for (int32 f = 0; f < Maps.Num(); ++f)
 638: 	{
 639: 		const FFrameMap& Map = Maps[f];
 640: 		float E = 0.f;
 641: 		double FrameUSum = 0.0;
 642: 		double FrameVSum = 0.0;
 643: 		double FrameWeight = 0.0;
 644: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < Map.Pixels.Num(); ++i)
 645: 		{
 646: 			const FMappedPixel& Pixel = Map.Pixels[i];
 647: 			if (!Pixel.bSolid || !Pixel.bHot)
 648: 			{
 649: 				continue;
 650: 			}
 651: 			E += Pixel.Value;
 652: 			if (Map.Width > 0 && Map.Height > 0)
 653: 			{
 654: 				const float U = (static_cast<float>(i % Map.Width) + 0.5f) / static_cast<float>(Map.Width);
 655: 				const float V = (static_cast<float>(i / Map.Width) + 0.5f) / static_cast<float>(Map.Height);
 656: 				FrameUSum += U * Pixel.Value;
 657: 				FrameVSum += V * Pixel.Value;
 658: 				FrameWeight += Pixel.Value;
 659: 			}
 660: 		}
 661: 		OutTrack[f] = E;
 662: 		MaxE = FMath::Max(MaxE, E);
 663: 		if (FrameWeight > 0.0)
 664: 		{
 665: 			AnchorUSum += FrameUSum / FrameWeight;
 666: 			AnchorVSum += FrameVSum / FrameWeight;
 667: 			AnchorWeight += 1.0;
 668: 		}
 669: 	}
 670: 	if (MaxE > 0.f)
 671: 	{
 672: 		for (float& Entry : OutTrack)
 673: 		{
 674: 			Entry /= MaxE;
 675: 		}
 676: 	}
 677: 	else
 678: 	{
 679: 		for (float& Entry : OutTrack)
 680: 		{
 681: 			Entry = 0.f;
 682: 		}
 683: 	}
 684: 	if (AnchorWeight > 0.0)
 685: 	{
 686: 		OutAnchorU = static_cast<float>(AnchorUSum / AnchorWeight);
 687: 		OutAnchorV = static_cast<float>(AnchorVSum / AnchorWeight);
 688: 	}
 689: }
 690: 
 691: int32 CountDistinctColors(const TArray<TArray<FColor>>& Frames)
 692: {
 693: 	TSet<uint32> Seen;
 694: 	for (const TArray<FColor>& Pixels : Frames)
 695: 	{
 696: 		for (const FColor& Pixel : Pixels)
 697: 		{
 698: 			if (Pixel.A == 0)
 699: 			{
 700: 				continue;
 701: 			}
 702: 			Seen.Add((static_cast<uint32>(Pixel.R) << 16) | (static_cast<uint32>(Pixel.G) << 8) | Pixel.B);
 703: 		}
 704: 	}
 705: 	return Seen.Num();
 706: }
 707: }

 138: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxSplitSortTest, "GBPixelVfx.Mapping.SplitRouting",
 139: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
 140: bool FGBPixelVfxSplitSortTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
 141: {
 142: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
 143: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal;
 144: 	FSettings Settings = MakeRampSettings();
 145: 	Settings.ColorMode = TEXT("Split");
 146: 	Settings.AlphaMode = TEXT("Opacity");
 147: 	Settings.HotLuminance = 0.55f;
 148: 	Settings.HotSaturation = 0.9f; // only luminance decides in this test
 149: 	TArray<FColor> ColdRamp;
 150: 	ColdRamp.Add(FColor(16, 16, 16));
 151: 	ColdRamp.Add(FColor(64, 64, 64));
 152: 	Settings.Ramps.Add(ColdRamp);
 153: 	FWorkFrame Frame;
 154: 	Frame.Width = 2;
 155: 	Frame.Height = 1;
 156: 	Frame.Color.SetNum(2);
 157: 	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(2);
 158: 	// Hot: bright saturated orange. Cold: dim neutral grey.
 159: 	Frame.Color[0] = FLinearColor(3.f, 0.9f, 0.1f);
 160: 	Frame.Color[1] = FLinearColor(0.2f, 0.2f, 0.2f);
 161: 	Frame.Opacity[0] = 1.f;
 162: 	Frame.Opacity[1] = 1.f;
 163: 	float Peak = 1.f;
 164: 	float Hot = 1.f;
 165: 	float Cold = 1.f;
 166: 	ComputePeaks({ Frame }, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
 167: 	const FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
 168: 	TestTrue(TEXT("bright saturated pixel is hot"), Map.Pixels[0].bHot);
 169: 	TestTrue(TEXT("hot pixel uses ramp 0"), Map.Pixels[0].Ramp == 0);
 170: 	TestFalse(TEXT("dim grey pixel is not hot"), Map.Pixels[1].bHot);
 171: 	TestTrue(TEXT("cold pixel uses ramp 1"), Map.Pixels[1].Ramp == 1);
 172: 	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
 173: 	ResolveFrames({ Map }, Settings, Pixels);
 174: 	TestEqual(TEXT("hot pixel resolves to a ramp 0 color"), Pixels[0][0], Settings.Ramps[0][Map.Pixels[0].Index]);
 175: 	TestEqual(TEXT("cold pixel resolves to a ramp 1 color"), Pixels[0][1], Settings.Ramps[1][Map.Pixels[1].Index]);
 176: 	return true;
 177: }

 179: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxHysteresisHoldTest, "GBPixelVfx.Temporal.HysteresisBand",
 180: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
 181: bool FGBPixelVfxHysteresisHoldTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
 182: {
 183: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
 184: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal;
 185: 	FSettings Settings = MakeRampSettings();
 186: 	Settings.Hysteresis = 0.05f;
 187: 	Settings.LevelsPercentile = 1.f;
 188: 	// A value that sits in band 2 of 4 ([0.5, 0.75)) but wobbles across the lower edge by
 189: 	// less than the margin must keep one color; a second run wobbles far past it and must change.
 190: 	auto RunWobble = [&Settings](float Base, float Wobble) -> TArray<int32>
 191: 	{
 192: 		TArray<int32> Indices;
 193: 		FFrameMap Prev;
 194: 		bool bFirst = true;
 195: 		for (int32 f = 0; f < 6; ++f)
 196: 		{
 197: 			const float V = (f % 2 == 0) ? Base - Wobble : Base + Wobble;
 198: 			FWorkFrame Frame;
 199: 			Frame.Width = 1;
 200: 			Frame.Height = 1;
 201: 			Frame.Color.SetNum(1);
 202: 			Frame.Opacity.SetNum(1);
 203: 			Frame.Color[0] = FLinearColor(V, V, V);
 204: 			Frame.Opacity[0] = 1.f;
 205: 			// Fixed peak of 1 so V is the levels value directly.
 206: 			FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, 1.f, 1.f, 1.f);
 207: 			if (!bFirst)
 208: 			{
 209: 				ApplyHysteresis(Map, Prev, Settings);
 210: 			}
 211: 			bFirst = false;
 212: 			Prev = Map;
 213: 			Indices.Add(Map.Pixels[0].Index);
 214: 		}
 215: 		return Indices;
 216: 	};
 217: 	// Band 2 of 4 spans [0.5, 0.75); wobble 0.02 around 0.51 stays inside the widened band.
 218: 	const TArray<int32> Held = RunWobble(0.51f, 0.02f);
 219: 	bool bAllSame = true;
 220: 	for (int32 Index : Held)
 221: 	{
 222: 		bAllSame = bAllSame && Index == Held[0];
 223: 	}
 224: 	TestTrue(TEXT("a wobble inside the margin keeps one color over 6 frames"), bAllSame);
 225: 	// Wobble 0.3 around 0.6 crosses into far bands: the color must change at least once.
 226: 	const TArray<int32> Moved = RunWobble(0.6f, 0.3f);
 227: 	bool bChanged = false;
 228: 	for (int32 Index : Moved)
 229: 	{
 230: 		bChanged = bChanged || Index != Moved[0];
 231: 	}
 232: 	TestTrue(TEXT("a wobble far past the margin changes the color"), bChanged);
 233: 	return true;
 234: }

 236: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxCleanupIslandsTest, "GBPixelVfx.Cleanup.IslandsAndHoles",
 237: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
 238: bool FGBPixelVfxCleanupIslandsTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
 239: {
 240: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
 241: 	// A 2-pixel island in a 6x6 frame.
 242: 	auto MakeIsland = []() -> FFrameMap
 243: 	{
 244: 		FFrameMap Map;
 245: 		Map.Width = 6;
 246: 		Map.Height = 6;
 247: 		Map.Pixels.SetNum(36);
 248: 		Map.Pixels[0].bSolid = true;
 249: 		Map.Pixels[1].bSolid = true;
 250: 		return Map;
 251: 	};
 252: 	FFrameMap Removed = MakeIsland();
 253: 	RemoveSmallIslands(Removed, 3);
 254: 	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2-pixel island is removed when the minimum is 3"), Removed.SolidCount(), 0);
 255: 	FFrameMap Kept = MakeIsland();
 256: 	RemoveSmallIslands(Kept, 2);
 257: 	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2-pixel island is kept when the minimum is 2"), Kept.SolidCount(), 2);
 258: 
 259: 	// A single transparent hole fully surrounded by solid pixels is filled.
 260: 	FFrameMap Hole;
 261: 	Hole.Width = 3;
 262: 	Hole.Height = 3;
 263: 	Hole.Pixels.SetNum(9);
 264: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < 9; ++i)
 265: 	{
 266: 		Hole.Pixels[i].bSolid = true;
 267: 		Hole.Pixels[i].Ramp = 0;
 268: 		Hole.Pixels[i].Index = 2;
 269: 	}
 270: 	Hole.Pixels[4].bSolid = false;
 271: 	FillOnePixelHoles(Hole);
 272: 	TestTrue(TEXT("a one-pixel hole is filled"), Hole.Pixels[4].bSolid);
 273: 	TestEqual(TEXT("the filled hole takes the neighbor color"), Hole.Pixels[4].Index, 2);
 274: 	return true;
 275: }

 277: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxOutlineShapeTest, "GBPixelVfx.Cleanup.OutlineRing",
 278: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
 279: bool FGBPixelVfxOutlineShapeTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
 280: {
 281: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
 282: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxTestLocal;
 283: 	FSettings Settings = MakeRampSettings();
 284: 	Settings.Outline = TEXT("Black");
 285: 	FFrameMap Map;
 286: 	Map.Width = 6;
 287: 	Map.Height = 6;
 288: 	Map.Pixels.SetNum(36);
 289: 	for (int32 y = 2; y < 4; ++y)
 290: 	{
 291: 		for (int32 x = 2; x < 4; ++x)
 292: 		{
 293: 			FMappedPixel& Pixel = Map.Pixels[y * 6 + x];
 294: 			Pixel.bSolid = true;
 295: 			Pixel.Ramp = 0;
 296: 			Pixel.Index = 1;
 297: 		}
 298: 	}
 299: 	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
 300: 	ResolveFrames({ Map }, Settings, Pixels);
 301: 	const FColor Outline(0, 0, 0, 255);
 302: 	// The 8 4-adjacent pixels around the 2x2 block.
 303: 	const int32 Expected[8][2] = { {2,1},{3,1},{1,2},{4,2},{1,3},{4,3},{2,4},{3,4} };
 304: 	for (const auto& Cell : Expected)
 305: 	{
 306: 		TestEqual(TEXT("4-adjacent pixel is outlined"), Pixels[0][Cell[1] * 6 + Cell[0]], Outline);
 307: 	}
 308: 	TestEqual(TEXT("distant pixel stays transparent"), Pixels[0][0], FColor(0, 0, 0, 0));
 309: 	TestEqual(TEXT("solid pixel keeps its color"), Pixels[0][2 * 6 + 2], Settings.Ramps[0][1]);
 310: 
 311: 	// A block in the corner must not draw outside the frame.
 312: 	FFrameMap Corner;
 313: 	Corner.Width = 6;
 314: 	Corner.Height = 6;
 315: 	Corner.Pixels.SetNum(36);
 316: 	Corner.Pixels[0].bSolid = true;
 317: 	Corner.Pixels[0].Ramp = 0;
 318: 	Corner.Pixels[0].Index = 1;
 319: 	Corner.Pixels[1].bSolid = true;
 320: 	Corner.Pixels[1].Ramp = 0;
 321: 	Corner.Pixels[1].Index = 1;
 322: 	Corner.Pixels[6].bSolid = true;
 323: 	Corner.Pixels[6].Ramp = 0;
 324: 	Corner.Pixels[6].Index = 1;
 325: 	Corner.Pixels[7].bSolid = true;
 326: 	Corner.Pixels[7].Ramp = 0;
 327: 	Corner.Pixels[7].Index = 1;
 328: 	TArray<TArray<FColor>> CornerPixels;
 329: 	ResolveFrames({ Corner }, Settings, CornerPixels);
 330: 	TestTrue(TEXT("corner block resolves without crashing"), CornerPixels[0].Num() == 36);
 331: 	int32 Outlined = 0;
 332: 	for (const FColor& Pixel : CornerPixels[0])
 333: 	{
 334: 		if (Pixel == Outline)
 335: 		{
 336: 			++Outlined;
 337: 		}
 338: 	}
 339: 	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2x2 block in the corner gets 4 in-frame outline pixels"), Outlined, 4);
 340: 	return true;
 341: }

 343: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxLoopBlendFlowTest, "GBPixelVfx.Temporal.LoopBlend",
 344: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
 345: bool FGBPixelVfxLoopBlendFlowTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
 346: {
 347: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
 348: 	auto SolidFrame = [](float Bright) -> FWorkFrame
 349: 	{
 350: 		FWorkFrame Frame;
 351: 		Frame.Width = 2;
 352: 		Frame.Height = 2;
 353: 		Frame.Color.SetNum(4);
 354: 		Frame.Opacity.SetNum(4);
 355: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
 356: 		{
 357: 			Frame.Color[i] = FLinearColor(Bright, Bright, Bright);
 358: 			Frame.Opacity[i] = 1.f;
 359: 		}
 360: 		return Frame;
 361: 	};
 362: 	TArray<FWorkFrame> Frames;
 363: 	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(1.f)); // frame 0: dim
 364: 	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(1.f)); // frame 1: dim
 365: 	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(1.f)); // frame 2: dim
 366: 	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(1.f)); // frame 3: dim, outside the blend window
 367: 	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(9.f)); // extra 0: bright
 368: 	Frames.Add(SolidFrame(9.f)); // extra 1: bright
 369: 	ApplyLoopBlend(Frames, 4, 2, true);
 370: 	TestEqual(TEXT("the extra frames are dropped"), Frames.Num(), 4);
 371: 	// Frame 0 blends 75% extra / 25% self: it must be much closer to the extra frame than to itself.
 372: 	const float Frame0 = Frames[0].Color[0].R;
 373: 	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 0 mostly equals the extra frame"), FMath::Abs(Frame0 - 7.f) < 0.01f);
 374: 	// Frame 3 is past the blend window: the last frame of the clip is unchanged.
 375: 	const float Frame3 = Frames[3].Color[0].R;
 376: 	TestTrue(TEXT("the last frame is unchanged"), FMath::Abs(Frame3 - 1.f) < 0.01f);
 377: 	return true;
 378: }

 380: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxClipPlaybackTest, "GBPixelVfx.Clip.PlaybackHelpers",
 381: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
 382: bool FGBPixelVfxClipPlaybackTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
 383: {
 384: 	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
 385: 	Clip.Columns = 8;
 386: 	Clip.FrameWidth = 48;
 387: 	Clip.FrameHeight = 64;
 388: 	Clip.FrameCount = 24;
 389: 	Clip.Fps = 12;
 390: 	Clip.bLoop = true;
 391: 	// Frame 9 of an 8-column atlas: column 1, row 1 of a 384x192 atlas.
 392: 	const FVector4f Rect = FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameRect(Clip, 9);
 393: 	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 9 offset U is 1/8"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Rect.X, 0.125f));
 394: 	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 9 offset V is 1/3"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Rect.Y, 1.f / 3.f, 0.001f));
 395: 	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 9 scale U is 1/8"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Rect.Z, 0.125f));
 396: 	TestTrue(TEXT("frame 9 scale V is 1/3"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Rect.W, 1.f / 3.f, 0.001f));
 397: 
 398: 	bool bFinished = false;
 399: 	TestEqual(TEXT("a looping clip wraps"), FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(Clip, 2.5f, bFinished), 6);
 400: 	TestFalse(TEXT("a looping clip never finishes"), bFinished);
 401: 	Clip.bLoop = false;
 402: 	TestEqual(TEXT("a one-shot clamps to the last frame"), FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(Clip, 99.f, bFinished), 23);
 403: 	TestTrue(TEXT("a one-shot finishes past its end"), bFinished);
 404: 	bFinished = true;
 405: 	TestEqual(TEXT("a one-shot shows frame 0 at time 0"), FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(Clip, 0.f, bFinished), 0);
 406: 	TestFalse(TEXT("a one-shot is not finished at time 0"), bFinished);
 407: 
 408: 	// JSON round trip keeps the light track.
 409: 	Clip.Id = FName(TEXT("RoundTrip"));
 410: 	Clip.AtlasFile = TEXT("RoundTrip.png");
 411: 	Clip.Light.bEnabled = true;
 412: 	Clip.Light.Track = { 0.2f, 0.9f, 1.f };
 413: 	FString Json;
 414: 	TestTrue(TEXT("clip serializes to JSON"), Clip.SaveToJsonString(Json));
 415: 	FGBPixelVfxClip Back;
 416: 	TestTrue(TEXT("clip parses back from JSON"), Back.LoadFromJsonString(Json));
 417: 	TestEqual(TEXT("the light track survives the round trip"), Back.Light.Track.Num(), 3);
 418: 	if (Back.Light.Track.Num() == 3)
 419: 	{
 420: 		TestTrue(TEXT("the brightest track value survives"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Back.Light.Track[2], 1.f));
 421: 	}
 422: 	TestTrue(TEXT("the id survives the round trip"), Back.Id == FName(TEXT("RoundTrip")));
 423: 	return true;
 424: }
Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source/GBPixelVfxBaker/Private/GBPixelVfxBakeJob.cpp
   1: // FGBPixelVfxBakeJob: JSON loader. Reads exactly the keys of the Bake/Jobs files.
   2: #include "GBPixelVfxBakeJob.h"
   3: 
   4: #include "Dom/JsonObject.h"
   5: #include "Dom/JsonValue.h"
   6: #include "HAL/FileManager.h"
   7: #include "Misc/FileHelper.h"
   8: #include "Misc/Paths.h"
   9: #include "Serialization/JsonReader.h"
  10: #include "Serialization/JsonSerializer.h"
  11: #include "GBPixelVfxConvert.h"
  12: 
  13: namespace GBPixelVfxJobLocal
  14: {
  15: 	bool ParseHex(const FString& Text, FLinearColor& OutColor)
  16: 	{
  17: 		FColor Srgb;
  18: 		if (!GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(Text, Srgb))
  19: 		{
  20: 			return false;
  21: 		}
  22: 		OutColor = FLinearColor(Srgb);
  23: 		return true;
  24: 	}
  25: 
  26: 	void ReadFloats(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, float>& Out)
  27: 	{
  28: 		if (!Obj.IsValid())
  29: 		{
  30: 			return;
  31: 		}
  32: 		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
  33: 		{
  34: 			Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), static_cast<float>(Pair.Value->AsNumber()));
  35: 		}
  36: 	}
  37: 
  38: 	void ReadVectors(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, FVector>& Out)
  39: 	{
  40: 		if (!Obj.IsValid())
  41: 		{
  42: 			return;
  43: 		}
  44: 		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
  45: 		{
  46: 			const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Arr = nullptr;
  47: 			if (Pair.Value->TryGetArray(Arr) && Arr && Arr->Num() == 3)
  48: 			{
  49: 				Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), FVector(
  50: 					(*Arr)[0]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[1]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[2]->AsNumber()));
  51: 			}
  52: 		}
  53: 	}
  54: 
  55: 	void ReadColors(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, FLinearColor>& Out)
  56: 	{
  57: 		if (!Obj.IsValid())
  58: 		{
  59: 			return;
  60: 		}
  61: 		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
  62: 		{
  63: 			FString Text;
  64: 			if (Pair.Value->TryGetString(Text))
  65: 			{
  66: 				FLinearColor Color;
  67: 				if (ParseHex(Text, Color))
  68: 				{
  69: 					Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), Color);
  70: 				}
  71: 			}
  72: 		}
  73: 	}
  74: 
  75: 	FVector ReadVector3(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, const FVector& Default)
  76: 	{
  77: 		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Arr = nullptr;
  78: 		if (!Obj.IsValid() || !Obj->TryGetArrayField(Key, Arr) || !Arr || Arr->Num() != 3)
  79: 		{
  80: 			return Default;
  81: 		}
  82: 		return FVector((*Arr)[0]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[1]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[2]->AsNumber());
  83: 	}
  84: 
  85: 	double ReadNumber(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, double Default)
  86: 	{
  87: 		double Value = Default;
  88: 		if (Obj.IsValid())
  89: 		{
  90: 			Obj->TryGetNumberField(Key, Value);
  91: 		}
  92: 		return Value;
  93: 	}
  94: 
  95: 	bool ReadBool(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, bool Default)
  96: 	{
  97: 		bool Value = Default;
  98: 		if (Obj.IsValid())
  99: 		{
 100: 			Obj->TryGetBoolField(Key, Value);
 101: 		}
 102: 		return Value;
 103: 	}
 104: 
 105: 	FString ReadString(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, const FString& Default)
 106: 	{
 107: 		FString Value = Default;
 108: 		if (Obj.IsValid())
 109: 		{
 110: 			Obj->TryGetStringField(Key, Value);
 111: 		}
 112: 		return Value;
 113: 	}
 114: 
 115: 	void ReadRamps(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Pixel, TArray<TArray<FColor>>& Out)
 116: 	{
 117: 		Out.Reset();
 118: 		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Ramps = nullptr;
 119: 		if (!Pixel.IsValid() || !Pixel->TryGetArrayField(TEXT("Ramps"), Ramps) || !Ramps)
 120: 		{
 121: 			return;
 122: 		}
 123: 		for (const TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>& Ramp : *Ramps)
 124: 		{
 125: 			const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Colors = nullptr;
 126: 			if (!Ramp->TryGetArray(Colors) || !Colors)
 127: 			{
 128: 				continue;
 129: 			}
 130: 			TArray<FColor> RampColors;
 131: 			for (const TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>& Entry : *Colors)
 132: 			{
 133: 				FString Text;
 134: 				if (Entry->TryGetString(Text))
 135: 				{
 136: 					FColor Srgb;
 137: 					if (GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(Text, Srgb))
 138: 					{
 139: 						RampColors.Add(Srgb);
 140: 					}
 141: 				}
 142: 			}
 143: 			if (RampColors.Num() > 0)
 144: 			{
 145: 				Out.Add(MoveTemp(RampColors));
 146: 			}
 147: 		}
 148: 	}
 149: }
 150: 
 151: TArray<FString> FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::ListJobFiles()
 152: {
 153: 	TArray<FString> Files;
 154: 	const FString Dir = FPaths::ProjectPluginsDir() / TEXT("GBPixelVfx/Bake/Jobs");
 155: 	IFileManager::Get().FindFiles(Files, *(Dir / TEXT("*.json")), true, false);
 156: 	Files.Sort();
 157: 	for (FString& File : Files)
 158: 	{
 159: 		File = Dir / File;
 160: 	}
 161: 	return Files;
 162: }
 163: 
 164: bool FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::LoadFromFile(const FString& Path)
 165: {
 166: 	FString Text;
 167: 	if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToString(Text, *Path))
 168: 	{
 169: 		return false;
 170: 	}
 171: 	TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Root;
 172: 	const TSharedRef<TJsonReader<>> Reader = TJsonReaderFactory<>::Create(Text);
 173: 	if (!FJsonSerializer::Deserialize(Reader, Root) || !Root.IsValid())
 174: 	{
 175: 		return false;
 176: 	}
 177: 
 178: 	FGBPixelVfxBakeJob Fresh;
 179: 	FString IdText;
 180: 	if (!Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("Id"), IdText) || IdText.IsEmpty())
 181: 	{
 182: 		return false;
 183: 	}
 184: 	Fresh.Id = FName(*IdText);
 185: 	Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("System"), Fresh.SystemPath);
 186: 
 187: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>* Sub = nullptr;
 188: 	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserFloats"), Sub) && Sub)
 189: 	{
 190: 		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadFloats(*Sub, Fresh.UserFloats);
 191: 	}
 192: 	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserVectors"), Sub) && Sub)
 193: 	{
 194: 		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadVectors(*Sub, Fresh.UserVectors);
 195: 	}
 196: 	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserColors"), Sub) && Sub)
 197: 	{
 198: 		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadColors(*Sub, Fresh.UserColors);
 199: 	}
 200: 
 201: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Capture = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("Capture"));
 202: 	Fresh.Resolution = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("Resolution"), Fresh.Resolution));
 203: 	Fresh.bAutoFrame = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Capture, TEXT("AutoFrame"), Fresh.bAutoFrame);
 204: 	Fresh.AutoFramePadding = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("AutoFramePadding"), Fresh.AutoFramePadding));
 205: 	Fresh.WidthCm = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("WidthCm"), Fresh.WidthCm));
 206: 	Fresh.CenterCm = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadVector3(Capture, TEXT("CenterCm"), Fresh.CenterCm);
 207: 	Fresh.ViewYawDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("ViewYawDeg"), Fresh.ViewYawDeg));
 208: 	Fresh.ViewPitchDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("ViewPitchDeg"), Fresh.ViewPitchDeg));
 209: 	Fresh.Projection = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Capture, TEXT("Projection"), Fresh.Projection);
 210: 	Fresh.FovDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("FovDeg"), Fresh.FovDeg));
 211: 	Fresh.WarmupSeconds = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("WarmupSeconds"), Fresh.WarmupSeconds));
 212: 	Fresh.DurationSeconds = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("DurationSeconds"), Fresh.DurationSeconds));
 213: 	Fresh.SimFps = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("SimFps"), Fresh.SimFps));
 214: 	Fresh.Exposure = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("Exposure"), Fresh.Exposure));
 215: 	Fresh.bKeyLight = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Capture, TEXT("KeyLight"), Fresh.bKeyLight);
 216: 	Fresh.KeyLightIntensity = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("KeyLightIntensity"), Fresh.KeyLightIntensity));
 217:

 220: 	Fresh.FrameHeight = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("FrameHeight"), Fresh.FrameHeight));

 222: 	Fresh.bLoop = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Pixel, TEXT("Loop"), Fresh.bLoop);
 223: 	Fresh.LoopBlendFrames = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("LoopBlendFrames"), Fresh.LoopBlendFrames));
 224: 	Fresh.AlphaMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("AlphaMode"), Fresh.AlphaMode);
 225: 	Fresh.AlphaThreshold = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("AlphaThreshold"), Fresh.AlphaThreshold));
 226: 	Fresh.ColorMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("ColorMode"), Fresh.ColorMode);
 227: 	Fresh.HotLuminance = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("HotLuminance"), Fresh.HotLuminance));
 228: 	Fresh.HotSaturation = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("HotSaturation"), Fresh.HotSaturation));
 229: 	GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadRamps(Pixel, Fresh.Ramps);
 230: 	Fresh.LevelsPercentile = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("LevelsPercentile"), Fresh.LevelsPercentile));
 231: 	Fresh.Gamma = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("Gamma"), Fresh.Gamma));
 232: 	Fresh.Hysteresis = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("Hysteresis"), Fresh.Hysteresis));
 233: 	Fresh.MinIslandPixels = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("MinIslandPixels"), Fresh.MinIslandPixels));
 234: 	Fresh.bFillHoles = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Pixel, TEXT("FillHoles"), Fresh.bFillHoles);
 235: 	Fresh.Outline = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("Outline"), Fresh.Outline);
 236: 
 237: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Light = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("Light"));
 238: 	Fresh.bLightEnabled = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Light, TEXT("Enabled"), Fresh.bLightEnabled);
 239: 	const FString LightHex = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Light, TEXT("Color"), Fresh.LightColorHex);
 240: 	FLinearColor ParsedColor;
 241: 	if (GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ParseHex(LightHex, ParsedColor))
 242: 	{
 243: 		Fresh.LightColorHex = LightHex;
 244: 		Fresh.LightColor = ParsedColor;
 245: 	}
 246: 	Fresh.LightIntensity = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Light, TEXT("Intensity"), Fresh.LightIntensity));
 247: 	Fresh.LightRadiusCm = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Light, TEXT("RadiusCm"), Fresh.LightRadiusCm));
 248: 	Fresh.bLightCastShadows = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Light, TEXT("CastShadows"), Fresh.bLightCastShadows);
 249: 	Fresh.LightTrackMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Light, TEXT("TrackMode"), Fresh.LightTrackMode);
 250: 
 251: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> World = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("World"));
 252: 	Fresh.Facing = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(World, TEXT("Facing"), Fresh.Facing);
 253: 	Fresh.Glow = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(World, TEXT("Glow"), Fresh.Glow));
 254: 	Fresh.bLit = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(World, TEXT("Lit"), Fresh.bLit);
 255: 
 256: 	*this = Fresh;
 257: 	return true;
 258: }

  40: 	bool EnsureDir(const FString& Dir)
  41: 	{
  42: 		IPlatformFile& Files = FPlatformFileManager::Get().GetPlatformFile();
  43: 		return Files.CreateDirectoryTree(*Dir);
  44: 	}
  45: 
  46: 	// Bilinear resample of a linear HDR frame to the review cell size, tone-mapped x/(1+x).
  47: 	void BlitSourceCell(const FLinearColor* Src, int32 SrcW, int32 SrcH, FColor* Dst, int32 DstW, int32 DstH)
  48: 	{
  49: 		for (int32 y = 0; y < DstH; ++y)
  50: 		{
  51: 			const float SrcY = (static_cast<float>(y) + 0.5f) * SrcH / DstH - 0.5f;
  52: 			const int32 Y0 = FMath::Clamp(FMath::FloorToInt(SrcY), 0, SrcH - 1);
  53: 			const int32 Y1 = FMath::Min(Y0 + 1, SrcH - 1);
  54: 			const float TY = FMath::Clamp(SrcY - Y0, 0.f, 1.f);
  55: 			for (int32 x = 0; x < DstW; ++x)
  56: 			{
  57: 				const float SrcX = (static_cast<float>(x) + 0.5f) * SrcW / DstW - 0.5f;
  58: 				const int32 X0 = FMath::Clamp(FMath::FloorToInt(SrcX), 0, SrcW - 1);
  59: 				const int32 X1 = FMath::Min(X0 + 1, SrcW - 1);
  60: 				const float TX = FMath::Clamp(SrcX - X0, 0.f, 1.f);
  61: 				const FLinearColor A = Src[Y0 * SrcW + X0] * (1.f - TX) + Src[Y0 * SrcW + X1] * TX;
  62: 				const FLinearColor B = Src[Y1 * SrcW + X0] * (1.f - TX) + Src[Y1 * SrcW + X1] * TX;
  63: 				const FLinearColor C = A * (1.f - TY) + B * TY;
  64: 				Dst[y * DstW + x] = FColor(
  65: 					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.R / (1.f + C.R), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
  66: 					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.G / (1.f + C.G), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
  67: 					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.B / (1.f + C.B), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
  68: 					255);
  69: 			}
  70: 		}
  71: 	}
  72: 
  73: 	// Nearest upscale of one baked frame.
  74: 	void BlitPixelCell(const FColor* Src, int32 SrcW, int32 SrcH, FColor* Dst, int32 DstW, int32 DstH)
  75: 	{
  76: 		for (int32 y = 0; y < DstH; ++y)
  77: 		{
  78: 			const int32 SY = FMath::Min(y * SrcH / DstH, SrcH - 1);
  79: 			for (int32 x = 0; x < DstW; ++x)
  80: 			{
  81: 				const int32 SX = FMath::Min(x * SrcW / DstW, SrcW - 1);
  82: 				Dst[y * DstW + x] = Src[SY * SrcW + SX];
  83: 			}
  84: 		}
  85: 	}
  86: 
  87: 	bool SavePng(const FString& Path, int32 W, int32 H, const TArray<FColor>& Pixels)
  88: 	{
  89: 		if (Pixels.Num() != W * H)
  90: 		{
  91: 			return false;
  92: 		}
  93: 		TArray64<uint8> Compressed;
  94: 		FImageUtils::PNGCompressImageArray(W, H, TArrayView64<const FColor>(Pixels.GetData(), Pixels.Num()), Compressed);
  95: 		if (Compressed.Num() == 0)
  96: 		{
  97: 			return false;
  98: 		}
  99: 		TArray<uint8> Bytes;
 100: 		Bytes.Append(Compressed.GetData(), static_cast<int32>(FMath::Min<int64>(Compressed.Num(), MAX_int32)));
 101: 		return FFileHelper::SaveArrayToFile(Bytes, *Path);
 102: 	}

 105: AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode()
 106: {
 107: 	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
 108: 	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
 109: 	DefaultPawnClass = nullptr;
 110: 	HUDClass = nullptr;

 112: 
 113: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::ParseCommandLine()
 114: {
 115: 	FString BakeArg;
 116: 	FString CompareArg;
 117: 	if (FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("-PvfxBake="), BakeArg))
 118: 	{
 119: 		RunMode = ERunMode::Bake;
 120: 		RunArg = BakeArg;
 121: 	}
 122: 	else if (FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("-PvfxCompare="), CompareArg))
 123: 	{
 124: 		RunMode = ERunMode::Compare;
 125: 		RunArg = CompareArg;
 126: 	}
 127: 	else if (FString(FCommandLine::Get()).Contains(TEXT("-PvfxShowcase")))
 128: 	{
 129: 		RunMode = ERunMode::Showcase;
 130: 	}
 131: }
 132: 
 133: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BeginPlay()
 134: {
 135: 	Super::BeginPlay();
 136: 	ParseCommandLine();
 137: 	if (RunMode == ERunMode::None)
 138: 	{
 139: 		return;
 140: 	}
 141: 	if (RunMode == ERunMode::Bake)
 142: 	{
 143: 		BakeJobFiles.Reset();
 144: 		for (const FString& File : FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::ListJobFiles())
 145: 		{
 146: 			if (RunArg.Equals(TEXT("All"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)
 147: 				|| FPaths::GetBaseFilename(File).Equals(RunArg, ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 148: 			{
 149: 				BakeJobFiles.Add(File);
 150: 			}
 151: 		}
 152: 		if (BakeJobFiles.Num() == 0)
 153: 		{
 154: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX no bake job matches '%s'."), *RunArg);
 155: 			FinishRun();
 156: 		}
 157: 	}
 158: }
 159: 
 160: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
 161: {
 162: 	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
 163: 	switch (RunMode)
 164: 	{
 165: 	case ERunMode::Bake: TickBake(); break;
 166: 	case ERunMode::Showcase: TickShowcase(); break;
 167: 	case ERunMode::Compare: TickCompare(); break;
 168: 	default: break;
 169: 	}
 170: }
 171: 
 172: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::FinishRun()
 173: {
 174: 	UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX DONE"));
 175: 	RunMode = ERunMode::None;
 176: 	FPlatformMisc::RequestExit(false);
 177: }
