 180: 
 181: FVector AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeCameraDirection() const
 182: {
 183: 	const float Yaw = FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.ViewYawDeg);
 184: 	const float Pitch = FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.ViewPitchDeg);
 185: 	return FVector(FMath::Cos(Pitch) * FMath::Cos(Yaw), FMath::Cos(Pitch) * FMath::Sin(Yaw),
 186: 		FMath::Sin(Pitch));
 187: }
 188: 
 189: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeDestroyRig()
 190: {
 191: 	if (NiagaraActor)
 192: 	{
 193: 		NiagaraActor->Destroy();
 194: 	}
 195: 	NiagaraActor = nullptr;
 196: 	NiagaraComp = nullptr;
 197: 	NiagaraSystem = nullptr;
 198: 	if (CaptureComp)
 199: 	{
 200: 		if (AActor* CaptureActor = CaptureComp->GetOwner())
 201: 		{
 202: 			CaptureActor->Destroy();
 203: 		}
 204: 	}
 205: 	CaptureComp = nullptr;
 206: 	CaptureRT = nullptr;
 207: 	if (ViewCam)
 208: 	{
 209: 		ViewCam->Destroy();
 210: 	}
 211: 	ViewCam = nullptr;
 212: 	if (KeyLight)
 213: 	{
 214: 		KeyLight->Destroy();
 215: 	}
 216: 	KeyLight = nullptr;
 217: }
 218: 
 219: bool AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeSpawnRig(int32 RTWidth)
 220: {
 221: 	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
 222: 	if (!World)
 223: 	{
 224: 		return false;
 225: 	}
 226: 	NiagaraSystem = LoadObject<UNiagaraSystem>(nullptr, *Job.SystemPath);
 227: 	if (!NiagaraSystem)
 228: 	{
 229: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: cannot load Niagara system '%s' (is NiagaraFluids on the command line?)."),
 230: 			*Job.Id.ToString(), *Job.SystemPath);
 231: 		return false;
 232: 	}
 233: 	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
 234: 	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
 235: 	NiagaraActor = World->SpawnActor<AActor>(AActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
 236: 	if (!NiagaraActor)
 237: 	{
 238: 		return false;
 239: 	}
 240: 	NiagaraComp = NewObject<UNiagaraComponent>(NiagaraActor, TEXT("Niagara"));
 241: 	NiagaraActor->SetRootComponent(NiagaraComp);
 242: 	NiagaraComp->RegisterComponent();
 243: 	NiagaraComp->SetAsset(NiagaraSystem);
 244: 	NiagaraComp->SetRandomSeedOffset(0);
 245: 	NiagaraComp->SetAllowScalability(false);
 246: 	NiagaraComp->bAutoActivate = false;
 247: 	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserFloats)
 248: 	{
 249: 		NiagaraComp->SetVariableFloat(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
 250: 	}
 251: 	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserVectors)
 252: 	{
 253: 		NiagaraComp->SetVariableVec3(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
 254: 	}
 255: 	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserColors)
 256: 	{
 257: 		NiagaraComp->SetVariableLinearColor(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
 258: 	}
 259: 	NiagaraComp->Activate(true);
 260: 
 261: 	const FVector Dir = BakeCameraDirection();
 262: 	const FRotator CamRot = FRotationMatrix::MakeFromX(Dir).Rotator();
 263: 	const FVector RightV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
 264: 	const FVector UpV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
 265: 	const bool bOrtho = Job.Projection.Equals(TEXT("Ortho"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
 266: 	const int32 RTW = FMath::Max(16, RTWidth);
 267: 	const int32 RTH = FMath::Max(16, FMath::RoundToInt(RTW * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth))));
 268: 
 269: 	AActor* CaptureActor = World->SpawnActor<AActor>(AActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
 270: 	CaptureComp = NewObject<USceneCaptureComponent2D>(CaptureActor, TEXT("PvfxCapture"));
 271: 	CaptureActor->SetRootComponent(CaptureComp);
 272: 	CaptureComp->RegisterComponent();
 273: 	CaptureComp->PrimitiveRenderMode = ESceneCapturePrimitiveRenderMode::PRM_UseShowOnlyList;
 274: 	CaptureComp->CaptureSource = ESceneCaptureSource::SCS_SceneColorHDR;
 275: 	if (!bLegacyCapture)
 276: 	{
 277: 		CaptureComp->ShowOnlyComponent(NiagaraComp);
 278: 	}
 279: 	CaptureComp->bCaptureEveryFrame = false;
 280: 	CaptureComp->bCaptureOnMovement = false;
 281: 	CaptureComp->bAlwaysPersistRenderingState = true;
 282: 	CaptureComp->bInheritMainViewCameraPostProcessSettings = false;
 283: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetBloom(false);
 284: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetEyeAdaptation(false);
 285: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetMotionBlur(false);
 286: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetTonemapper(false);
 287: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetToneCurve(false);
 288: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetTemporalAA(false);
 289: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetGrain(false);
 290: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetVignette(false);
 291: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetCameraImperfections(false);
 292: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetLensFlares(false);
 293: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetDepthOfField(false);
 294: 	if (bOrtho)
 295: 	{
 296: 		CaptureComp->ProjectionType = ECameraProjectionMode::Orthographic;
 297: 		CaptureComp->OrthoWidth = FramingWidthCm;
 298: 		CaptureActor->SetActorLocation(FramingCenterCm - Dir * 2000.f);
 299: 	}
 300: 	else
 301: 	{
 302: 		CaptureComp->ProjectionType = ECameraProjectionMode::Perspective;
 303: 		CaptureComp->FOVAngle = Job.FovDeg;
 304: 		const float Dist = (FramingWidthCm * 0.5f) / FMath::Tan(FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.FovDeg * 0.5f));
 305: 		CaptureActor->SetActorLocation(FramingCenterCm - Dir * Dist);
 306: 	}
 307: 	CaptureActor->SetActorRotation(CamRot);
 308: 	CaptureRT = NewObject<UTextureRenderTarget2D>(GetTransientPackage(), NAME_None, RF_Transient);
 309: 	CaptureRT->InitCustomFormat(RTW, RTH, PF_FloatRGBA, true);
 310: 	CaptureRT->UpdateResourceImmediate(true);
 311: 	CaptureComp->TextureTarget = CaptureRT;
 312: 
 313: 	if (Job.bKeyLight)
 314: 	{
 315: 		KeyLight = World->SpawnActor<ADirectionalLight>(ADirectionalLight::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
 316: 		// Style guide: light from the top left as seen by the capture camera.
 317: 		const FVector Travel = (Dir - UpV + RightV * 0.8f).GetSafeNormal();
 318: 		if (UDirectionalLightComponent* LightComp = Cast<UDirectionalLightComponent>(KeyLight->GetLightComponent()))
 319: 		{
 320: 			LightComp->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
 321: 			LightComp->SetIntensity(Job.KeyLightIntensity);
 322: 			LightComp->SetLightColor(FLinearColor::White);
 323: 		}
 324: 		KeyLight->SetActorRotation(Travel.Rotation());
 325: 	}
 326: 
 327: 	// The main view must render every frame and must look at the effect (GPU sims do not
 328: 	// advance in scene captures): park a camera actor on the capture and make it the view target.
 329: 	ViewCam = World->SpawnActor<ACameraActor>(ACameraActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
 330: 	ViewCam->SetActorLocation(CaptureActor->GetActorLocation());
 331: 	ViewCam->SetActorRotation(CamRot);
 332: 	if (bOrtho)
 333: 	{
 334: 		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->ProjectionMode = ECameraProjectionMode::Orthographic;
 335: 		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->OrthoWidth = FramingWidthCm;
 336: 	}
 337: 	else
 338: 	{
 339: 		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->ProjectionMode = ECameraProjectionMode::Perspective;
 340: 		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->FieldOfView = Job.FovDeg;
 341: 	}
 342: 	if (APlayerController* PC = EnsurePlayerController())
 343: 	{
 344: 		PC->SetViewTarget(ViewCam);
 345: 	}
 346: 	else
 347: 	{
 348: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: no player controller, the main view may not advance the sim."),
 349: 			*Job.Id.ToString());
 350: 	}
 351: 	return true;
 352: }
 353: 
 354: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeFillCaptureTicks(bool bPrepassNow)
 355: {
 356: 	CaptureAtTicks.Reset();
 357: 	CaptureCursor = 0;
 358: 	const int32 WarmupFrames = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(Job.WarmupSeconds * Job.SimFps));
 359: 	if (bPrepassNow)
 360: 	{
 361: 		// The framing union covers the warmed-up window only, like the real pass: the
 362: 		// transient rise from t = 0 would inflate the box of a looping clip.
 363: 		const int32 TotalFrames = WarmupFrames + FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(Job.DurationSeconds * Job.SimFps));
 364: 		for (int32 Tick = WarmupFrames; Tick < TotalFrames; Tick += 4)
 365: 		{
 366: 			CaptureAtTicks.Add(Tick);
 367: 		}
 368: 		CaptureTotalFrames = CaptureAtTicks.Num();
 369: 	}
 370: 	else
 371: 	{
 372: 		const int32 Blend = (Job.bLoop && Job.LoopBlendFrames > 0) ? Job.LoopBlendFrames : 0;
 373: 		const int32 Total = Job.FrameCount() + Blend;
 374: 		CaptureStride = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(static_cast<float>(Job.SimFps) / FMath::Max(1, Job.Fps)));
 375: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < Total; ++i)
 376: 		{
 377: 			CaptureAtTicks.Add(WarmupFrames + i * CaptureStride);
 378: 		}
 379: 		CaptureTotalFrames = Total;
 380: 	}
 381: }
 382: 
 383: bool AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeCaptureOne()
 384: {
 385: 	if (!CaptureComp || !CaptureRT)
 386: 	{
 387: 		return false;
 388: 	}
 389: 	CaptureComp->CaptureScene();
 390: 	FTextureRenderTargetResource* Res = CaptureRT->GameThread_GetRenderTargetResource();
 391: 	if (!Res)
 392: 	{
 393: 		return false;
 394: 	}
 395: 	TArray<FLinearColor> Raw;
 396: 	if (!Res->ReadLinearColorPixels(Raw) || Raw.Num() != CaptureRT->SizeX * CaptureRT->SizeY)
 397: 	{
 398: 		return false;
 399: 	}
 400: 	FSimFrame Frame;
 401: 	Frame.Width = CaptureRT->SizeX;
 402: 	Frame.Height = CaptureRT->SizeY;
 403: 	Frame.Color.SetNum(Raw.Num());
 404: 	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(Raw.Num());
 405: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Raw.Num(); ++i)
 406: 	{
 407: 		Frame.Color[i] = Raw[i];
 408: 		Frame.Opacity[i] = GBPixelVfxBakeLocal::Saturate(1.f - Raw[i].A);
 409: 	}
 410: 	SimFrames.Add(MoveTemp(Frame));
 411: 	return true;
 412: }
 413: 
 414: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeAnalysePrepass()
 415: {
 416: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxBakeLocal;
 417: 	const float TestLevel = Job.AlphaThreshold * 0.5f;
 418: 	// The visibility test uses the job's alpha rule: additive effects (fire) stay near A = 1
 419: 	// in the capture, so opacity alone would call them invisible.
 420: 	const bool bLuminance = Job.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Luminance"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
 421: 	const bool bMax = !bLuminance && !Job.AlphaMode.Equals(TEXT("Opacity"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
 422: 	const int32 RTW = SimFrames.Num() > 0 ? SimFrames[0].Width : 0;
 423: 	const int32 RTH = SimFrames.Num() > 0 ? SimFrames[0].Height : 0;
 424: 	int32 MinX = RTW;
 425: 	int32 MaxX = -1;
 426: 	int32 MinY = RTH;
 427: 	int32 MaxY = -1;
 428: 	float MaxOpacity = 0.f;
 429: 	float MaxLuminance = 0.f;
 430: 	for (const FSimFrame& Frame : SimFrames)
 431: 	{
 432: 		for (int32 y = 0; y < Frame.Height; ++y)
 433: 		{
 434: 			for (int32 x = 0; x < Frame.Width; ++x)
 435: 			{
 436: 				const int32 At = y * Frame.Width + x;
 437: 				const float Opacity = Frame.Opacity[At];
 438: 				const FLinearColor& RGB = Frame.Color[At];
 439: 				const float Lum = RGB.R * 0.2126f + RGB.G * 0.7152f + RGB.B * 0.0722f;
 440: 				MaxOpacity = FMath::Max(MaxOpacity, Opacity);
 441: 				MaxLuminance = FMath::Max(MaxLuminance, Lum);
 442: 				float A = Opacity;
 443: 				if (bLuminance)
 444: 				{
 445: 					A = Saturate(Lum);
 446: 				}
 447: 				else if (bMax)
 448: 				{
 449: 					A = FMath::Max(Opacity, Saturate(Lum));
 450: 				}
 451: 				if (A > TestLevel)
 452: 				{
 453: 					MinX = FMath::Min(MinX, x);
 454: 					MaxX = FMath::Max(MaxX, x);
 455: 					MinY = FMath::Min(MinY, y);
 456: 					MaxY = FMath::Max(MaxY, y);
 457: 				}
 458: 			}
 459: 		}
 460: 	}
 461: 	if (MaxX < MinX)
 462: 	{
 463: 		if (!bLegacyCapture)
 464: 		{
 465: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: nothing visible with the show-only list (max opacity %.2f, max luminance %.2f), retrying with the full scene."),
 466: 				*Job.Id.ToString(), MaxOpacity, MaxLuminance);
 467: 			bLegacyCapture = true;
 468: 			BakeDestroyRig();
 469: 			BakeSpawnRig(256);
 470: 			BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
 471: 			StepTick = 0;
 472: 			return;
 473: 		}
 474: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: nothing visible (max opacity %.2f, max luminance %.2f), failing the job."),
 475: 			*Job.Id.ToString(), MaxOpacity, MaxLuminance);
 476: 		BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
 477: 		return;
 478: 	}
 479: 	const bool bTouchesEdge = (MinX <= 1 || MinY <= 1 || MaxX >= RTW - 2 || MaxY >= RTH - 2);
 480: 	if (bTouchesEdge && !bRetriedWide)
 481: 	{
 482: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: the first framing guess is too tight, doubling the width once and retrying."),
 483: 			*Job.Id.ToString());
 484: 		bRetriedWide = true;
 485: 		FramingWidthCm *= 2.f;
 486: 		BakeDestroyRig();
 487: 		BakeSpawnRig(256);
 488: 		BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
 489: 		StepTick = 0;
 490: 		return;
 491: 	}
 492: 	if (bTouchesEdge)
 493: 	{
 494: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: the effect still touches the capture edge after doubling."), *Job.Id.ToString());
 495: 	}
 496: 	// Pixels to world offsets on the capture plane (center-plane approximation for perspective).
 497: 	const FRotator CamRot = CaptureComp->GetComponentRotation();
 498: 	const FVector RightV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
 499: 	const FVector UpV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
 500: 	const float FramingHeightCm = FramingWidthCm * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth));
 501: 	const FVector OldCenter = FramingCenterCm;
 502: 	float MinA = FLT_MAX;
 503: 	float MaxA = -FLT_MAX;
 504: 	float MinB = FLT_MAX;
 505: 	float MaxB = -FLT_MAX;
 506: 	for (int32 Corner = 0; Corner < 4; ++Corner)
 507: 	{
 508: 		const int32 PX = (Corner % 2 == 0) ? MinX : MaxX;
 509: 		const int32 PY = (Corner < 2) ? MinY : MaxY;
 510: 		const float A = ((static_cast<float>(PX) + 0.5f) / RTW - 0.5f) * FramingWidthCm;
 511: 		const float B = (0.5f - (static_cast<float>(PY) + 0.5f) / RTH) * FramingHeightCm;
 512: 		MinA = FMath::Min(MinA, A);
 513: 		MaxA = FMath::Max(MaxA, A);
 514: 		MinB = FMath::Min(MinB, B);
 515: 		MaxB = FMath::Max(MaxB, B);
 516: 	}
 517: 	float BoxW = (MaxA - MinA) * (1.f + 2.f * Job.AutoFramePadding);
 518: 	float BoxH = (MaxB - MinB) * (1.f + 2.f * Job.AutoFramePadding);
 519: 	// Keep the frame aspect: grow the short side.
 520: 	const float Aspect = Job.FrameWidth / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameHeight));
 521: 	if (BoxW / FMath::Max(1.f, BoxH) < Aspect)
 522: 	{
 523: 		BoxW = BoxH * Aspect;
 524: 	}
 525: 	else
 526: 	{
 527: 		BoxH = BoxW / Aspect;
 528: 	}
 529: 	FramingCenterCm = OldCenter + RightV * ((MinA + MaxA) * 0.5f) + UpV * ((MinB + MaxB) * 0.5f);
 530: 	FramingWidthCm = BoxW;
 531: 	UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX %s: auto-frame %.0f cm at (%.0f, %.0f, %.0f)."),
 532: 		*Job.Id.ToString(), FramingWidthCm, FramingCenterCm.X, FramingCenterCm.Y, FramingCenterCm.Z);
 533: 	// Real pass with the new framing: destroy and re-spawn the system.
 534: 	BakeDestroyRig();
 535: 	bRespawned = true;
 536: 	BakeSpawnRig(Job.Resolution);
 537: 	BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
 538: 	StepTick = 0;
 539: }
 540: 
 541: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeConvertAndWrite()
 542: {
 543: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxBakeLocal;
 544: 	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
 545: 
 546: 	FSettings Settings;
 547: 	Settings.FrameWidth = Job.FrameWidth;
 548: 	Settings.FrameHeight = Job.FrameHeight;
 549: 	Settings.Fps = Job.Fps;
 550: 	Settings.bLoop = Job.bLoop;
 551: 	Settings.LoopBlendFrames = Job.LoopBlendFrames;
 552: 	Settings.AlphaMode = Job.AlphaMode;
 553: 	Settings.AlphaThreshold = Job.AlphaThreshold;
 554: 	Settings.ColorMode = Job.ColorMode;
 555: 	Settings.HotLuminance = Job.HotLuminance;
 556: 	Settings.HotSaturation = Job.HotSaturation;
 557: 	Settings.Ramps = Job.Ramps;
 558: 	Settings.LevelsPercentile = Job.LevelsPercentile;
 559: 	Settings.Gamma = Job.Gamma;
 560: 	Settings.Hysteresis = Job.Hysteresis;
 561: 	Settings.MinIslandPixels = Job.MinIslandPixels;
 562: 	Settings.bFillHoles = Job.bFillHoles;
 563: 	Settings.Outline = Job.Outline;
 564: 	Settings.bLightEnabled = Job.bLightEnabled;
 565: 
 566: 	if (Settings.Ramps.Num() == 0)
 567: 	{
 568: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: the job has no palette ramps."), *Job.Id.ToString());
 569: 		BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
 570: 		return;
 571: 	}
 572: 	TArray<FWorkFrame> Frames;
 573: 	Frames.Reserve(SimFrames.Num());
 574: 	for (const FSimFrame& Captured : SimFrames)
 575: 	{
 576: 		FWorkFrame Frame;
 577: 		Frame.Width = Captured.Width;
 578: 		Frame.Height = Captured.Height;
 579: 		Frame.Color = Captured.Color;
 580: 		Frame.Opacity = Captured.Opacity;
 581: 		Frames.Add(MoveTemp(Frame));
 582: 	}
 583: 	ApplyExposure(Frames, Job.Exposure);
 584: 	ApplyLoopBlend(Frames, Job.FrameCount(), Settings.LoopBlendFrames, Settings.bLoop);
 585: 	TArray<FWorkFrame> Small;
 586: 	Small.Reserve(Frames.Num());
 587: 	for (const FWorkFrame& Frame : Frames)
 588: 	{
 589: 		Small.Add(DownsampleFrame(Frame, Settings.FrameWidth, Settings.FrameHeight));
 590: 	}
 591: 	float Peak = 1.f;
 592: 	float HotPeak = 1.f;
 593: 	float ColdPeak = 1.f;
 594: 	ComputePeaks(Small, Settings, Peak, HotPeak, ColdPeak);
 595: 	TArray<FFrameMap> Maps;
 596: 	Maps.Reserve(Small.Num());
 597: 	for (const FWorkFrame& Frame : Small)
 598: 	{
 599: 		Maps.Add(MapFrame(Frame, Settings, Peak, HotPeak, ColdPeak));
 600: 		if (Maps.Num() > 1)
 601: 		{
 602: 			ApplyHysteresis(Maps.Last(), Maps[Maps.Num() - 2], Settings);
 603: 		}
 604: 	}
 605: 	if (Settings.bLoop && Maps.Num() > 1)
 606: 	{
 607: 		ApplyHysteresis(Maps[0], Maps.Last(), Settings);
 608: 	}
 609: 	for (FFrameMap& Map : Maps)
 610: 	{
 611: 		RemoveSmallIslands(Map, Settings.MinIslandPixels);
 612: 		if (Settings.bFillHoles)
 613: 		{
 614: 			FillOnePixelHoles(Map);
 615: 		}
 616: 	}
 617: 	TArray<float> Track;
 618: 	float AnchorU = 0.5f;
 619: 	float AnchorV = 0.6f;
 620: 	ComputeLightTrack(Maps, Settings, Track, AnchorU, AnchorV);
 621: 	TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
 622: 	ResolveFrames(Maps, Settings, Pixels);
 623: 
 624: 	// Pivot: project the system origin on the capture plane.
 625: 	const FRotator CamRot = CaptureComp->GetComponentRotation();
 626: 	const FVector RightV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
 627: 	const FVector UpV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
 628: 	const float FramingHeightCm = FramingWidthCm * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth));
 629: 	const FVector Rel = FVector::ZeroVector - FramingCenterCm;
 630: 	const float PivotU = 0.5f + FVector::DotProduct(Rel, RightV) / FMath::Max(1.f, FramingWidthCm);
 631: 	const float PivotV = 0.5f - FVector::DotProduct(Rel, UpV) / FMath::Max(1.f, FramingHeightCm);
 632: 
 633: 	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
 634: 	Clip.Id = Job.Id;
 635: 	Clip.AtlasFile = Job.Id.ToString() + TEXT(".png");
 636: 	Clip.FrameWidth = Job.FrameWidth;
 637: 	Clip.FrameHeight = Job.FrameHeight;
 638: 	int32 Columns = FMath::Max(1, FMath::CeilToInt(FMath::Sqrt(static_cast<float>(Maps.Num()))));
 639: 	while (Columns > 1 && Columns * Job.FrameWidth > 1024)
 640: 	{
 641: 		--Columns;
 642: 	}
 643: 	Clip.Columns = Columns;
 644: 	Clip.FrameCount = Maps.Num();
 645: 	Clip.Fps = Job.Fps;
 646: 	Clip.bLoop = Job.bLoop;
 647: 	Clip.WorldWidthCm = FramingWidthCm;
 648: 	Clip.WorldHeightCm = FramingHeightCm;
 649: 	Clip.PivotU = FMath::Clamp(PivotU, 0.f, 1.f);
 650: 	Clip.PivotV = FMath::Clamp(PivotV, 0.f, 1.f);
 651: 	if (Job.Facing.Equals(TEXT("Billboard"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Clip.Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Billboard;
 652: 	else if (Job.Facing.Equals(TEXT("Ground"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Clip.Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground;
 653: 	else if (Job.Facing.Equals(TEXT("Fixed"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)) Clip.Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed;
 654: 	else Clip.Facing = EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright;
 655: 	Clip.Glow = Job.Glow;
 656: 	Clip.bLit = Job.bLit;
 657: 	Clip.Light.bEnabled = Job.bLightEnabled;
 658: 	Clip.Light.ColorHex = Job.LightColorHex;
 659: 	Clip.Light.Color = Job.LightColor;
 660: 	Clip.Light.Intensity = Job.LightIntensity;
 661: 	Clip.Light.RadiusCm = Job.LightRadiusCm;
 662: 	Clip.Light.bCastShadows = Job.bLightCastShadows;
 663: 	Clip.Light.TrackMode = Job.LightTrackMode;
 664: 	Clip.Light.AnchorU = AnchorU;
 665: 	Clip.Light.AnchorV = AnchorV;
 666: 	if (Job.LightTrackMode.Equals(TEXT("Constant"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 667: 	{
 668: 		Clip.Light.Track.SetNum(Maps.Num());
 669: 		for (float& Entry : Clip.Light.Track)
 670: 		{
 671: 			Entry = 1.f;
 672: 		}
 673: 	}
 674: 	else
 675: 	{
 676: 		Clip.Light.Track = Track;
 677: 	}
 678: 	Clip.SourceSystem = Job.SystemPath;
 679: 	Clip.SourceDurationSeconds = Job.DurationSeconds;
 680: 	Clip.SourceSimFps = Job.SimFps;
 681: 	Clip.EngineVersion = FEngineVersion::Current().ToString();
 682: 	Clip.BakedUtc = FDateTime::UtcNow().ToIso8601();
 683: 
 684: 	const FString DataDir = FPaths::ProjectContentDir() / TEXT("Data/Vfx");
 685: 	EnsureDir(DataDir);
 686: 	const FString PngPath = DataDir / Clip.AtlasFile;
 687: 	const FString JsonPath = DataDir / (Job.Id.ToString() + TEXT(".json"));
 688: 
 689: 	const int32 Rows = Clip.Rows();
 690: 	TArray<FColor> Atlas;
 691: 	Atlas.SetNumZeroed(Columns * Job.FrameWidth * Rows * Job.FrameHeight);
 692: 	for (int32 f = 0; f < Pixels.Num(); ++f)
 693: 	{
 694: 		const int32 Col = f % Columns;
 695: 		const int32 Row = f / Columns;
 696: 		for (int32 y = 0; y < Job.FrameHeight; ++y)
 697: 		{
 698: 			for (int32 x = 0; x < Job.FrameWidth; ++x)
 699: 			{
 700: 				Atlas[(Row * Job.FrameHeight + y) * Columns * Job.FrameWidth + Col * Job.FrameWidth + x] =
 701: 					Pixels[f][y * Job.FrameWidth + x];
 702: 			}
 703: 		}
 704: 	}
 705: 	if (!SavePng(PngPath, Columns * Job.FrameWidth, Rows * Job.FrameHeight, Atlas))
 706: 	{
 707: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: could not write %s."), *Job.Id.ToString(), *PngPath);
 708: 		BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
 709: 		return;
 710: 	}
 711: 	FString JsonText;
 712: 	if (!Clip.SaveToJsonString(JsonText) || !FFileHelper::SaveStringToFile(JsonText, *JsonPath))
 713: 	{
 714: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: could not write %s."), *Job.Id.ToString(), *JsonPath);
 715: 		BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
 716: 		return;
 717: 	}
 718: 
 719: 	// Review files.
 720: 	const FString ReviewDir = FPaths::ProjectSavedDir() / TEXT("PixelVfx") / Job.Id.ToString();
 721: 	EnsureDir(ReviewDir);
 722: 	const int32 CellW = Job.FrameWidth * 6;
 723: 	const int32 CellH = Job.FrameHeight * 6;
 724: 	const int32 Gutter = 6;
 725: 	const int32 SheetCols = 8;
 726: 	const int32 SheetRows = (Pixels.Num() + SheetCols - 1) / SheetCols;
 727: 	const int32 SheetW = SheetCols * CellW + (SheetCols + 1) * Gutter;
 728: 	const int32 SheetH = SheetRows * CellH + (SheetRows + 1) * Gutter;
 729: 	const FColor Bg(0x3A, 0x3A, 0x3A, 255);
 730: 	TArray<FColor> Sheet;
 731: 	Sheet.SetNum(SheetW * SheetH);
 732: 	for (FColor& Cell : Sheet)
 733: 	{
 734: 		Cell = Bg;
 735: 	}
 736: 	TArray<FColor> Source;
 737: 	Source.SetNum(SheetW * SheetH);
 738: 	for (FColor& Cell : Source)
 739: 	{
 740: 		Cell = FColor::Black;
 741: 	}
 742: 	TArray<FColor> CellBuf;
 743: 	CellBuf.SetNum(CellW * CellH);
 744: 	for (int32 f = 0; f < Pixels.Num(); ++f)
 745: 	{
 746: 		const int32 SX = Gutter + (f % SheetCols) * (CellW + Gutter);
 747: 		const int32 SY = Gutter + (f / SheetCols) * (CellH + Gutter);
 748: 		BlitPixelCell(Pixels[f].GetData(), Job.FrameWidth, Job.FrameHeight, CellBuf.GetData(), CellW, CellH);
 749: 		for (int32 y = 0; y < CellH; ++y)
 750: 		{
 751: 			for (int32 x = 0; x < CellW; ++x)
 752: 			{
 753: 				Sheet[(SY + y) * SheetW + SX + x] = CellBuf[y * CellW + x];
 754: 			}
 755: 		}
 756: 		// The matching source frame before conversion (post exposure, tone-mapped, smooth).
 757: 		if (SimFrames.IsValidIndex(f))
 758: 		{
 759: 			BlitSourceCell(SimFrames[f].Color.GetData(), SimFrames[f].Width, SimFrames[f].Height,
 760: 				CellBuf.GetData(), CellW, CellH);
 761: 			for (int32 y = 0; y < CellH; ++y)
 762: 			{
 763: 				for (int32 x = 0; x < CellW; ++x)
 764: 				{
 765: 					Source[(SY + y) * SheetW + SX + x] = CellBuf[y * CellW + x];
 766: 				}
 767: 			}
 768: 		}
 769: 	}
 770: 	SavePng(ReviewDir / TEXT("sheet.png"), SheetW, SheetH, Sheet);
 771: 	SavePng(ReviewDir / TEXT("source.png"), SheetW, SheetH, Source);
 772: 	int32 BigFrame = 0;
 773: 	for (int32 f = 0; f < Clip.Light.Track.Num(); ++f)
 774: 	{
 775: 		if (Clip.Light.Track[f] >= Clip.Light.Track[BigFrame])
 776: 		{
 777: 			BigFrame = f;
 778: 		}
 779: 	}
 780: 	if (Pixels.IsValidIndex(BigFrame))
 781: 	{
 782: 		const int32 BigW = Job.FrameWidth * 12;
 783: 		const int32 BigH = Job.FrameHeight * 12;
 784: 		TArray<FColor> Big;
 785: 		Big.SetNum(BigW * BigH);
 786: 		BlitPixelCell(Pixels[BigFrame].GetData(), Job.FrameWidth, Job.FrameHeight, Big.GetData(), BigW, BigH);
 787: 		SavePng(ReviewDir / TEXT("frame_big.png"), BigW, BigH, Big);
 788: 	}
 789: 
 790: 	int32 SolidTotal = 0;
 791: 	for (const FFrameMap& Map : Maps)
 792: 	{
 793: 		SolidTotal += Map.SolidCount();
 794: 	}
 795: 	const float SolidAvg = Maps.Num() > 0 && Job.FrameWidth > 0 && Job.FrameHeight > 0
 796: 		? 100.f * SolidTotal / (Maps.Num() * Job.FrameWidth * Job.FrameHeight)
 797: 		: 0.f;
 798: 	if (SolidAvg < 1.f || SolidAvg > 95.f)
 799: 	{
 800: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: average solid coverage is %.1f%% (framing or threshold may be wrong)."),
 801: 			*Job.Id.ToString(), SolidAvg);
 802: 	}
 803: 	UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display,
 804: 		TEXT("PVFX BAKE %s: frames=%d frame=%dx%d world=%.0fx%.0f cm peak=%.2f solid=%.1f%% colors=%d -> %s"),
 805: 		*Job.Id.ToString(), Maps.Num(), Job.FrameWidth, Job.FrameHeight,
 806: 		FramingWidthCm, FramingHeightCm, Peak, SolidAvg, CountDistinctColors(Pixels), *PngPath);
 807: 	BakeLogAndCleanup(true);
 808: }
 809: 
 810: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeLogAndCleanup(bool bOk)
 811: {
 812: 	if (!bOk)
 813: 	{
 814: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: job failed."), *Job.Id.ToString());
 815: 	}
 816: 	BakeDestroyRig();
 817: 	FApp::SetUseFixedTimeStep(false);
 818: 	++BakeJobIndex;
 819: 	BakeStep = EBakeStep::NextJob;
 820: 	StepTick = 0;
 821: }
 822: 
 823: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::TickBake()
 824: {
 825: 	switch (BakeStep)
 826: 	{
 827: 	case EBakeStep::StartJob:
 828: 	{
 829: 		if (BakeJobIndex >= BakeJobFiles.Num())
 830: 		{
 831: 			FinishRun();
 832: 			return;
 833: 		}
 834: 		if (!Job.LoadFromFile(BakeJobFiles[BakeJobIndex]))
 835: 		{
 836: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX cannot read job file '%s'."),
 837: 				*BakeJobFiles[BakeJobIndex]);
 838: 			++BakeJobIndex;
 839: 			return;
 840: 		}
 841: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX baking %s (%s)."), *Job.Id.ToString(), *Job.SystemPath);
 842: 		FApp::SetUseFixedTimeStep(true);
 843: 		FApp::SetFixedDeltaTime(1.0 / FMath::Max(1, Job.SimFps));
 844: 		FramingWidthCm = Job.WidthCm;
 845: 		FramingCenterCm = Job.CenterCm;
 846: 		bLegacyCapture = false;
 847: 		bRetriedWide = false;
 848: 		bRespawned = false;
 849: 		SimFrames.Reset();
 850: 		if (!BakeSpawnRig(Job.bAutoFrame ? 256 : Job.Resolution))
 851: 		{
 852: 			BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
 853: 			++BakeJobIndex;
 854: 			return;
 855: 		}
 856: 		SimTick = 0;
 857: 		BakeStep = EBakeStep::WaitReady;
 858: 		StepTick = 0;
 859: 		break;
 860: 	}
 861: 	case EBakeStep::WaitReady:
 862: 	{
 863: 		++StepTick;
 864: 		if (NiagaraSystem && NiagaraSystem->IsReadyToRun())
 865: 		{
 866: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX %s: system ready, running the sim."),
 867: 				*Job.Id.ToString());
 868: 			// Fresh pass: the prepass and the real pass each simulate from t = 0.
 869: 			SimTick = 0;
 870: 			CaptureCursor = 0;
 871: 			SimFrames.Reset();
 872: 			bRunningPrepass = Job.bAutoFrame && !bRespawned;
 873: 			BakeFillCaptureTicks(bRunningPrepass);
 874: 			BakeStep = EBakeStep::RunSim;
 875: 			StepTick = 0;
 876: 		}
 877: 		else if (StepTick == 1 || StepTick % 600 == 0)
 878: 		{
 879: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX %s: waiting for the system to compile (%d)."),
 880: 				*Job.Id.ToString(), StepTick);
 881: 		}
 882: 		else if (StepTick > 3600)
 883: 		{
 884: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: the system never became ready to run."),
 885: 				*Job.Id.ToString());
 886: 			BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
 887: 			++BakeJobIndex;
 888: 		}
 889: 		break;
 890: 	}
 891: 	case EBakeStep::RunSim:
 892: 	{
 893: 		++SimTick;
 894: 		if (CaptureCursor < CaptureAtTicks.Num() && SimTick >= CaptureAtTicks[CaptureCursor])
 895: 		{
 896: 			if (!BakeCaptureOne())
 897: 			{
 898: 				UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: capture readback failed at sim tick %d."),
 899: 					*Job.Id.ToString(), SimTick);
 900: 				BakeLogAndCleanup(false);
 901: 				++BakeJobIndex;
 902: 				break;
 903: 			}
 904: 			++CaptureCursor;
 905: 		}
 906: 		if (CaptureCursor >= CaptureTotalFrames && CaptureTotalFrames > 0)
 907: 		{
 908: 			if (bRunningPrepass)
 909: 			{
 910: 				bRunningPrepass = false;
 911: 				BakeAnalysePrepass();
 912: 			}
 913: 			else
 914: 			{
 915: 				BakeStep = EBakeStep::ConvertWrite;
 916: 			}
 917: 		}
 918: 		break;
 919: 	}
 920: 	case EBakeStep::ConvertWrite:
 921: 		BakeConvertAndWrite();
 922: 		BakeStep = EBakeStep::NextJob;
 923: 		StepTick = 0;
 924: 		break;
 925: 	case EBakeStep::NextJob:
 926: 		BakeStep = EBakeStep::StartJob;
 927: 		break;
 928: 	}
 929: }

  40: 	bool EnsureDir(const FString& Dir)
  41: 	{
  42: 		IPlatformFile& Files = FPlatformFileManager::Get().GetPlatformFile();
  43: 		return Files.CreateDirectoryTree(*Dir);
  44: 	}
  45: 
  46: 	// Bilinear resample of a linear HDR frame to the review cell size, tone-mapped x/(1+x).
  47: 	void BlitSourceCell(const FLinearColor* Src, int32 SrcW, int32 SrcH, FColor* Dst, int32 DstW, int32 DstH)
  48: 	{
  49: 		for (int32 y = 0; y < DstH; ++y)
  50: 		{
  51: 			const float SrcY = (static_cast<float>(y) + 0.5f) * SrcH / DstH - 0.5f;
  52: 			const int32 Y0 = FMath::Clamp(FMath::FloorToInt(SrcY), 0, SrcH - 1);
  53: 			const int32 Y1 = FMath::Min(Y0 + 1, SrcH - 1);
  54: 			const float TY = FMath::Clamp(SrcY - Y0, 0.f, 1.f);
  55: 			for (int32 x = 0; x < DstW; ++x)
  56: 			{
  57: 				const float SrcX = (static_cast<float>(x) + 0.5f) * SrcW / DstW - 0.5f;
  58: 				const int32 X0 = FMath::Clamp(FMath::FloorToInt(SrcX), 0, SrcW - 1);
  59: 				const int32 X1 = FMath::Min(X0 + 1, SrcW - 1);
  60: 				const float TX = FMath::Clamp(SrcX - X0, 0.f, 1.f);
  61: 				const FLinearColor A = Src[Y0 * SrcW + X0] * (1.f - TX) + Src[Y0 * SrcW + X1] * TX;
  62: 				const FLinearColor B = Src[Y1 * SrcW + X0] * (1.f - TX) + Src[Y1 * SrcW + X1] * TX;
  63: 				const FLinearColor C = A * (1.f - TY) + B * TY;
  64: 				Dst[y * DstW + x] = FColor(
  65: 					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.R / (1.f + C.R), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
  66: 					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.G / (1.f + C.G), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
  67: 					static_cast<uint8>(FMath::Clamp(C.B / (1.f + C.B), 0.f, 1.f) * 255.f + 0.5f),
  68: 					255);
  69: 			}
  70: 		}
  71: 	}
  72: 
  73: 	// Nearest upscale of one baked frame.
  74: 	void BlitPixelCell(const FColor* Src, int32 SrcW, int32 SrcH, FColor* Dst, int32 DstW, int32 DstH)
  75: 	{
  76: 		for (int32 y = 0; y < DstH; ++y)
  77: 		{
  78: 			const int32 SY = FMath::Min(y * SrcH / DstH, SrcH - 1);
  79: 			for (int32 x = 0; x < DstW; ++x)
  80: 			{
  81: 				const int32 SX = FMath::Min(x * SrcW / DstW, SrcW - 1);
  82: 				Dst[y * DstW + x] = Src[SY * SrcW + SX];
  83: 			}
  84: 		}
  85: 	}
  86: 
  87: 	bool SavePng(const FString& Path, int32 W, int32 H, const TArray<FColor>& Pixels)
  88: 	{
  89: 		if (Pixels.Num() != W * H)
  90: 		{
  91: 			return false;
  92: 		}
  93: 		TArray64<uint8> Compressed;
  94: 		FImageUtils::PNGCompressImageArray(W, H, TArrayView64<const FColor>(Pixels.GetData(), Pixels.Num()), Compressed);
  95: 		if (Compressed.Num() == 0)
  96: 		{
  97: 			return false;
  98: 		}
  99: 		TArray<uint8> Bytes;
 100: 		Bytes.Append(Compressed.GetData(), static_cast<int32>(FMath::Min<int64>(Compressed.Num(), MAX_int32)));
 101: 		return FFileHelper::SaveArrayToFile(Bytes, *Path);
 102: 	}

 105: AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode()
 106: {
 107: 	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
 108: 	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
 109: 	DefaultPawnClass = nullptr;

 112: 
 113: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::ParseCommandLine()
 114: {
 115: 	FString BakeArg;
 116: 	FString CompareArg;
 117: 	if (FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("-PvfxBake="), BakeArg))
 118: 	{
 119: 		RunMode = ERunMode::Bake;
 120: 		RunArg = BakeArg;
 121: 	}
 122: 	else if (FParse::Value(FCommandLine::Get(), TEXT("-PvfxCompare="), CompareArg))
 123: 	{
 124: 		RunMode = ERunMode::Compare;
 125: 		RunArg = CompareArg;
 126: 	}
 127: 	else if (FString(FCommandLine::Get()).Contains(TEXT("-PvfxShowcase")))
 128: 	{
 129: 		RunMode = ERunMode::Showcase;
 130: 	}
 131: }
 132: 
 133: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BeginPlay()
 134: {
 135: 	Super::BeginPlay();
 136: 	ParseCommandLine();
 137: 	if (RunMode == ERunMode::None)
 138: 	{
 139: 		return;
 140: 	}
 141: 	if (RunMode == ERunMode::Bake)
 142: 	{
 143: 		BakeJobFiles.Reset();
 144: 		for (const FString& File : FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::ListJobFiles())
 145: 		{
 146: 			if (RunArg.Equals(TEXT("All"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase)
 147: 				|| FPaths::GetBaseFilename(File).Equals(RunArg, ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 148: 			{
 149: 				BakeJobFiles.Add(File);
 150: 			}
 151: 		}
 152: 		if (BakeJobFiles.Num() == 0)
 153: 		{
 154: 			UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX no bake job matches '%s'."), *RunArg);
 155: 			FinishRun();
 156: 		}
 157: 	}
 158: }
 159: 
 160: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
 161: {
 162: 	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
 163: 	switch (RunMode)
 164: 	{
 165: 	case ERunMode::Bake: TickBake(); break;
 166: 	case ERunMode::Showcase: TickShowcase(); break;
 167: 	case ERunMode::Compare: TickCompare(); break;
 168: 	default: break;
 169: 	}
 170: }
 171: 
 172: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::FinishRun()
 173: {
 174: 	UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX DONE"));
 175: 	RunMode = ERunMode::None;
 176: 	FPlatformMisc::RequestExit(false);
 177: }

 179: // ---------------------------------------------------------------- bake mode ----
 180: 
 181: FVector AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeCameraDirection() const
 182: {
 183: 	const float Yaw = FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.ViewYawDeg);
 184: 	const float Pitch = FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.ViewPitchDeg);
 185: 	return FVector(FMath::Cos(Pitch) * FMath::Cos(Yaw), FMath::Cos(Pitch) * FMath::Sin(Yaw),
 186: 		FMath::Sin(Pitch));
 187: }
 188: 
 189: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeDestroyRig()
 190: {
 191: 	if (NiagaraActor)
 192: 	{
 193: 		NiagaraActor->Destroy();
 194: 	}
 195: 	NiagaraActor = nullptr;
 196: 	NiagaraComp = nullptr;
 197: 	NiagaraSystem = nullptr;
 198: 	if (CaptureComp)
 199: 	{
 200: 		if (AActor* CaptureActor = CaptureComp->GetOwner())
 201: 		{
 202: 			CaptureActor->Destroy();
 203: 		}
 204: 	}
 205: 	CaptureComp = nullptr;
 206: 	CaptureRT = nullptr;
 207: 	if (ViewCam)
 208: 	{
 209: 		ViewCam->Destroy();
 210: 	}
 211: 	ViewCam = nullptr;
 212: 	if (KeyLight)
 213: 	{
 214: 		KeyLight->Destroy();
 215: 	}
 216: 	KeyLight = nullptr;
 217: }
 218: 
 219: bool AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeSpawnRig(int32 RTWidth)
 220: {
 221: 	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
 222: 	if (!World)
 223: 	{
 224: 		return false;
 225: 	}
 226: 	NiagaraSystem = LoadObject<UNiagaraSystem>(nullptr, *Job.SystemPath);
 227: 	if (!NiagaraSystem)
 228: 	{
 229: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Error, TEXT("PVFX %s: cannot load Niagara system '%s' (is NiagaraFluids on the command line?)."),
 230: 			*Job.Id.ToString(), *Job.SystemPath);
 231: 		return false;
 232: 	}
 233: 	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
 234: 	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
 235: 	NiagaraActor = World->SpawnActor<AActor>(AActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
 236: 	if (!NiagaraActor)
 237: 	{
 238: 		return false;
 239: 	}
 240: 	NiagaraComp = NewObject<UNiagaraComponent>(NiagaraActor, TEXT("Niagara"));
 241: 	NiagaraActor->SetRootComponent(NiagaraComp);
 242: 	NiagaraComp->RegisterComponent();
 243: 	NiagaraComp->SetAsset(NiagaraSystem);
 244: 	NiagaraComp->SetRandomSeedOffset(0);
 245: 	NiagaraComp->SetAllowScalability(false);
 246: 	NiagaraComp->bAutoActivate = false;
 247: 	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserFloats)
 248: 	{
 249: 		NiagaraComp->SetVariableFloat(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
 250: 	}
 251: 	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserVectors)
 252: 	{
 253: 		NiagaraComp->SetVariableVec3(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
 254: 	}
 255: 	for (const auto& Pair : Job.UserColors)
 256: 	{
 257: 		NiagaraComp->SetVariableLinearColor(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
 258: 	}
 259: 	NiagaraComp->Activate(true);
 260: 
 261: 	const FVector Dir = BakeCameraDirection();
 262: 	const FRotator CamRot = FRotationMatrix::MakeFromX(Dir).Rotator();
 263: 	const FVector RightV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector);
 264: 	const FVector UpV = CamRot.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
 265: 	const bool bOrtho = Job.Projection.Equals(TEXT("Ortho"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
 266: 	const int32 RTW = FMath::Max(16, RTWidth);
 267: 	const int32 RTH = FMath::Max(16, FMath::RoundToInt(RTW * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth))));
 268: 
 269: 	AActor* CaptureActor = World->SpawnActor<AActor>(AActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
 270: 	CaptureComp = NewObject<USceneCaptureComponent2D>(CaptureActor, TEXT("PvfxCapture"));
 271: 	CaptureActor->SetRootComponent(CaptureComp);
 272: 	CaptureComp->RegisterComponent();
 273: 	CaptureComp->PrimitiveRenderMode = ESceneCapturePrimitiveRenderMode::PRM_UseShowOnlyList;
 274: 	CaptureComp->CaptureSource = ESceneCaptureSource::SCS_SceneColorHDR;
 275: 	if (!bLegacyCapture)
 276: 	{
 277: 		CaptureComp->ShowOnlyComponent(NiagaraComp);
 278: 	}
 279: 	CaptureComp->bCaptureEveryFrame = false;
 280: 	CaptureComp->bCaptureOnMovement = false;
 281: 	CaptureComp->bAlwaysPersistRenderingState = true;
 282: 	CaptureComp->bInheritMainViewCameraPostProcessSettings = false;
 283: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetBloom(false);
 284: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetEyeAdaptation(false);
 285: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetMotionBlur(false);
 286: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetTonemapper(false);
 287: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetToneCurve(false);
 288: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetTemporalAA(false);
 289: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetGrain(false);
 290: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetVignette(false);
 291: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetCameraImperfections(false);
 292: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetLensFlares(false);
 293: 	CaptureComp->ShowFlags.SetDepthOfField(false);
 294: 	if (bOrtho)
 295: 	{
 296: 		CaptureComp->ProjectionType = ECameraProjectionMode::Orthographic;
 297: 		CaptureComp->OrthoWidth = FramingWidthCm;
 298: 		CaptureActor->SetActorLocation(FramingCenterCm - Dir * 2000.f);
 299: 	}
 300: 	else
 301: 	{
 302: 		CaptureComp->ProjectionType = ECameraProjectionMode::Perspective;
 303: 		CaptureComp->FOVAngle = Job.FovDeg;
 304: 		const float Dist = (FramingWidthCm * 0.5f) / FMath::Tan(FMath::DegreesToRadians(Job.FovDeg * 0.5f));
 305: 		CaptureActor->SetActorLocation(FramingCenterCm - Dir * Dist);
 306: 	}
 307: 	CaptureActor->SetActorRotation(CamRot);
 308: 	CaptureRT = NewObject<UTextureRenderTarget2D>(GetTransientPackage(), NAME_None, RF_Transient);
 309: 	CaptureRT->InitCustomFormat(RTW, RTH, PF_FloatRGBA, true);
 310: 	CaptureRT->UpdateResourceImmediate(true);
 311: 	CaptureComp->TextureTarget = CaptureRT;
 312: 
 313: 	if (Job.bKeyLight)
 314: 	{
 315: 		KeyLight = World->SpawnActor<ADirectionalLight>(ADirectionalLight::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
 316: 		// Style guide: light from the top left as seen by the capture camera.
 317: 		const FVector Travel = (Dir - UpV + RightV * 0.8f).GetSafeNormal();
 318: 		if (UDirectionalLightComponent* LightComp = Cast<UDirectionalLightComponent>(KeyLight->GetLightComponent()))
 319: 		{
 320: 			LightComp->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
 321: 			LightComp->SetIntensity(Job.KeyLightIntensity);
 322: 			LightComp->SetLightColor(FLinearColor::White);
 323: 		}
 324: 		KeyLight->SetActorRotation(Travel.Rotation());
 325: 	}
 326: 
 327: 	// The main view must render every frame and must look at the effect (GPU sims do not
 328: 	// advance in scene captures): park a camera actor on the capture and make it the view target.
 329: 	ViewCam = World->SpawnActor<ACameraActor>(ACameraActor::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
 330: 	ViewCam->SetActorLocation(CaptureActor->GetActorLocation());
 331: 	ViewCam->SetActorRotation(CamRot);
 332: 	if (bOrtho)
 333: 	{
 334: 		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->ProjectionMode = ECameraProjectionMode::Orthographic;
 335: 		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->OrthoWidth = FramingWidthCm;
 336: 	}
 337: 	else
 338: 	{
 339: 		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->ProjectionMode = ECameraProjectionMode::Perspective;
 340: 		ViewCam->GetCameraComponent()->FieldOfView = Job.FovDeg;
 341: 	}
 342: 	if (APlayerController* PC = EnsurePlayerController())
 343: 	{
 344: 		PC->SetViewTarget(ViewCam);
 345: 	}
 346: 	else
 347: 	{
 348: 		UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Warning, TEXT("PVFX %s: no player controller, the main view may not advance the sim."),
 349: 			*Job.Id.ToString());
 350: 	}
 351: 	return true;
 352: }
 353: 
 354: void AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeFillCaptureTicks(bool bPrepassNow)
 355: {
 356: 	CaptureAtTicks.Reset();
 357: 	CaptureCursor = 0;
 358: 	const int32 WarmupFrames = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(Job.WarmupSeconds * Job.SimFps));
 359: 	if (bPrepassNow)
 360: 	{
 361: 		// The framing union covers the warmed-up window only, like the real pass: the
 362: 		// transient rise from t = 0 would inflate the box of a looping clip.
 363: 		const int32 TotalFrames = WarmupFrames + FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(Job.DurationSeconds * Job.SimFps));
 364: 		for (int32 Tick = WarmupFrames; Tick < TotalFrames; Tick += 4)
 365: 		{
 366: 			CaptureAtTicks.Add(Tick);
 367: 		}
 368: 		CaptureTotalFrames = CaptureAtTicks.Num();
 369: 	}
 370: 	else
 371: 	{
 372: 		const int32 Blend = (Job.bLoop && Job.LoopBlendFrames > 0) ? Job.LoopBlendFrames : 0;
 373: 		const int32 Total = Job.FrameCount() + Blend;
 374: 		CaptureStride = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(static_cast<float>(Job.SimFps) / FMath::Max(1, Job.Fps)));
 375: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < Total; ++i)
 376: 		{
 377: 			CaptureAtTicks.Add(WarmupFrames + i * CaptureStride);
 378: 		}
 379: 		CaptureTotalFrames = Total;
 380: 	}
 381: }
 382: 
 383: bool AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::BakeCaptureOne()
 384: {
 385: 	if (!CaptureComp || !CaptureRT)
 386: 	{
 387: 		return false;
 388: 	}
 389: 	CaptureComp->CaptureScene();
 390: 	FTextureRenderTargetResource* Res = CaptureRT->GameThread_GetRenderTargetResource();
 391: 	if (!Res)
 392: 	{
 393: 		return false;
 394: 	}
 395: 	TArray<FLinearColor> Raw;
 396: 	if (!Res->ReadLinearColorPixels(Raw) || Raw.Num() != CaptureRT->SizeX * CaptureRT->SizeY)
 397: 	{
 398: 		return false;
 399: 	}
 400: 	FSimFrame Frame;
 401: 	Frame.Width = CaptureRT->SizeX;
 402: 	Frame.Height = CaptureRT->SizeY;
 403: 	Frame.Color.SetNum(Raw.Num());
 404: 	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(Raw.Num());
 405: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Raw.Num(); ++i)
 406: 	{
 407: 		Frame.Color[i] = Raw[i];
 408: 		Frame.Opacity[i] = GBPixelVfxBakeLocal::Saturate(1.f - Raw[i].A);
 409: 	}
 410: 	SimFrames.Add(MoveTemp(Frame));
 411: 	return true;
 412: }

   1: // FGBPixelVfxBakeJob: JSON loader. Reads exactly the keys of the Bake/Jobs files.
   2: #include "GBPixelVfxBakeJob.h"
   3: 
   4: #include "Dom/JsonObject.h"
   5: #include "Dom/JsonValue.h"
   6: #include "HAL/FileManager.h"
   7: #include "Misc/FileHelper.h"
   8: #include "Misc/Paths.h"
   9: #include "Serialization/JsonReader.h"
  10: #include "Serialization/JsonSerializer.h"
  11: #include "GBPixelVfxConvert.h"
  12: 
  13: namespace GBPixelVfxJobLocal
  14: {
  15: 	bool ParseHex(const FString& Text, FLinearColor& OutColor)
  16: 	{
  17: 		FColor Srgb;
  18: 		if (!GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(Text, Srgb))
  19: 		{
  20: 			return false;
  21: 		}
  22: 		OutColor = FLinearColor(Srgb);
  23: 		return true;
  24: 	}
  25: 
  26: 	void ReadFloats(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, float>& Out)
  27: 	{
  28: 		if (!Obj.IsValid())
  29: 		{
  30: 			return;
  31: 		}
  32: 		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
  33: 		{
  34: 			Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), static_cast<float>(Pair.Value->AsNumber()));
  35: 		}
  36: 	}
  37: 
  38: 	void ReadVectors(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, FVector>& Out)
  39: 	{
  40: 		if (!Obj.IsValid())
  41: 		{
  42: 			return;
  43: 		}
  44: 		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
  45: 		{
  46: 			const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Arr = nullptr;
  47: 			if (Pair.Value->TryGetArray(Arr) && Arr && Arr->Num() == 3)
  48: 			{
  49: 				Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), FVector(
  50: 					(*Arr)[0]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[1]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[2]->AsNumber()));
  51: 			}
  52: 		}
  53: 	}
  54: 
  55: 	void ReadColors(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, TMap<FName, FLinearColor>& Out)
  56: 	{
  57: 		if (!Obj.IsValid())
  58: 		{
  59: 			return;
  60: 		}
  61: 		for (const auto& Pair : Obj->Values)
  62: 		{
  63: 			FString Text;
  64: 			if (Pair.Value->TryGetString(Text))
  65: 			{
  66: 				FLinearColor Color;
  67: 				if (ParseHex(Text, Color))
  68: 				{
  69: 					Out.Add(FName(*Pair.Key), Color);
  70: 				}
  71: 			}
  72: 		}
  73: 	}
  74: 
  75: 	FVector ReadVector3(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, const FVector& Default)
  76: 	{
  77: 		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Arr = nullptr;
  78: 		if (!Obj.IsValid() || !Obj->TryGetArrayField(Key, Arr) || !Arr || Arr->Num() != 3)
  79: 		{
  80: 			return Default;
  81: 		}
  82: 		return FVector((*Arr)[0]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[1]->AsNumber(), (*Arr)[2]->AsNumber());
  83: 	}
  84: 
  85: 	double ReadNumber(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, double Default)
  86: 	{
  87: 		double Value = Default;
  88: 		if (Obj.IsValid())
  89: 		{
  90: 			Obj->TryGetNumberField(Key, Value);
  91: 		}
  92: 		return Value;
  93: 	}
  94: 
  95: 	bool ReadBool(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, bool Default)
  96: 	{
  97: 		bool Value = Default;
  98: 		if (Obj.IsValid())
  99: 		{
 100: 			Obj->TryGetBoolField(Key, Value);
 101: 		}
 102: 		return Value;
 103: 	}
 104: 
 105: 	FString ReadString(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Obj, const TCHAR* Key, const FString& Default)
 106: 	{
 107: 		FString Value = Default;
 108: 		if (Obj.IsValid())
 109: 		{
 110: 			Obj->TryGetStringField(Key, Value);
 111: 		}
 112: 		return Value;
 113: 	}
 114: 
 115: 	void ReadRamps(const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& Pixel, TArray<TArray<FColor>>& Out)
 116: 	{
 117: 		Out.Reset();
 118: 		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Ramps = nullptr;
 119: 		if (!Pixel.IsValid() || !Pixel->TryGetArrayField(TEXT("Ramps"), Ramps) || !Ramps)
 120: 		{
 121: 			return;
 122: 		}
 123: 		for (const TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>& Ramp : *Ramps)
 124: 		{
 125: 			const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* Colors = nullptr;
 126: 			if (!Ramp->TryGetArray(Colors) || !Colors)
 127: 			{
 128: 				continue;
 129: 			}
 130: 			TArray<FColor> RampColors;
 131: 			for (const TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>& Entry : *Colors)
 132: 			{
 133: 				FString Text;
 134: 				if (Entry->TryGetString(Text))
 135: 				{
 136: 					FColor Srgb;
 137: 					if (GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(Text, Srgb))
 138: 					{
 139: 						RampColors.Add(Srgb);
 140: 					}
 141: 				}
 142: 			}
 143: 			if (RampColors.Num() > 0)
 144: 			{
 145: 				Out.Add(MoveTemp(RampColors));
 146: 			}
 147: 		}
 148: 	}
 149: }

 151: TArray<FString> FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::ListJobFiles()
 152: {
 153: 	TArray<FString> Files;
 154: 	const FString Dir = FPaths::ProjectPluginsDir() / TEXT("GBPixelVfx/Bake/Jobs");
 155: 	IFileManager::Get().FindFiles(Files, *(Dir / TEXT("*.json")), true, false);
 156: 	Files.Sort();
 157: 	for (FString& File : Files)
 158: 	{
 159: 		File = Dir / File;
 160: 	}
 161: 	return Files;
 162: }
 163: 
 164: bool FGBPixelVfxBakeJob::LoadFromFile(const FString& Path)
 165: {
 166: 	FString Text;
 167: 	if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToString(Text, *Path))
 168: 	{
 169: 		return false;
 170: 	}
 171: 	TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Root;
 172: 	const TSharedRef<TJsonReader<>> Reader = TJsonReaderFactory<>::Create(Text);
 173: 	if (!FJsonSerializer::Deserialize(Reader, Root) || !Root.IsValid())
 174: 	{
 175: 		return false;
 176: 	}
 177: 
 178: 	FGBPixelVfxBakeJob Fresh;
 179: 	FString IdText;
 180: 	if (!Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("Id"), IdText) || IdText.IsEmpty())
 181: 	{
 182: 		return false;
 183: 	}
 184: 	Fresh.Id = FName(*IdText);
 185: 	Root->TryGetStringField(TEXT("System"), Fresh.SystemPath);
 186: 
 187: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>* Sub = nullptr;
 188: 	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserFloats"), Sub) && Sub)
 189: 	{
 190: 		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadFloats(*Sub, Fresh.UserFloats);
 191: 	}
 192: 	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserVectors"), Sub) && Sub)
 193: 	{
 194: 		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadVectors(*Sub, Fresh.UserVectors);
 195: 	}
 196: 	if (Root->TryGetObjectField(TEXT("UserColors"), Sub) && Sub)
 197: 	{
 198: 		GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadColors(*Sub, Fresh.UserColors);
 199: 	}
 200: 
 201: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Capture = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("Capture"));
 202: 	Fresh.Resolution = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("Resolution"), Fresh.Resolution));
 203: 	Fresh.bAutoFrame = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Capture, TEXT("AutoFrame"), Fresh.bAutoFrame);
 204: 	Fresh.AutoFramePadding = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("AutoFramePadding"), Fresh.AutoFramePadding));
 205: 	Fresh.WidthCm = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("WidthCm"), Fresh.WidthCm));
 206: 	Fresh.CenterCm = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadVector3(Capture, TEXT("CenterCm"), Fresh.CenterCm);
 207: 	Fresh.ViewYawDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("ViewYawDeg"), Fresh.ViewYawDeg));
 208: 	Fresh.ViewPitchDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("ViewPitchDeg"), Fresh.ViewPitchDeg));
 209: 	Fresh.Projection = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Capture, TEXT("Projection"), Fresh.Projection);
 210: 	Fresh.FovDeg = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("FovDeg"), Fresh.FovDeg));
 211: 	Fresh.WarmupSeconds = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("WarmupSeconds"), Fresh.WarmupSeconds));
 212: 	Fresh.DurationSeconds = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("DurationSeconds"), Fresh.DurationSeconds));
 213: 	Fresh.SimFps = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("SimFps"), Fresh.SimFps));
 214: 	Fresh.Exposure = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("Exposure"), Fresh.Exposure));
 215: 	Fresh.bKeyLight = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Capture, TEXT("KeyLight"), Fresh.bKeyLight);
 216: 	Fresh.KeyLightIntensity = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Capture, TEXT("KeyLightIntensity"), Fresh.KeyLightIntensity));
 217: 
 218: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Pixel = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("Pixel"));
 219: 	Fresh.FrameWidth = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("FrameWidth"), Fresh.FrameWidth));
 220: 	Fresh.FrameHeight = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("FrameHeight"), Fresh.FrameHeight));
 221: 	Fresh.Fps = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("Fps"), Fresh.Fps));
 222: 	Fresh.bLoop = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Pixel, TEXT("Loop"), Fresh.bLoop);
 223: 	Fresh.LoopBlendFrames = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("LoopBlendFrames"), Fresh.LoopBlendFrames));
 224: 	Fresh.AlphaMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("AlphaMode"), Fresh.AlphaMode);
 225: 	Fresh.AlphaThreshold = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("AlphaThreshold"), Fresh.AlphaThreshold));
 226: 	Fresh.ColorMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("ColorMode"), Fresh.ColorMode);
 227: 	Fresh.HotLuminance = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("HotLuminance"), Fresh.HotLuminance));
 228: 	Fresh.HotSaturation = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("HotSaturation"), Fresh.HotSaturation));
 229: 	GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadRamps(Pixel, Fresh.Ramps);
 230: 	Fresh.LevelsPercentile = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("LevelsPercentile"), Fresh.LevelsPercentile));
 231: 	Fresh.Gamma = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("Gamma"), Fresh.Gamma));
 232: 	Fresh.Hysteresis = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("Hysteresis"), Fresh.Hysteresis));
 233: 	Fresh.MinIslandPixels = static_cast<int32>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Pixel, TEXT("MinIslandPixels"), Fresh.MinIslandPixels));
 234: 	Fresh.bFillHoles = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Pixel, TEXT("FillHoles"), Fresh.bFillHoles);
 235: 	Fresh.Outline = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Pixel, TEXT("Outline"), Fresh.Outline);
 236: 
 237: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Light = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("Light"));
 238: 	Fresh.bLightEnabled = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Light, TEXT("Enabled"), Fresh.bLightEnabled);
 239: 	const FString LightHex = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Light, TEXT("Color"), Fresh.LightColorHex);
 240: 	FLinearColor ParsedColor;
 241: 	if (GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ParseHex(LightHex, ParsedColor))
 242: 	{
 243: 		Fresh.LightColorHex = LightHex;
 244: 		Fresh.LightColor = ParsedColor;
 245: 	}
 246: 	Fresh.LightIntensity = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Light, TEXT("Intensity"), Fresh.LightIntensity));
 247: 	Fresh.LightRadiusCm = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(Light, TEXT("RadiusCm"), Fresh.LightRadiusCm));
 248: 	Fresh.bLightCastShadows = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(Light, TEXT("CastShadows"), Fresh.bLightCastShadows);
 249: 	Fresh.LightTrackMode = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(Light, TEXT("TrackMode"), Fresh.LightTrackMode);
 250: 
 251: 	const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> World = Root->GetObjectField(TEXT("World"));
 252: 	Fresh.Facing = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadString(World, TEXT("Facing"), Fresh.Facing);
 253: 	Fresh.Glow = static_cast<float>(GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadNumber(World, TEXT("Glow"), Fresh.Glow));
 254: 	Fresh.bLit = GBPixelVfxJobLocal::ReadBool(World, TEXT("Lit"), Fresh.bLit);
 255: 
 256: 	*this = Fresh;
 257: 	return true;
 258: }

  21: 	float ViewPitchDeg = 0.f;

  23: 	float FovDeg = 10.f;

  58:

  61: 	float Glow = 1.5f;
  62: 	bool bLit = false;
  63:

  65:

   1: // Bake / showcase / compare entry point. Started by PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 with one of
   2: // -PvfxBake=<Id|All>, -PvfxCompare=<Id>, -PvfxShowcase. Runs a small Tick state machine,

Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Tools/make_pixelvfx_material.py
   1: # make_pixelvfx_material.py - Unreal Editor Python script that (re)builds the GBPixelVfx materials from text.
   2: #
   3: # The .uasset files are not in git (Content/ is ignored except Content/Data), so this script IS the source of the
   4: # materials. Run it through Tools/Jam/PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 -Action Materials (UnrealEditor-Cmd -run=pythonscript).
   5: #
   6: # M_PixelVfxSprite (/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelVfxSprite): the baked pixel-art flipbook quad for effects
   7: # that emit light (fire, explosions, arcane fire). Masked, Unlit, two-sided. Parameters:
   8: #   Atlas     (texture object) the baked atlas, sampled with the texture's own sampler so nearest filtering survives
   9: #   FrameU/V/W/H (scalars)   UV = TexCoord0 * (FrameW, FrameH) + (FrameU, FrameV) (set per frame from C++)
  10: #   Tint      (vector)         multiplies the texture color
  11: #   Glow      (scalar)         emissive = sample.rgb * Tint.rgb * (1 + Glow)
  12: #
  13: # M_PixelVfxSpriteLit: same layout, Masked, Default Lit, for effects that do not emit (smoke). It receives the
  14: # scene's light and shadows, including the light of a pixel fire next to it. Base color = sample * Tint,
  15: # roughness 1, specular 0, emissive = sample * Tint * Glow.
  16: #
  17: # M_PixelVfxStage (/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelVfxStage): Opaque, Default Lit, used only by the bake
  18: # showcase stage (a glossy floor to see reflections). Parameters:
  19: #   Color    (vector) base color
  20: #   Roughness (scalar, default 0.6)
  21: #   Metallic  (scalar, default 0)
  22: 
  23: import unreal
  24: 
  25: MATERIAL_DIR = "/Game/Glyphbound/Materials"
  26: SPRITE = "M_PixelVfxSprite"
  27: SPRITE_LIT = "M_PixelVfxSpriteLit"
  28: STAGE = "M_PixelVfxStage"
  29: DEFAULT_TEXTURE = "/Engine/EngineMaterials/DefaultDiffuse.DefaultDiffuse"
  30: 
  31: MEL = unreal.MaterialEditingLibrary
  32: 
  33: 
  34: def log(message):
  35:     unreal.log("make_pixelvfx_material: " + message)
  36: 
  37: 
  38: def fail(message):
  39:     unreal.log_error("make_pixelvfx_material: ERROR " + message)
  40:     raise RuntimeError(message)
  41: 
  42: 
  43: def get_or_create_material(directory, name):
  44:     path = directory + "/" + name
  45:     if unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.does_asset_exist(path):
  46:         material = unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.load_asset(path)
  47:         if not isinstance(material, unreal.Material):
  48:             fail("%s exists but is not a Material" % path)
  49:         MEL.delete_all_material_expressions(material)
  50:         log("rebuilding %s" % path)
  51:         return material
  52:     if not unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.does_directory_exist(directory):
  53:         unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.make_directory(directory)
  54:     tools = unreal.AssetToolsHelpers.get_asset_tools()
  55:     material = tools.create_asset(name, directory, unreal.Material, unreal.MaterialFactoryNew())
  56:     if material is None:
  57:         fail("could not create %s" % path)
  58:     log("created %s" % path)
  59:     return material
  60: 
  61: 
  62: class Graph(object):
  63:     def __init__(self, material):
  64:         self.material = material
  65: 
  66:     def node(self, expression_class, x, y, **properties):
  67:         expression = MEL.create_material_expression(self.material, expression_class, x, y)
  68:         if expression is None:
  69:             fail("could not create %s" % expression_class.__name__)
  70:         for key, value in properties.items():
  71:             expression.set_editor_property(key, value)
  72:         return expression
  73: 
  74:     def connect(self, source, source_output, target, target_inputs):
  75:         """target_inputs: one input name or a list of accepted names (script name first, full name second)."""
  76:         if isinstance(target_inputs, str):
  77:             target_inputs = [target_inputs]
  78:         for target_input in target_inputs:
  79:             if MEL.connect_material_expressions(source, source_output, target, target_input):
  80:                 return
  81:         fail("cannot connect %s.%r -> %s.%r (outputs %s, inputs %s)" % (
  82:             source.get_class().get_name(), source_output, target.get_class().get_name(), target_inputs,
  83:             list(MEL.get_material_expression_output_names(source)),
  84:             list(MEL.get_material_expression_input_names(source))))
  85: 
  86:     def to_property(self, source, source_output, material_property):
  87:         if not MEL.connect_material_property(source, source_output, material_property):
  88:             fail("cannot connect %s.%r -> %s (outputs %s)" % (
  89:                 source.get_class().get_name(), source_output, material_property,
  90:                 list(MEL.get_material_expression_output_names(source))))
  91: 
  92: 
  93: def mask(graph, source, x, y, r=False, g=False, b=False, a=False):
  94:     expression = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionComponentMask, x, y, r=r, g=g, b=b, a=a)
  95:     graph.connect(source, "", expression, ["", "Input"])
  96:     return expression
  97: 
  98: 
  99: def add_sprite_params(graph):
 100:     default_texture = unreal.load_asset(DEFAULT_TEXTURE)
 101:     if default_texture is None:
 102:         fail("cannot load the default texture %s" % DEFAULT_TEXTURE)
 103:     atlas = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionTextureObjectParameter, -1400, -500,
 104:                        parameter_name="Atlas", texture=default_texture,
 105:                        sampler_type=unreal.MaterialSamplerType.SAMPLERTYPE_COLOR)
 106:     # The frame rect as four scalars (NOT one vector): masks on a uniform that feeds the
 107:     # texture UVs are folded into the preshader as float3 and fail to compile, so the rect
 108:     # stays scalar and the only masks sit on the vertex-stage texture coordinate.
 109:     frame_u = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -1400, -260,
 110:                          parameter_name="FrameU", default_value=0.0)
 111:     frame_v = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -1400, -180,
 112:                          parameter_name="FrameV", default_value=0.0)
 113:     frame_w = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -1400, -100,
 114:                          parameter_name="FrameW", default_value=1.0)
 115:     frame_h = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -1400, -20,
 116:                          parameter_name="FrameH", default_value=1.0)
 117:     tint = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionVectorParameter, -600, -250,
 118:                       parameter_name="Tint", default_value=unreal.LinearColor(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0))
 119:     glow = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -400, 150,
 120:                       parameter_name="Glow", default_value=0.0)
 121:     return atlas, frame_u, frame_v, frame_w, frame_h, tint, glow
 122: 
 123: 
 124: def single_mask(graph, source, x, y, channel):
 125:     kwargs = {channel: True}
 126:     return mask(graph, source, x, y, **kwargs)
 127: 
 128: 
 129: def add_sprite_sampling(graph, atlas, frame_u, frame_v, frame_w, frame_h):
 130:     # UV = TexCoord0 * (FrameW, FrameH) + (FrameU, FrameV), sampled with the texture's own filter.
 131:     texcoord = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionTextureCoordinate, -1100, -100, coordinate_index=0)
 132:     tex_u = single_mask(graph, texcoord, -900, -200, "r")
 133:     tex_v = single_mask(graph, texcoord, -900, -100, "g")
 134:     scaled_u = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionMultiply, -700, -200)
 135:     graph.connect(tex_u, "", scaled_u, "A")
 136:     graph.connect(frame_w, "", scaled_u, "B")
 137:     uv_u = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAdd, -500, -200)
 138:     graph.connect(scaled_u, "", uv_u, "A")
 139:     graph.connect(frame_u, "", uv_u, "B")
 140:     scaled_v = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionMultiply, -700, 0)
 141:     graph.connect(tex_v, "", scaled_v, "A")
 142:     graph.connect(frame_h, "", scaled_v, "B")
 143:     uv_v = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAdd, -500, 0)
 144:     graph.connect(scaled_v, "", uv_v, "A")
 145:     graph.connect(frame_v, "", uv_v, "B")
 146:     uv = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAppendVector, -300, -100)
 147:     graph.connect(uv_u, "", uv, "A")
 148:     graph.connect(uv_v, "", uv, "B")
 149:     sample = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionTextureSample, -150, -300,
 150:                         sampler_source=unreal.SamplerSourceMode.SSM_FROM_TEXTURE_ASSET,
 151:                         sampler_type=unreal.MaterialSamplerType.SAMPLERTYPE_COLOR)
 152:     graph.connect(atlas, "", sample, ["Tex", "TextureObject"])
 153:     graph.connect(uv, "", sample, ["UVs", "Coordinates"])
 154:     return sample
 155: 
 156: 
 157: def add_sprite_color(graph, sample, tint, glow):
 158:     tint_r = single_mask(graph, tint, -150, -300, "r")
 159:     tint_g = single_mask(graph, tint, -150, -220, "g")
 160:     tint_b = single_mask(graph, tint, -150, -140, "b")
 161:     tint_rgb = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAppendVector, 0, -260)
 162:     graph.connect(tint_r, "", tint_rgb, "A")
 163:     graph.connect(tint_g, "", tint_rgb, "B")
 164:     tint_rgb2 = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAppendVector, 150, -260)
 165:     graph.connect(tint_rgb, "", tint_rgb2, "A")
 166:     graph.connect(tint_b, "", tint_rgb2, "B")
 167:     color = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionMultiply, 300, -300)
 168:     graph.connect(sample, "RGB", color, "A")
 169:     graph.connect(tint_rgb2, "", color, "B")
 170:     one = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionConstant, 300, 0, r=1.0)
 171:     one_plus_glow = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionAdd, 450, 0)
 172:     graph.connect(one, "", one_plus_glow, "A")
 173:     graph.connect(glow, "", one_plus_glow, "B")
 174:     emissive = graph.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionMultiply, 600, -150)
 175:     graph.connect(color, "", emissive, "A")
 176:     graph.connect(one_plus_glow, "", emissive, "B")
 177:     return color, emissive
 178: 
 179: 
 180: def build_sprite(name, lit):
 181:     material = get_or_create_material(MATERIAL_DIR, name)
 182:     material.set_editor_property("blend_mode", unreal.BlendMode.BLEND_MASKED)
 183:     if lit:
 184:         material.set_editor_property("shading_model", unreal.MaterialShadingModel.MSM_DEFAULT_LIT)
 185:     else:
 186:         material.set_editor_property("shading_model", unreal.MaterialShadingModel.MSM_UNLIT)
 187:     material.set_editor_property("two_sided", True)
 188:     material.set_editor_property("opacity_mask_clip_value", 0.5)
 189: 
 190:     g = Graph(material)
 191:     atlas, frame_u, frame_v, frame_w, frame_h, tint, glow = add_sprite_params(g)
 192:     sample = add_sprite_sampling(g, atlas, frame_u, frame_v, frame_w, frame_h)
 193:     color, emissive = add_sprite_color(g, sample, tint, glow)
 194: 
 195:     if lit:
 196:         g.to_property(color, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_BASE_COLOR)
 197:         roughness = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionConstant, 0, 250, r=1.0)
 198:         specular = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionConstant, 150, 250, r=0.0)
 199:         g.to_property(roughness, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_ROUGHNESS)
 200:         g.to_property(specular, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_SPECULAR)
 201:     g.to_property(emissive, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_EMISSIVE_COLOR)
 202:     g.to_property(sample, "A", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_OPACITY_MASK)
 203: 
 204:     check_and_save(material, name)
 205: 
 206: 
 207: def build_stage():
 208:     material = get_or_create_material(MATERIAL_DIR, STAGE)
 209:     material.set_editor_property("blend_mode", unreal.BlendMode.BLEND_OPAQUE)
 210:     material.set_editor_property("shading_model", unreal.MaterialShadingModel.MSM_DEFAULT_LIT)
 211:     material.set_editor_property("two_sided", False)
 212: 
 213:     g = Graph(material)
 214:     color_param = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionVectorParameter, -600, -250,
 215:                          parameter_name="Color", default_value=unreal.LinearColor(0.02, 0.02, 0.025, 1.0))
 216:     roughness = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -400, 0,
 217:                        parameter_name="Roughness", default_value=0.6)
 218:     metallic = g.node(unreal.MaterialExpressionScalarParameter, -400, 150,
 219:                       parameter_name="Metallic", default_value=0.0)
 220: 
 221:     color_rgb = mask(g, color_param, -150, -250, r=True, g=True, b=True)
 222: 
 223:     g.to_property(color_rgb, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_BASE_COLOR)
 224:     g.to_property(roughness, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_ROUGHNESS)
 225:     g.to_property(metallic, "", unreal.MaterialProperty.MP_METALLIC)
 226: 
 227:     check_and_save(material, STAGE)
 228: 
 229: 
 230: def check_and_save(material, name):
 231:     errors = MEL.recompile_material(material)
 232:     if errors:
 233:         for error in errors:
 234:             unreal.log_error("make_pixelvfx_material: ERROR compile: %s" % error)
 235:         fail("%s has compile errors" % name)
 236:     bad_nodes = MEL.get_material_error_expressions(material)
 237:     if bad_nodes:
 238:         fail("%s: %d expressions report errors: %s" % (
 239:             name, len(bad_nodes), [n.get_class().get_name() for n in bad_nodes]))
 240: 
 241:     path = MATERIAL_DIR + "/" + name
 242:     if not unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.save_asset(path, only_if_is_dirty=False):
 243:         fail("could not save %s" % path)
 244:     log("saved %s" % path)
 245: 
 246: 
 247: def main():
 248:     build_sprite(SPRITE, False)
 249:     build_sprite(SPRITE_LIT, True)
 250:     build_stage()
 251:     log("OK")
 252: 
 253: 
 254: main()
Glyphbound/Plugins/GBPixelVfx/Source/GBPixelVfxBaker/Private/GBPixelVfxBakerModule.cpp
   1: // GBPixelVfxBaker module: no startup work. The bake game mode only runs when a -Pvfx... switch
   2: // is on the command line; the cook's Editor process can load this module and must see no side effects.
   3: // The log category is defined again here (same name as the runtime module's): log categories are
   4: // matched by name, and this keeps both DLLs free of cross-module data imports.
   5: #include "GBPixelVfxLog.h"
   6: 
   7: #include "Modules/ModuleManager.h"
   8: 
   9: DEFINE_LOG_CATEGORY(LogGBPixelVfx);
  10: 
  11: IMPLEMENT_MODULE(FDefaultGameModuleImpl, GBPixelVfxBaker);
