<#
PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 - Everything the GBPixelVfx plugin needs from the machine, in one script:
build, pure-logic tests, the sprite material, baking Niagara effects to pixel-art flipbooks, the showcase run.

Why its own script: while the plugin is being written it is NOT enabled in Glyphbound.uproject
("EnabledByDefault": false in the .uplugin), so another chat's builds never compile a half-written plugin.
This script turns it on from the command line (UBT -EnablePlugin=, runtime -EnablePlugins=). NiagaraFluids is
only ever enabled here, on the bake command line: the packaged game does not carry it.

Machine rule: one build or run at a time. Every action takes the machine lock
(Glyphbound/Saved/machine.lock: who, what, UTC time) and removes it at the end. If someone else holds a lock
younger than 45 minutes, the script waits (up to -LockWaitMinutes) instead of running on top of it.

Usage (from the repo root):
  powershell -File Tools/Jam/PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 -Action Build
  powershell -File Tools/Jam/PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 -Action Test
  powershell -File Tools/Jam/PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 -Action Materials
  powershell -File Tools/Jam/PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 -Action Bake -Id FluidFire      # or -Id All
  powershell -File Tools/Jam/PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 -Action Compare -Id FluidFire   # source vs baked: frame time + screenshot
  powershell -File Tools/Jam/PixelVfx-Jam.ps1 -Action Showcase                # every baked clip on a small lit stage
  Add -HardwareRT to Compare / Showcase to run that process with hardware ray tracing on (project config untouched).
  Add -Window to see the run instead of rendering offscreen.
Exit code: 0 = the action reported success (build Succeeded, tests green, "PVFX DONE" with no errors).
#>
param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][ValidateSet('Build', 'Test', 'Materials', 'Bake', 'Compare', 'Showcase')][string]$Action,
    [string]$Id = 'All',
    [switch]$HardwareRT,
    [switch]$Window,
    [string]$Owner = 'pixelvfx',
    [int]$LockWaitMinutes = 40,
    [int]$TimeoutSeconds = 900,
    [string]$Project = '',
    [string]$EngineDir = 'C:\Program Files\Epic Games\UE_5.9'
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
# $PSScriptRoot came out empty while this script's parameters were being bound (it has [Parameter()] attributes),
# so the default project path is resolved here instead of in the param block.
$scriptDir = if ($PSScriptRoot) { $PSScriptRoot } else { Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path }
if (-not $Project) { $Project = Join-Path $scriptDir '..\..\Glyphbound\Glyphbound.uproject' }
$Project = (Resolve-Path $Project).Path
$projDir = Split-Path $Project -Parent
$logDir = Join-Path $projDir 'Saved\Logs'
[void][IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($logDir)
$stamp = [DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString('yyyyMMdd-HHmmss')
$lock = Join-Path $projDir 'Saved\machine.lock'
$pluginDir = Join-Path $projDir 'Plugins\GBPixelVfx'
$editor = Join-Path $EngineDir 'Engine\Binaries\Win64\UnrealEditor.exe'
$editorCmd = Join-Path $EngineDir 'Engine\Binaries\Win64\UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe'

function Wait-MachineLock {
    $deadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddMinutes($LockWaitMinutes)
    while ($true) {
        $busy = $null
        if (Test-Path $lock) {
            $age = ([DateTime]::UtcNow - (Get-Item $lock).LastWriteTimeUtc).TotalMinutes
            if ($age -lt 45) { $busy = "lock held: " + ((Get-Content $lock -Raw) -replace '\s+', ' ') }
        }
        if (-not $busy) {
            # The interactive Editor holds the module DLLs and the GPU: never run on top of it.
            $ed = @(Get-Process -Name 'UnrealEditor', 'UnrealEditor-Cmd' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
            if ($ed.Count -gt 0) { $busy = "an Unreal process is running (PID $($ed[0].Id))" }
        }
        if (-not $busy) { break }
        if ([DateTime]::UtcNow -gt $deadline) { Write-Host "LOCK TIMEOUT after $LockWaitMinutes min: $busy"; exit 5 }
        Write-Host "waiting for the machine: $busy"
        Start-Sleep 15
    }
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($lock, ("{0} | {1} {2} | {3}" -f $Owner, $Action, $Id, [DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString('o')))
}
function Remove-MachineLock {
    if (Test-Path $lock) {
        $text = Get-Content $lock -Raw
        if ($text -like "$Owner |*") { Remove-Item $lock -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
    }
}
function Clean-Line($line) { ($line -replace '^\[[^\]]*\]\[[^\]]*\]', '').Trim() }

# Runs a game-mode process of the plugin (bake / compare / showcase) and reads its log.
function Invoke-PixelVfxRun([string]$modeArg, [int]$resX, [int]$resY) {
    $log = Join-Path $logDir "pixelvfx_$($Action.ToLower())_$stamp.log"
    $map = '/Engine/Maps/Entry?game=/Script/GBPixelVfxBaker.GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode'
    $argList = @("`"$Project`"", $map, '-game', "-abslog=`"$log`"", '-windowed', "-ResX=$resX", "-ResY=$resY",
        '-unattended', '-nosplash', '-nosound', '-nocrashreports', '-EnablePlugins=GBPixelVfx,NiagaraFluids', $modeArg)
    if (-not $Window) { $argList += '-RenderOffscreen' }
    # Bakes count simulation frames: the fixed step must be in place before the first frame (BeginPlay is too late for it).
    if ($Action -eq 'Bake') { $argList += @('-UseFixedTimeStep', '-FPS=60') }
    if ($HardwareRT) {
        # r.RayTracing is read once at startup: it has to come from the ini layer, not from a console command.
        $argList += @('-dx12', '-ini:Engine:[/Script/Engine.RendererSettings]:r.RayTracing=1,r.Lumen.HardwareRayTracing=1')
    }
    $sw = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
    $p = Start-Process -FilePath $editor -ArgumentList $argList -PassThru
    $timedOut = -not $p.WaitForExit($TimeoutSeconds * 1000)
    if ($timedOut) {
        # A game process started by this script, not the Editor: stopping it is safe.
        Write-Host "TIMEOUT after $TimeoutSeconds s: stopping the process."
        Stop-Process -Id $p.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Start-Sleep 2
    }
    $sw.Stop()
    if (-not (Test-Path $log)) { Write-Host "No log at $log (exit $($p.ExitCode))"; return 2 }
    $lines = Get-Content $log -Encoding UTF8
    $ours = @($lines | Where-Object { $_ -match 'PVFX' })
    $bad = @($lines | Where-Object { $_ -match 'LogGBPixelVfx: (Error|Warning)|Fatal error|Unhandled Exception|Assertion failed|Ensure condition failed|appError' })
    $ours | Select-Object -First 200 | ForEach-Object { Clean-Line $_ }
    Write-Host '---- problems (LogGBPixelVfx warnings/errors, asserts, crashes) ----'
    if ($bad.Count -eq 0) { Write-Host '(none)' } else { $bad | Select-Object -First 40 | ForEach-Object { Clean-Line $_ } }
    $done = @($ours | Where-Object { $_ -match 'PVFX DONE' }).Count -gt 0
    $crashed = @($bad | Where-Object { $_ -match 'Fatal error|Unhandled Exception|Assertion failed|appError' }).Count -gt 0
    $errorCount = @($bad | Where-Object { $_ -match 'LogGBPixelVfx: Error' }).Count
    Write-Host ("PixelVfx {0}: done={1} crashed={2} errors={3} timedOut={4} wall={5:N0} s  log: {6}" -f $Action, $done, $crashed, $errorCount, $timedOut, $sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, $log)
    if ($done -and -not $crashed -and $errorCount -eq 0 -and -not $timedOut) { return 0 } else { return 1 }
}

$code = 1
Wait-MachineLock
try {
    switch ($Action) {
        'Build' {
            $log = Join-Path $logDir 'build_pixelvfx.log'
            $bat = Join-Path $EngineDir 'Engine\Build\BatchFiles\Build.bat'
            $sw = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
            & cmd.exe /d /c "`"$bat`" GlyphboundEditor Win64 Development -project=`"$Project`" -waitmutex -EnablePlugin=GBPixelVfx > `"$log`" 2>&1"
            $code = $LASTEXITCODE
            $sw.Stop()
            $errors = Select-String -Path $log -Pattern '(: error |: fatal error |error LNK|Error: |ERROR: |UHT.*[Ee]rror)' | Select-Object -First 60
            if ($errors) { $errors | ForEach-Object { $_.Line.Trim() } }
            $result = Select-String -Path $log -Pattern '^Result: ' | Select-Object -Last 1
            if ($result) { Write-Host $result.Line } else { Write-Host "No 'Result:' line found (exit $code). See $log" }
            Write-Host ("Build time: {0:N0} s  (exit {1})  log: {2}" -f $sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, $code, $log)
        }
        'Test' {
            $runDir = Join-Path $projDir "Saved\TestRuns\pixelvfx_$stamp"
            [void][IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($runDir)
            $log = Join-Path $runDir 'editor.log'
            $argList = @("`"$Project`"", '-ExecCmds="Automation RunTests ^GBPixelVfx.; Quit"', '-TestExit="Automation Test Queue Empty"',
                "-ReportExportPath=`"$runDir`"", "-abslog=`"$log`"", '-EnablePlugins=GBPixelVfx',
                '-unattended', '-nopause', '-nosplash', '-NullRHI', '-nosound', '-nocrashreports')
            $sw = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
            $p = Start-Process -FilePath $editorCmd -ArgumentList $argList -PassThru -WindowStyle Hidden
            if (-not $p.WaitForExit($TimeoutSeconds * 1000)) {
                Write-Host "TIMEOUT after $TimeoutSeconds s: stopping the test process."
                Stop-Process -Id $p.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                $code = 3
            } else {
                $sw.Stop()
                $index = Join-Path $runDir 'index.json'
                if (-not (Test-Path $index)) {
                    Write-Host "FAIL: no report was written (exit $($p.ExitCode)). Did the module load? Log: $log"
                    Select-String -Path $log -Pattern 'Error:|error:|No automation tests matched' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 15 | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }
                    $code = 2
                } else {
                    $report = Get-Content $index -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
                    $tests = @($report.tests)
                    $failed = @($tests | Where-Object { $_.state -ne 'Success' })
                    foreach ($t in $failed) {
                        Write-Host "FAILED: $($t.fullTestPath) [$($t.state)]"
                        foreach ($e in @($t.entries)) { if ($e.event.type -eq 'Error') { Write-Host "   $($e.event.message)" } }
                    }
                    Write-Host ("Tests: {0} run, {1} failed, wall time {2:N0} s. Report: {3}" -f $tests.Count, $failed.Count, $sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, $runDir)
                    if ($tests.Count -lt 1) { Write-Host 'FAIL: no test ran.'; $code = 4 }
                    elseif ($failed.Count -gt 0) { $code = 1 } else { $code = 0 }
                }
            }
        }
        'Materials' {
            $script = Join-Path $pluginDir 'Tools\make_pixelvfx_material.py'
            $log = Join-Path $logDir "pixelvfx_materials_$stamp.log"
            # Compiling a material needs a renderer: commandlets only start one with -AllowCommandletRendering.
            $argList = @("`"$Project`"", '-run=pythonscript', "-script=`"$script`"", "-abslog=`"$log`"",
                '-AllowCommandletRendering', '-unattended', '-nopause', '-nosplash', '-nosound', '-nocrashreports')
            $p = Start-Process -FilePath $editorCmd -ArgumentList $argList -PassThru -WindowStyle Hidden
            if (-not $p.WaitForExit($TimeoutSeconds * 1000)) { Stop-Process -Id $p.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; Write-Host 'TIMEOUT'; $code = 3 }
            else {
                $lines = Get-Content $log -Encoding UTF8
                $ours = @($lines | Where-Object { $_ -match 'make_pixelvfx_material:' })
                $errors = @($lines | Where-Object { $_ -match 'LogPython: Error|make_pixelvfx_material: ERROR|Traceback|Fatal error|Assertion failed' })
                $ours | ForEach-Object { Clean-Line $_ }
                $errors | Select-Object -First 40 | ForEach-Object { Clean-Line $_ }
                $asset = Join-Path $projDir 'Content\Glyphbound\Materials\M_PixelVfxSprite.uasset'
                $ok = (@($ours | Where-Object { $_ -match 'make_pixelvfx_material: OK' }).Count -gt 0) -and (Test-Path $asset) -and ($errors.Count -eq 0)
                Write-Host ("Materials: ok={0} log: {1}" -f $ok, $log)
                $code = if ($ok) { 0 } else { 1 }
            }
        }
        'Bake' { $code = Invoke-PixelVfxRun "-PvfxBake=$Id" 640 360 }
        'Compare' { $code = Invoke-PixelVfxRun "-PvfxCompare=$Id" 1280 720 }
        'Showcase' { $code = Invoke-PixelVfxRun '-PvfxShowcase' 1280 720 }
    }
}
finally { Remove-MachineLock }
exit $code
# Jam dev log (goal: the Glass Box prize for the best dev log)

Everything any agent or person does on the project must be recorded here. If it is not in the dev log, it did not happen as far as the judges are concerned.

**Language:** the Official Rules (7(i)) require every part of the submission in English, and that includes the published dev log. Write `days/*.md`, `media/` captions and `PRE_JAM_DECLARATION.md` in English. Raw logs (`raw/`) and the team's prompts to agents can be in any language. When a day entry quotes a prompt written in Spanish, include the original and an English translation.

## Layout

```
Devlog/
  README.md                  this file (the rules)
  TEMPLATE_DAY.md            template for the daily entry
  PRE_JAM_DECLARATION.md     what was prepared BEFORE Nov 12 (the jam requires declaring it)
  PREJAM_PREP.md             full pre-jam log: timeline, tests, tools evaluated, rules read
  raw/<MACHINE>/             raw automatic logs (NEVER published as they are)
    <YYYY-MM-DD>/claude-<session>.jsonl      every Claude Code event (hook)
    <YYYY-MM-DD>/transcripts/<session>.jsonl full transcript of each session
    <YYYY-MM-DD>/agents.jsonl                every run of an external agent
    <YYYY-MM-DD>/agents/<run>/               prompt, full output and diffstat of that run
    unreal/<log>.jsonl                       agent actions inside the Editor (MCP, Assistant, Canvas)
  days/<YYYY-MM-DD>_DRAFT.md                 automatic draft (already redacted)
  days/<YYYY-MM-DD>.md                       final entry for the day, written by a person (in English)
  media/<YYYY-MM-DD>/                        screenshots, gifs and clips of the day
```

## Rules for everyone (people and agents)

1. **Claude Code** is logged automatically by the **user-level** hooks that `Tools/Devlog/Install-DevlogHooks.ps1` installs on each machine. They log every session opened in the jam root or any subfolder (the root is the folder that contains `Devlog/.jamroot`). Do not disable them and do not delete `.jamroot`.
2. **Any other agent** (Codex, cursor-agent, OpenCode, DeepSeek, Gemini, generation scripts) is ALWAYS launched with:
   `powershell -File Tools/Devlog/Invoke-LoggedAgent.ps1 -Agent <name> -Model <model> -PromptFile <prompt.md> -Purpose "<what for>" -Command '<command>'`
   - Use `-Shell cmd` if the command contains `< file`.
   - That is what records it. An agent launched by hand leaves no record.
   - **Cursor** (the art LAPTOP, which has no repo) is logged by user-level Cursor hooks instead: `Tools/Devlog/Install-CursorHooks.ps1` installs `cursor-devlog-hook.ps1`, which writes `C:\JamLog\raw\<MACHINE>\<date>\cursor-<conversation>.jsonl` (plus transcripts). `Register-LaptopDevlogTasks.ps1` copies that folder every 10 minutes to `Incoming\LAPTOP\_devlog_raw` on CENTRAL, and CENTRAL's `JamDevlogImport` task (`Import-RemoteDevlog.ps1`) moves it into `Devlog/raw/`, where `New-DevlogDay.ps1` reads it. The same kit runs on ASSETS (destination `Incoming\ASSETS\_devlog_raw`).
   - **Codex chats** (desktop app, IDE extension or interactive CLI, i.e. anything not launched through `Invoke-LoggedAgent.ps1`) on machines without the repo: before each sync, `Collect-CodexSessions.ps1` copies the session files Codex keeps in `%USERPROFILE%\.codex\sessions` (full prompts, replies and tool calls) into `C:\JamLog\raw\<MACHINE>\<date>\codex-*.jsonl`. `New-DevlogDay.ps1` reads those too.
   - **Cursor transcripts:** `Collect-CursorTranscripts.ps1` also copies the full agent transcripts Cursor keeps in `%USERPROFILE%.cursorprojects*gent-transcripts`, so chats from before the hook was installed are not lost. The draft takes Cursor prompts from the transcript whenever there is one (it is complete; the hook missed some prompts before the mutex fix).
   - **Claude Code and Gemini CLI** on machines without the repo: `Collect-OtherAgents.ps1` copies their session files (`%USERPROFILE%.claudeprojects`, `%USERPROFILE%.gemini	mp`). OpenCode keeps its sessions in a SQLite database, so it is always launched through `Invoke-LoggedAgent.ps1`; on ASSETS the sync also sends that repo's `Devlog
aw` (`-ExtraSource`).
   - **Size:** every collector strips base64 images (screenshots, renders) from the copies (`JamLogCopy.ps1`): one Codex session on ASSETS went from 110 MB to 4.2 MB with every message and tool call intact. `Import-RemoteDevlog.ps1` refuses files over 50 MB and logs them, because `Devlog/raw` goes to GitHub, which rejects files over 100 MB.
   - On CENTRAL, Codex and OpenCode always run through `Invoke-LoggedAgent.ps1`, so this collection is only needed on machines without the repo.
3. **Prompts as files:** external agents get their prompt in a `.md` under `Prompts/`, never typed loose in the terminal.
4. **Web or desktop tools** (Fab, cloud Canvas, audio or image generators, Blender by hand): in the day entry, write down the tool, the prompt, the result and the final asset, and save a screenshot in `media/`.
5. **Unreal Editor:** always close it with `Tools/Jam/Stop-EditorMcp.ps1`, which closes open asset editors first (an open animation editor crashes the Editor on exit) and collects the log. If it was closed some other way, or before deleting `Saved/`, run `powershell -File Tools/Devlog/Collect-UnrealAgentLog.ps1 -ProjectDir <folder of the .uproject>`.
6. **`JAM_MACHINE` variable:** each machine sets its own (`CENTRAL`, `ASSETS`â€¦) so we know where each thing happened:
   `setx JAM_MACHINE CENTRAL`
7. **Small, frequent commits** with clear messages: the daily draft lists them. Commit `Devlog/raw/` too (the repo is private). Epic may ask for the version control history.
8. **End of day:** run `powershell -File Tools/Devlog/New-DevlogDay.ps1 -Date <YYYY-MM-DD>`. Then a person writes `days/<date>.md` from `TEMPLATE_DAY.md`, starting from the draft.
9. **Publish:** only `days/*.md`, `media/`, `PRE_JAM_DECLARATION.md` and `PREJAM_PREP.md`. Raw logs may contain keys or emails: the draft is already redacted, but check it before publishing. The dev log link goes in the submission, **before the deadline**.
10. **Times in UTC.** The jam ends on Nov 15 at 20:59 EST, which is **01:59 UTC on Nov 16**.

namespace GBPixelVfxClipLocal
{
	EGBPixelVfxFacing FacingFromString(const FString& Text)
	{
		if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Billboard"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
		{
			return EGBPixelVfxFacing::Billboard;
		}
		if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Ground"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
		{
			return EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground;
		}
		if (Text.Equals(TEXT("Fixed"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
		{
			return EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed;
		}
		return EGBPixelVfxFacing::Upright;
	}

	FString FacingToString(EGBPixelVfxFacing Facing)
	{
		switch (Facing)
		{
		case EGBPixelVfxFacing::Billboard: return TEXT("Billboard");
		case EGBPixelVfxFacing::Ground: return TEXT("Ground");
		case EGBPixelVfxFacing::Fixed: return TEXT("Fixed");
		default: return TEXT("Upright");
		}
	}

	int32 HexDigit(TCHAR C)
	{
		if (C >= TEXT('0') && C <= TEXT('9')) return C - TEXT('0');
		if (C >= TEXT('a') && C <= TEXT('f')) return 10 + C - TEXT('a');
		if (C >= TEXT('A') && C <= TEXT('F')) return 10 + C - TEXT('A');
		return -1;
	}

	// "#RRGGBB" (the '#' is optional) -> linear color. Returns white when the text is broken.
	FLinearColor ColorFromHex(const FString& Hex)
	{
		FString Text = Hex;
		Text.TrimStartAndEndInline();
		if (Text.StartsWith(TEXT("#")))
		{
			Text.RightChopInline(1);
		}
		if (Text.Len() != 6)
		{
			return FLinearColor::White;
		}
		uint8 Bytes[3] = { 255, 255, 255 };
		for (int32 i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
		{
			const int32 Hi = HexDigit(Text[i * 2]);
			const int32 Lo = HexDigit(Text[i * 2 + 1]);
			if (Hi < 0 || Lo < 0)
			{
				return FLinearColor::White;
			}
			Bytes[i] = static_cast<uint8>(Hi * 16 + Lo);
		}
		return FLinearColor(FColor(Bytes[0], Bytes[1], Bytes[2]));
	}

	FString ColorToHex(const FLinearColor& Color)
	{
		const FColor Srgb = Color.ToFColor(true);
		return FString::Printf(TEXT("#%02X%02X%02X"), Srgb.R, Srgb.G, Srgb.B);
	}
}

FVector4f FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameRect(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, int32 FrameIndex)
{
	if (Clip.Columns <= 0 || Clip.FrameWidth <= 0 || Clip.FrameHeight <= 0 || Clip.Rows() <= 0)
	{
		return FVector4f(0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f);
	}
	const int32 Clamped = FMath::Clamp(FrameIndex, 0, Clip.FrameCount - 1);
	const float AtlasW = static_cast<float>(Clip.Columns * Clip.FrameWidth);
	const float AtlasH = static_cast<float>(Clip.Rows() * Clip.FrameHeight);
	const int32 Col = Clamped % Clip.Columns;
	const int32 Row = Clamped / Clip.Columns;
	return FVector4f(
		Col * Clip.FrameWidth / AtlasW,
		Row * Clip.FrameHeight / AtlasH,
		Clip.FrameWidth / AtlasW,
		Clip.FrameHeight / AtlasH);
}

int32 FGBPixelVfxClip::FrameAtTime(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, float Seconds, bool& bOutFinished)
{
	bOutFinished = false;
	if (Clip.FrameCount <= 0 || Clip.Fps <= 0)
	{
		bOutFinished = true;
		return 0;
	}
	const int32 Stepped = FMath::FloorToInt(Seconds * static_cast<float>(Clip.Fps));
	if (Clip.bLoop)
	{
		const int32 Wrapped = Stepped % Clip.FrameCount;
		return Wrapped >= 0 ? Wrapped : Wrapped + Clip.FrameCount;
	}
	if (Stepped >= Clip.FrameCount)
	{
		bOutFinished = true;
		return Clip.FrameCount - 1;
	}
	return FMath::Max(0, Stepped);
}

void GetShowOnlyAndHiddenComponents(USceneCaptureComponent* SceneCaptureComponent, TSet<FPrimitiveComponentId>& HiddenPrimitives, TOptional<TSet<FPrimitiveComponentId>>& ShowOnlyPrimitives)
{
	check(SceneCaptureComponent);
	for (auto It = SceneCaptureComponent->HiddenComponents.CreateConstIterator(); It; ++It)
	{
		// If the primitive component was destroyed, the weak pointer will return NULL.
		UPrimitiveComponent* PrimitiveComponent = It->Get();
		if (PrimitiveComponent)
		{
			HiddenPrimitives.Add(PrimitiveComponent->GetPrimitiveSceneId());
		}
	}

	for (auto It = SceneCaptureComponent->HiddenActors.CreateConstIterator(); It; ++It)
	{
		AActor* Actor = *It;

		if (Actor)
		{
			for (UActorComponent* Component : Actor->GetComponents())
			{
				if (UPrimitiveComponent* PrimComp = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(Component))
				{
					HiddenPrimitives.Add(PrimComp->GetPrimitiveSceneId());
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if (SceneCaptureComponent->PrimitiveRenderMode == ESceneCapturePrimitiveRenderMode::PRM_UseShowOnlyList)
	{
		ShowOnlyPrimitives.Emplace();

		for (auto It = SceneCaptureComponent->ShowOnlyComponents.CreateConstIterator(); It; ++It)
		{
			// If the primitive component was destroyed, the weak pointer will return NULL.
			UPrimitiveComponent* PrimitiveComponent = It->Get();
			if (PrimitiveComponent)
			{
				ShowOnlyPrimitives->Add(PrimitiveComponent->GetPrimitiveSceneId());
			}
		}

			{

					}
				}
			}
		}

void USceneComponent::DestroyComponent(bool bPromoteChildren/*= false*/)
{
	if (bPromoteChildren)
	{
		AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
		if (Owner != nullptr)
		{
			Owner->Modify();
			USceneComponent* ChildToPromote = nullptr;

			const TArray<USceneComponent*>& AttachedChildren = GetAttachChildren();
			// Handle removal of the root node
			if (this == Owner->GetRootComponent())
			{
				// Always choose non editor-only child nodes over editor-only child nodes (since we don't want editor-only nodes to end up with non editor-only child nodes)
				// Exclude scene components owned by attached child actors
				USceneComponent* const * FindResult =
					AttachedChildren.FindByPredicate([Owner](USceneComponent* Child){ return Child != nullptr && !Child->IsEditorOnly() && Child->GetOwner() == Owner; });

				const bool bIsNativeOwnerClass = Owner->GetClass()->IsNative();

				if (FindResult != nullptr)
				{
					ChildToPromote = *FindResult;
				}
				// Native C++ classes do not need to always have a DefaultSceneRoot, it can be empty on
				// instances placed in the level directly from the C++ class
				else if (!bIsNativeOwnerClass)
				{
					// Didn't find a suitable component to promote so create a new default component

					Rename(nullptr, GetOuter(), REN_DoNotDirty | REN_DontCreateRedirectors);

					// Construct a new default root component
					USceneComponent* NewRootComponent = NewObject<USceneComponent>(Owner, USceneComponent::GetDefaultSceneRootVariableName(), RF_Transactional);
					NewRootComponent->Mobility = Mobility;
					NewRootComponent->SetWorldLocationAndRotation(GetComponentLocation(), GetComponentRotation());
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
					NewRootComponent->bVisualizeComponent = true;
#endif
					Owner->AddInstanceComponent(NewRootComponent);
					NewRootComponent->RegisterComponent();

					// Designate the new default root as the child we're promoting
					ChildToPromote = NewRootComponent;
				}
APlayerController* AGBPixelVfxBakeGameMode::EnsurePlayerController()
{
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		if (APlayerController* PC = World->GetFirstPlayerController())
		{
			return PC;
		}
		return UGameplayStatics::CreatePlayer(World, 0);
	}
	return nullptr;
}

FLogCategoryBase::FLogCategoryBase(const FLogCategoryName& InCategoryName, ELogVerbosity::Type InDefaultVerbosity, ELogVerbosity::Type InCompileTimeVerbosity)
	: DefaultVerbosity(InDefaultVerbosity)
	, CompileTimeVerbosity(InCompileTimeVerbosity)
	, CategoryName(InCategoryName)
{
	TRACE_LOG_CATEGORY(this, *FName(CategoryName).ToString(), InDefaultVerbosity);
	ResetFromDefault();
	if (CompileTimeVerbosity > ELogVerbosity::NoLogging)
	{
		FLogSuppressionInterface::Get().AssociateSuppress(this);
	}
	checkSlow(!(Verbosity & ELogVerbosity::BreakOnLog)); // this bit is factored out of this variable, always
}

FLogCategoryBase::FLogCategoryBase(const TCHAR* InCategoryName, ELogVerbosity::Type InDefaultVerbosity, ELogVerbosity::Type InCompileTimeVerbosity)
	: FLogCategoryBase(FLogCategoryName{InCategoryName}, InDefaultVerbosity, InCompileTimeVerbosity)
{
}

FLogCategoryBase::~FLogCategoryBase()
{
	checkSlow(!(Verbosity & ELogVerbosity::BreakOnLog)); // this bit is factored out of this variable, always
	if (CompileTimeVerbosity > ELogVerbosity::NoLogging)
	{
		if (FLogSuppressionInterface* Singleton = FLogSuppressionInterface::TryGet())
		{
			Singleton->DisassociateSuppress(this);
		}
	}
}

bool FJsonObject::GetBoolField(FStringView FieldName) const
{
	return GetField<EJson::None>(FieldName)->AsBool();
}

bool FJsonObject::TryGetBoolField(FStringView FieldName, bool& OutBool) const
{
	TSharedPtr<FJsonValue> Field = TryGetField(FieldName);
	return Field.IsValid() && Field->TryGetBool(OutBool);
}

const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>& FJsonObject::GetArrayField(FStringView FieldName) const
{
	return GetField<EJson::Array>(FieldName)->AsArray();
}

bool FJsonObject::TryGetArrayField(FStringView FieldName, const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>*& OutArray) const
{
	TSharedPtr<FJsonValue> Field = TryGetField(FieldName);
	return Field.IsValid() && Field->TryGetArray(OutArray);
}

const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& FJsonObject::GetObjectField(FStringView FieldName) const
{
	return GetField<EJson::Object>(FieldName)->AsObject();
}

bool FJsonObject::TryGetObjectField(FStringView FieldName, const TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>*& OutObject) const
{
	TSharedPtr<FJsonValue> Field = TryGetField(FieldName);
	return Field.IsValid() && Field->TryGetObject(OutObject);

void USceneComponent::OnComponentDestroyed(bool bDestroyingHierarchy)
{
	Super::OnComponentDestroyed(bDestroyingHierarchy);

#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
	if (SpriteComponent)
	{
		SpriteComponent->DestroyComponent();
	}
#endif

	ScopedMovementStack.Reset();

	// If we're just destroying for the exit purge don't bother with any of this
	if (!GExitPurge && !bComputeBoundsOnceForGame)
	{
		// If we're destroying the hierarchy we only have to make sure that we detach children from other Actors
		AActor* MyOwner = GetOwner();

		// Do not involve objects which will be destroyed in hierarchy fixups
		const EInternalObjectFlags SkipFlags = EInternalObjectFlags::Garbage | EInternalObjectFlags::Unreachable;

		if (bDestroyingHierarchy)
		{
			// We'll lazily determine if we were attached in any way to a component from another Actor so that any of our children
			// can be attached to this anyways as that would have been what ultimately occurred after the entire hierarchy was torn down
			bool bExternalAttachParentDetermined = false;
			USceneComponent* ExternalAttachParent = nullptr;

			int32 ChildCount = AttachChildren.Num();

			// We cache the actual children to put back after the detach process
			TArray<TObjectPtr<USceneComponent>> CachedChildren;
			CachedChildren.Reserve(ChildCount);

			while (ChildCount > 0)
			{
				USceneComponent* Child = AttachChildren.Last();
				if (Child && Child->GetOwner() != MyOwner)
				{
					if (Child->GetAttachParent())
					{
						if (Child->GetAttachParent() == this)
						{
							bool bNeedsDetach = true;
							// If this child is going to be destroyed just detach it and don't find a new parent
							if (!Child->HasAnyInternalFlags(SkipFlags))
							{
								if (!bExternalAttachParentDetermined)
								{
									ExternalAttachParent = GetAttachParent();
									while (ExternalAttachParent)
									{
										// Only attach to a parent which will not soon be destroyed
										if (!ExternalAttachParent->HasAnyInternalFlags(SkipFlags) && ExternalAttachParent->GetOwner() != MyOwner)
										{
											break;
										}
										ExternalAttachParent = ExternalAttachParent->GetAttachParent();
									}
									bExternalAttachParentDetermined = true;
								}

								if (ExternalAttachParent)
								{
									bNeedsDetach = (Child->AttachToComponent(ExternalAttachParent, FAttachmentTransformRules::KeepWorldTransform) == false);
								}
							}
							if (bNeedsDetach)
							{
								Child->DetachFromComponent(FDetachmentTransformRules::KeepWorldTransform);
							}
						}
						else
						{
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
							// If we are in the middle of a transaction it isn't entirely unexpected that an AttachParent/AttachChildren pairing is wrong
							if (!ensureAlwaysMsgf(GIsTransacting, TEXT("Component '%s' has '%s' in its AttachChildren array, however, '%s' believes it is attached to '%s'"), *GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetAttachParent()->GetFullName()))
#endif
							{
								// We've gotten in to a bad state where the Child's AttachParent doesn't jive with the AttachChildren array
								// so instead of crashing, output an error and gracefully handle
								UE_LOGF(LogSceneComponent, Error, "Component '%ls' has '%ls' in its AttachChildren array, however, '%ls' believes it is attached to '%ls'", *GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetAttachParent()->GetFullName());
							}
							AttachChildren.Pop(EAllowShrinking::No);
						}
					}
					else 
					{
						// We've gotten in to a bad state where the Child's AttachParent doesn't jive with the AttachChildren array
						// so instead of crashing, gracefully handle and output an error. 
	return MID;
}

	void AssociateSuppressImpl(FLogCategoryBase* Destination)
	{
		FName NameFName(Destination->CategoryName);
		bool bFoundExisting = false;
		for (TMultiMap<FName, FLogCategoryBase*>::TKeyIterator It(ReverseAssociations, NameFName); It; ++It)
		{
			if (It.Value() == Destination)
			{
				if (!IsReloadActive())
				{
					UE_LOGF(LogHAL, Fatal,"Log suppression category %ls was somehow declared twice with the same data.", *NameFName.ToString());
				}
			}
			// if it is registered, it better be the same
			if (It.Value()->CompileTimeVerbosity != Destination->CompileTimeVerbosity)
			{
				UE_LOGF(LogHAL, Fatal,"Log suppression category %ls is defined multiple times with different compile time verbosity.", *NameFName.ToString());
			}
			// we take whatever the existing one has to keep them in sync always
			checkSlow(!(It.Value()->Verbosity & ELogVerbosity::BreakOnLog)); // this bit is factored out of this variable, always
			Destination->Verbosity = It.Value()->Verbosity;
			Destination->DebugBreakOnLog = It.Value()->DebugBreakOnLog;
			Destination->DefaultVerbosity = It.Value()->DefaultVerbosity;
			bFoundExisting = true;
		}
		ReverseAssociations.Add(NameFName, Destination);
		if (bFoundExisting)
		{
			return; // in no case is there anything more to do...we want to match the other ones
		}
		SetupSuppress(Destination, NameFName); // this might be done again later if this is being set up before appInit is called
	}

	virtual void DisassociateSuppress(FLogCategoryBase* Destination)
	{
		int32 PendingIndex = PendingAssociations.FindLast(Destination);

								while (NewParent && NewParent->HasAnyInternalFlags(SkipFlags))
								{
									NewParent = NewParent->GetAttachParent();
								}
								if(NewParent)
								{
									// if we reattach to a new parent, we won't need to detach, in that case AttachChildren should decrement
									// from the AttachToComponent call
									const bool bAttachedToNewParent = Child->AttachToComponent(NewParent, FAttachmentTransformRules::KeepWorldTransform);

									}
									bNeedsDetach = !bAttachedToNewParent;
								}
							}

							if (bNeedsDetach)
							{
								checkf(ChildAttachParentWas->GetAttachChildren().Contains(Child), TEXT("Did not find '%s', in Child->AttachParent->AttachChildren. Child: '%s', Child->AttachParent: '%s'"), *GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetAttachParent()->GetFullName());
								Child->DetachFromComponent(FDetachmentTransformRules::KeepWorldTransform);
								checkf(ChildCount > AttachChildren.Num(), TEXT("AttachChildren count increased while detaching '%s', likely caused by OnAttachmentChanged introducing new children, which could lead to an infinite loop."), *Child->GetName());
							}
						}
						else
						{
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
							// If we are in the middle of a transaction it isn't entirely unexpected that an AttachParent/AttachChildren pairing is wrong
							if (!ensureAlwaysMsgf(GIsTransacting || !bRegistered, TEXT("Component '%s' has '%s' in its AttachChildren array, however, '%s' believes it is attached to '%s'"), *GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetAttachParent()->GetFullName()))
#endif
							{
								// We've gotten in to a bad state where the Child's AttachParent doesn't jive with the AttachChildren array
								// so instead of crashing, output an error and gracefully handle
								UE_LOGF(LogSceneComponent, Error, "Component '%ls' has '%ls' in its AttachChildren array, however, '%ls' believes it is attached to '%ls'", *GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetAttachParent()->GetFullName());
							}
							AttachChildren.Pop(EAllowShrinking::No);
						}
					}
					else 
					{
						// We've gotten in to a bad state where the Child's AttachParent doesn't jive with the AttachChildren array
						// so instead of crashing, gracefully handle and output an error. 
						// We skip outputting the error if something is pending kill because this is likely a undo/redo situation that is not concerning.
						if (IsValidChecked(this) && IsValid(Child))
						{
							UE_LOGF(LogSceneComponent, Error, "Component '%ls' has '%ls' in its AttachChildren array, however, '%ls' believes it is not attached to anything", *GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName(), *Child->GetFullName());
						}
						AttachChildren.Pop(EAllowShrinking::No);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					AttachChildren.Pop(EAllowShrinking::No);
				}
				ChildCount = AttachChildren.Num();
			}
		}

		// Don't bother detaching from our parent if we're destroying the hierarchy, unless we're attached to
		// another Actor's component
		if (GetAttachParent() && (!bDestroyingHierarchy || GetAttachParent()->GetOwner() != MyOwner))
		{
			// Ensure we are detached before destroying
			DetachFromComponent(FDetachmentTransformRules::KeepWorldTransform);
		}

#if WITH_EDITOR
	// In case we're not yet ready to run due to compilation requests, go ahead and keep polling there..
	if (Asset->HasOutstandingCompilationRequests(true))
	{
		if (bWaitForCompilationOnActivate || NiagaraComponentPrivate::GForceWaitForCompilationOnActivate || GIsAutomationTesting)
		{
			Asset->WaitForCompilationComplete(true);
		}
		Asset->PollForCompilationComplete();
	}
#endif
	
	bool bIsReady = Asset->IsReadyToRun() && IsWorldReadyToRun();
	bIsReady &= CheckPSOCacheIsReadyToRun();

	if (!bIsReady)
	{
		bAwaitingActivationDueToNotReady = true;
		bActivateShouldResetWhenReady = bReset;
		ScopedComponentTickEnabled.bTickEnabled = true;
		UpdateInstanceSoloMode();

	if (bReset)

	}

	if (!bIsScalabilityCull && bIsCulledByScalability && ScalabilityManagerHandle != INDEX_NONE)
	{
		//If this is a non scalability activate call and we're still registered with the manager.
		//If we reach this point then we must have been previously culled by scalability so bail here.
		return;
	}

	if (!Asset->IsAllowedByScalability())
	{
		OnSystemComplete(true);
		return;

		bRenderCustomDepth = Asset->bOverrideRenderCustomDepth ? Asset->bRenderCustomDepth : bRenderCustomDepth;
		CustomDepthStencilWriteMask = Asset->bOverrideCustomDepthStencilValue ? Asset->CustomDepthStencilWriteMask : CustomDepthStencilWriteMask;
		CustomDepthStencilValue = Asset->bOverrideCustomDepthStencilWriteMask ? Asset->CustomDepthStencilValue : CustomDepthStencilValue;

		TranslucencySortPriority = Asset->bOverrideTranslucencySortPriority ? Asset->TranslucencySortPriority : TranslucencySortPriority;
		TranslucencySortDistanceOffset = Asset->bOverrideTranslucencySortDistanceOffset ? Asset->TranslucencySortDistanceOffset : TranslucencySortDistanceOffset;

		// Activate
		if ( IsRegistered() )
		{
			if (bAutoActivate || bWasActive)
			{
				Activate();
			}
		}
	}

protected:

	/** Calculate the new ComponentToWorld transform for this component.
		Parent is optional and can be used for computing ComponentToWorld based on arbitrary USceneComponent.
		If Parent is not passed in we use the component's AttachParent*/
	inline FTransform CalcNewComponentToWorld(const FTransform& NewRelativeTransform, const USceneComponent* Parent = nullptr, FName SocketName = NAME_None) const
	{
		SocketName = Parent ? SocketName : GetAttachSocketName();
		Parent = Parent ? Parent : GetAttachParent();
		if (Parent)
		{
			const bool bGeneral = IsUsingAbsoluteLocation() || IsUsingAbsoluteRotation() || IsUsingAbsoluteScale();
			if (!bGeneral)
			{
				return NewRelativeTransform * Parent->GetSocketTransform(SocketName);
			}
			
			return CalcNewComponentToWorld_GeneralCase(NewRelativeTransform, Parent, SocketName);
		}
		else
		{
			return NewRelativeTransform;
		}
	}

	/** Utility function to handle calculating transform with a parent */
	ENGINE_API FTransform CalcNewComponentToWorld_GeneralCase(const FTransform& NewRelativeTransform, const USceneComponent* Parent, FName SocketName) const;

bool FRenderTarget::ReadLinearColorPixels(TArray<FLinearColor> &OutImageData, FReadSurfaceDataFlags InFlags, FIntRect InSrcRect)
{
	if (InSrcRect == FIntRect(0, 0, 0, 0))
	{
		InSrcRect = FIntRect(0, 0, GetSizeXY().X, GetSizeXY().Y);
	}

	OutImageData.Reset();

	// Read the render target surface data back.	
	ENQUEUE_RENDER_COMMAND(ReadSurfaceCommand)(
		[RenderTarget_RT = this, SrcRect_RT = InSrcRect, OutData_RT = &OutImageData, Flags_RT = InFlags](FRHICommandListImmediate& RHICmdList)
	{
		FRHITexture* Texture = RenderTarget_RT->GetShaderResourceTexture();
		RHICmdList.Transition(FRHITransitionInfo(Texture, ERHIAccess::SRVMask, ERHIAccess::CopySrc));
		RHICmdList.ReadSurfaceData(Texture, SrcRect_RT, *OutData_RT, Flags_RT);
		RHICmdList.Transition(FRHITransitionInfo(Texture, ERHIAccess::CopySrc, ERHIAccess::SRVMask));
	});
	FlushRenderingCommands();

	return OutImageData.Num() > 0;
}

codex
1. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:252,317:** Confirmed: pivot and light offsets are expressed in quad XY but applied in the parent’s space. An Upright fire with PivotV=0.94 shifts sideways and puts its light beside the flame. Choose rotation first; with the quad’s final world linear transform `L` and mesh-local pivot/anchor points `p/a`, use `QuadWorldLocation = ComponentWorldLocation - L*p` and `LightWorldLocation = QuadWorldLocation + L*a`.

2. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:95:** Atlas, material variant, and Glow are assigned only when creating the quad. Calling `Play(FluidArcaneFire)` after `Play(FluidFire)` without `Stop()` keeps the fire atlas, while switching to a lit clip keeps the unlit material. Refresh clip-dependent material state on every successful Play.

3. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:105; GBPixelVfxComponent.h:64:** The spawned mesh/light belong to the owner actor, but destroying just the VFX component never calls Stop. Removing a looping VFX component from a surviving actor leaves its detached sprite and light registered; UE’s scene-component destruction reparents/detaches children rather than destroying them. Override destruction to explicitly destroy both spawned components.

4. **MAJOR — Tools/make_pixelvfx_material.py:193,201:** Both material variants use emissive `color*(1+Glow)`. A lit smoke clip with Glow=0 therefore still emits its full color in complete darkness. Connect `color*Glow` to emissive for the lit variant.

5. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxConvert.cpp:64:** Nearest matching tone-maps palette candidates as well as the captured color, changing the target palette’s positions in OKLab. With Peak=1, white input, and palette black/#BCBCBC/white, this selects white instead of the grey nearest the tone-mapped input. Convert palette colors directly from sRGB to linear and then OKLab; tone-map only the input.

6. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:273,467:** The “full scene” fallback retains `PRM_UseShowOnlyList` while omitting its sole component. UE’s `GetShowOnlyAndHiddenComponents` consequently creates an empty visibility set, so an empty first capture guarantees another empty capture. Set `PRM_LegacySceneCapture` when `bLegacyCapture` is true.

7. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:853,887,901:** These failure branches increment BakeJobIndex after BakeLogAndCleanup already increments it. During an All bake, a system-load, readiness, or readback failure silently skips the following job. Advance the index in exactly one place.

8. **MAJOR — GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:649:** Clamping the projected pivot silently breaks alignment when auto-framing excludes the source origin. If the visible plume starts 100 cm above its emitter, clamping its below-frame pivot makes runtime playback start at the emitter instead. Include the origin in the framing bounds before fitting the aspect ratio, so the stored pivot remains valid without clamping.

9. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxComponent.cpp:270:** Ground uses zero relative rotation, so it inherits the component’s rotation. A Ground clip spawned on an actor pitched 90 degrees becomes vertical. Set its world rotation so its normal remains world +Z.

10. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:439:** Auto-framing evaluates luminance before applying Capture.Exposure, whereas conversion evaluates it afterward. Raising exposure can make outer pixels visible in the final pass after the prepass has cropped them away. Apply the same exposure when calculating the prepass alpha rule.

11. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxBakeGameMode_Bake.cpp:374:** Rounding one capture stride changes animation speed for non-divisible rates. SimFps=60/Fps=24 captures every three simulation frames, recording 20 fps while declaring 24 fps. Schedule each sample independently with `WarmupFrames + Round(i*SimFps/Fps)`.

12. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxBakeJob.cpp:229; GBPixelVfxBakeJob.h:38,43,46:** Optional defaults diverge from the job contract: omitted Ramps produces a failed bake, omitted levels/alpha values differ from FluidFire, and MinIslandPixels defaults to 2 despite the notes requiring cleanup off. Initialize the required defaults and preserve default ramps when the array is absent.

13. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:121:** RampGradient passes `floor(v*(N-1))`, despite that implementation placing band boundaries incorrectly. Assert exact indices immediately below, at, and above several `k/N` boundaries.

14. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:159:** SplitRouting’s hot pixel exceeds both hot thresholds, so replacing the required OR with AND still passes. Add separate luminance-only and saturation-only hot pixels, and assert independently calculated hot/cold levels.

15. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:369:** LoopBlend passes an implementation that blends only frame 0 or leaves opacity untouched: frame 1 is unexamined and every opacity equals 1. Use distinct extra frames and opacities, then assert both blended frames and the non-loop case.

16. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:259:** IslandsAndHoles passes a hole filler that fills every transparent pixel and always copies its first neighbor. The fixture has just one transparent pixel and identical neighbor colors. Add an unfillable gap and a hole whose majority color differs from its first neighbor.

17. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:398,417:** PlaybackHelpers passes a one-shot implementation that finishes when the last frame begins, and serialization that replaces every track value with 1. Assert the last frame remains unfinished until `FrameCount/Fps`, and compare every round-tripped track entry.

18. **MINOR — GBPixelVfxConvertTests.cpp:61:** No tests exercise percentile selection, fractional-opacity un-premultiplication, light-track energy/centroids, or hysteresis across disappearance and rapid coverage changes. Implementations using maximum brightness, multiplying RGB by alpha again, returning constant lighting, or retaining disappeared pixels can escape the suite. Add small fixtures with hand-calculated peaks, colors, normalized energies, anchors, and solid masks.

Fix first tonight: **1 (pivot/light transforms), 2 (clip switching), 3 (component destruction cleanup).**
